01通关重点

TU6 — Pharmaceutical Engineering (Solid & Liquid Forms)

Closed-book Exam Memorization Pack (Bilingual CN/EN)

Goal
memorize this pack → answer short questions + solve 1 problem per part.

Version v4 (expanded, bilingual, exam-oriented)

Exam format and time plan (confirmed by the professor)

中文
2小时；两部分：固体剂型 + 液体剂型；每部分：若干短题 + 1道综合题；闭卷。EN: 2 hours; two parts: solid forms + liquid forms; each part: short questions + one problem; closed-book.
中文
推荐分配：0–30 min 做固体短题；30–60 min 做液体短题；60–105 min 固体大题；105–150 min 液体大题；最后10 min 全卷检查。EN: Suggested time: 0–30 min solid short Qs; 30–60 min liquid short Qs; 60–105 min solid problem; 105–150 min liquid problem; last 10 min check.
中文
闭卷写法：所有计算题必须写单位；所有机理题必须写“因果链（原因→影响→对策/结论）”。EN: Closed-book writing: always show units in calculations; for mechanism questions write a causal chain (cause → effect → fix/conclusion).

Lectures 1–3 essentials (必须背：Drug cycle + Biopharmacy + strategy)

中文
药物生命周期（Drug cycle）= 从想法/发现 → 研发与临床 → 上市 → 市场与药物警戒（pharmacovigilance）→ 专利到期/仿制药竞争。EN: Drug cycle = from discovery → development/clinical → launch → marketing & pharmacovigilance → patent expiry/generics.
中文
关键阶段（按答题顺序写）：Discovery（筛选/优化候选）→ Pre-clinical（体外/动物：有效性+ADME+毒理）→ Clinical phases I–III（人体：安全性→剂量→有效性）→ Registration/Authorization → Launch。EN: Stages to cite: Discovery → Preclinical (in vitro/in vivo; ADME & tox) → Clinical I–III → Authorization → Launch.
中文
专利一句话：专利通常在研发早期申请；专利期内才能回本；到期后仿制药进入，价格下降。EN: Patent: filed early; exclusivity enables profitability; after expiry generics enter and price drops.
中文
药物警戒一句话：上市后持续收集/评估不良反应，更新风险控制（Phase IV）。EN: Pharmacovigilance = post-marketing safety monitoring and risk management (Phase IV).
中文
制剂设计的总目标：把药以可接受的方式送到作用部位，疗效最大、副作用最小。EN: Formulation aim: deliver drug to site of action with maximal efficacy and minimal side effects.
中文
口服特点（常考短答）：优点—方便、可固体/液体、大剂量；缺点—pH/酶降解、吸收部位差异、首过效应。EN: Oral route: convenient (solid/liquid, large doses) but subject to pH/enzymes, variable absorption site, and first-pass metabolism.
中文
生物利用度（bioavailability）答题模板：F 受“释放/溶出（in solution）+ 渗透过膜 + 代谢/外排”共同限制。EN: Bioavailability is limited by dissolution/release, permeability, and metabolism/efflux.
中文
BCS 四类（必须能写全）：I 高溶解/高渗透；II 低溶解/高渗透；III 高溶解/低渗透；IV 低/低。EN: BCS: I (high S/high P), II (low S/high P), III (high S/low P), IV (low S/low P).
中文
BCS→策略（最赚分）：II 类优先‘提溶出/提溶解度’（减粒径、盐型、无定形、表活/固分散）；III 类优先‘提渗透/减少外排’（促渗、抑制外排、前药/改途径）。EN: Strategy: BCS II → boost solubility/dissolution (size reduction, salts, amorphous, surfactant/solid dispersion); BCS III → boost permeability/limit efflux (enhancers, efflux inhibition, prodrug/route change).

Part A — Solid forms

A-Prep. Preformulation of solid forms (固体前处方：粉体/晶型/稳定性) — short questions

中文
前处方的目的：在设计制剂与工艺前，先搞清 API 的关键固体性质（溶出、流动、压片性、稳定性）→ 决定配方与工艺。EN: Preformulation clarifies solid API properties (dissolution, flow, compactability, stability) to choose formulation + process.
中文
粒径分布（PSD）为什么重要：粒径↓ → 溶出↑；但太细 → 静电/团聚、流动差、易分层。EN: PSD matters: smaller particles ↑ dissolution; too fine may worsen flow and increase agglomeration/segregation.
中文
粒径测定（会选/会写）：Sieving（>40 μm，需样品量大）；Laser diffraction（0.1–2000 μm，快、重复性好）；Microscopy/SEM（看形态）。EN: Particle size: sieving (>40 μm), laser diffraction (0.1–2000 μm), microscopy/SEM (shape).
中文
晶型/固态性质：晶体结构决定熔点、溶解度、溶出速率、机械性质；多晶型/水合物/无定形会显著改变这些参数。EN: Solid state: crystal form controls melting point, solubility, dissolution rate, and mechanical properties; polymorphs/hydrates/amorphous forms can change them markedly.
中文
粉体密度与流动（必须会算/会判断）：bulk density ρb、tapped density ρt；Carr index = (ρt−ρb)/ρt×100%；Hausner ratio = ρt/ρb。Carr 小、Hausner 接近 1 → 流动更好。EN: Flow indices: ρb, ρt; Carr = (ρt−ρb)/ρt×100%; Hausner = ρt/ρb. Lower Carr and Hausner closer to 1 indicate better flow.
中文
含水与吸湿性：水分会影响流动、压片、降解（如水解）；需要控制干燥/包装与储存条件。EN: Moisture/hygroscopicity affects flow, compaction, and degradation (e.g., hydrolysis); control drying, packaging, storage.
中文
相容性/稳定性筛查：API+辅料混合后做加速应力（温度/湿度/光）；常用 DSC/XRPD/FTIR 看晶型变化与相互作用。EN: Compatibility/stability: stress API–excipient blends (T/RH/light); use DSC/XRPD/FTIR to detect solid-state changes/interactions.

A0. One master chain (use this to explain any oral solid issue)

中文
口服固体制剂表现 = 崩解/释放 → 溶出（进入溶液态）→ 吸收（过膜）→ 暴露/疗效。任何“BA低/起效慢/疗效差”先从这条链拆原因。
EN
Oral solid performance = disintegration/release → dissolution (must be in solution) → absorption (membrane crossing) → exposure/efficacy. Use this chain to diagnose any issue.

A1. Dissolution (溶出) — equation, sink, and method

中文
核心方程（背！）：dC/dt = (D·A/h)·(Cs − Ct)。D扩散系数；A表面积；h扩散层厚度；(Cs−Ct)为驱动力。EN: Core equation: dC/dt = (D·A/h)·(Cs − Ct). D diffusion; A surface area; h boundary layer; (Cs−Ct) driving force.
中文
提速三招（背！）：A↑（粒径↓/比表面积↑）；Cs↑（调pH/盐型/表活/无定形/固分）；h↓（搅拌↑/流体动力学更强）。EN: Speed-up levers: increase A (smaller particles); increase Cs (pH/salt/surfactant/amorphous/solid dispersion); decrease h (stronger agitation/hydrodynamics).
中文
Sink condition：实验介质需保证最终浓度远低于 Cs（常用 Cs/3，或更严格 Cs/10）。否则溶出变慢，曲线难区分。EN: Sink condition: keep final concentration far below Cs (often Cs/3 or stricter Cs/10). Otherwise dissolution slows and profiles lose discrimination.
中文
溶出装置（认识即可）：basket、paddle、flow-through cell；条件常见：37°C；转速 50–100 rpm更有区分度；必要时脱气。EN: Dissolution apparatus: basket, paddle, flow-through cell; common conditions: 37°C; 50–100 rpm is more discriminating; degas if needed.
中文
介质与释放类型：IR常用单一pH；肠溶/延迟释放常用“两步法”（酸段2h→pH 6.8段）；缓释需多个时间点描述释放窗口。EN: Medium & release: IR uses single pH; enteric/delayed often uses 2-step (acid 2h → pH 6.8); ER requires multiple time points defining release window.
中文
表面活性剂：可用于润湿，但方法学上需避免“增溶掩盖问题”；常写成“use surfactant below CMC for wetting”。EN: Surfactants: can improve wetting; avoid masking by excessive solubilization; write 'use surfactant below CMC for wetting'.

A2. Disintegration vs dissolution vs absorption (short-question answers)

中文
如果崩解慢：崩解剂不足/类型不当；过度润滑（Mg stearate包覆）；片剂太硬/孔隙率低；包衣阻水；黏合剂过多。EN: If disintegration is slow: insufficient/wrong disintegrant; over-lubrication (Mg stearate coating); too hard/low porosity; hydrophobic coating; too much binder.
中文
如果崩解正常但溶出慢：通常是Cs低（难溶/晶型）或A不够（粒径大）或h大（搅拌弱），而不是崩解问题。EN: If disintegration OK but dissolution slow: usually low Cs (poor solubility/polymorph), low A (large particles), or large h (weak hydrodynamics).
中文
如果溶出快但吸收差：多考虑渗透性/外排（P-gp）/代谢首过；策略：促渗、前药、改变给药途径。EN: If dissolution is fast but absorption poor: consider permeability/efflux/first-pass; strategies: permeation enhancers, prodrugs, alternative routes.

A3. Tabletting — key definitions + Heckel

中文
Compression：压力使粉床致密化形成片剂。Compressibility：体积/孔隙率减少能力。Compactability：形成足够机械强度能力。EN: Compression: densification under stress. Compressibility: ability to reduce volume/porosity. Compactability: ability to build mechanical strength.
中文
Heckel：ln(1/(1−D)) = K·P + A。D相对密度，P压力。用途：比较材料塑性/脆性变形趋势（写出‘线性段’即可）。EN: Heckel: ln(1/(1−D)) = K·P + A (D relative density, P pressure). Use: compare plastic/brittle densification (mention linear region).
中文
压片四阶段写法：重排/碎裂 → 塑性变形 → 弹性变形 → 卸压回弹（回弹过大→capping/lamination）。EN: 4-stage writing: rearrangement/fragmentation → plastic deformation → elastic deformation → recovery (excess recovery → capping/lamination).

A4. Tablet QC — calculations you must be able to do

中文
抗张强度：σ = 2F/(π·D·e)。F(N)，D(mm)，e(mm)。注意单位一致。EN: Tensile strength: σ = 2F/(π·D·e). Keep consistent units (F in N; D,e in mm).
中文
脆碎度：F% = (m0−m)/m0 × 100；常用标准：F% < 1%。EN: Friability: F% = (m0−m)/m0 × 100; typical limit: < 1%.
中文
必须能写出 QC 清单（短题常考）：外观、片重差异、含量/均匀度、硬度/强度、脆碎度、崩解、溶出、必要时水分。EN: You must list QC tests: appearance, weight variation, assay/content uniformity, hardness/strength, friability, disintegration, dissolution, moisture if needed.

A5. Common tablet defects (short answers)

中文
Capping/lamination：空气夹带、弹性回弹、压力释放太快、含水不当；对策：预压、降低转速、优化含水、改造粒。EN: Capping/lamination: trapped air, elastic recovery, too fast decompression, wrong moisture; fixes: pre-compression, lower speed, optimize moisture, granulate.
中文
Sticking/picking：潮湿、润滑不足、冲头表面问题；对策：干燥/降粘、加润滑、抛光或换冲头。EN: Sticking/picking: moisture, insufficient lubrication, punch surface; fixes: dry/adjust binder, add lubricant, polish/change tooling.

A6. Blending (混合) — ordered mix and segregation

中文
Random mix vs ordered mix：随机混合靠概率均匀；有序混合靠细粉吸附到大颗粒（低剂量API常用）。EN: Random mix is probabilistic uniformity; ordered mix is adhesion of fines on carriers (useful for low-dose API).
中文
离析三原因（必背）：trajectories（粒径/密度导致运动轨迹不同）、percolation（细粉下渗）、elutriation（细粉被气流带走）。EN: 3 segregation causes: trajectories, percolation of fines, elutriation by airflow.
中文
润滑剂要点（高分句）：Mg stearate 过度混合会包覆颗粒表面→压片结合变差/硬度下降/溶出变慢；因此通常最后加入且短时间混合。EN: High-score line: overmixing Mg stearate coats particles → weaker bonding, lower hardness, slower dissolution; therefore add last and mix briefly.

A7. Granulation (制粒) — S* and wetting states

中文
S*（饱和度）= V_solution / V_interparticle。区间：<0.25 成核；≥0.25 稳定生长；0.9–1.0 crumb growth；≥1 过湿成浆。EN: S* = V_solution / V_interparticle. Ranges: <0.25 nucleation; ≥0.25 steady growth; 0.9–1.0 crumb growth; ≥1 slurry/overwetting.
中文
液桥/润湿状态写法：pendular（少量液桥）→ funicular（孔隙接近填满）→ capillary（完全填满）→ droplet（过湿）。EN: Wetting states: pendular → funicular → capillary → droplet (overwet).
中文
湿法制粒大题模板：目的→方法选择→粘结剂与喷液→混合/剪切→干燥（流化床/烘箱）→整粒→关键参数（S*、功率曲线、含水）。EN: Wet granulation problem template: goal → method → binder & spraying → mixing/shear → drying → milling/sieving → CPPs (S*, power curve, moisture).

A8. Freeze drying (冻干) — full bilingual answer you can memorize

中文
冻干三步：Freezing → Primary drying（升华去除冰，主要水分）→ Secondary drying（脱附去除结合水，降低残余水分）。
EN
3 steps: Freezing → Primary drying (sublimation removes ice, most water) → Secondary drying (desorption removes bound water, lowers residual moisture).
中文
关键温度：结晶体系看 Te（共晶温度）；无定形体系看 Tg'；超过限制温度会软化/融化并导致 collapse（塌陷）。EN: Key temperatures: crystalline system uses Te; amorphous system uses Tg'; exceeding limits causes softening/melting and collapse.
中文
必须写出的控制句：Primary drying 时产品温度必须低于 Tc（或低于 Te/Tg' 对应限制温度），否则孔结构塌陷→复溶变慢→质量下降。EN: Mandatory control sentence: during primary drying, product temperature must stay below Tc (or Te/Tg' limit); otherwise pore collapse slows reconstitution and degrades quality.
中文
三阶段与关键控制（综合题写法）—①Freezing：控制冷却速率与成核→冰晶大小→孔结构。快冻→小冰晶→孔小、升华阻力大但结构细；慢冻→大冰晶→孔大、更易干燥但结构粗。EN: 3 stages & controls (problem writing)—(1) Freezing: control cooling rate & nucleation → crystal size → pore structure. Fast freezing → small crystals → small pores, higher resistance but finer structure; slow freezing → large crystals → large pores, easier drying but coarser.
中文
②Primary drying（升华）：控制 shelf temperature + chamber pressure；提高 shelf T 可加快升华，但必须避免产品温度超过 Tc/Te/Tg'。EN: (2) Primary drying (sublimation): control shelf temperature + chamber pressure; higher shelf T speeds drying but must not push product T above Tc/Te/Tg'.
中文
③Secondary drying：升温去除结合水→残余水分降低→稳定性提高（抗水解/抗氧化）；对生物大分子避免过热失活。EN: (3) Secondary drying: raise temperature to remove bound water → lower residual moisture → better stability; avoid overheating biomolecules.

Solid-forms 'problem' universal template (write this in the exam)

中文
写作顺序：1) 目标（IR/ER/enteric + CQA）；2) API性质（solubility/pKa/logP/稳定性/剂量）；3) 选择工艺（混合→制粒→压片→包衣/冻干）；4) 辅料（作用：稀释/黏合/崩解/润滑/助流/包衣聚合物）；5) CPP（S*、压片压力、混合时间、包衣增重、冻干T/P）；6) QC（片重/含量/硬度/脆碎/崩解/溶出/稳定性）。EN: Writing order: (1) goal (IR/ER/enteric + CQAs); (2) API properties; (3) process route; (4) excipients & functions; (5) CPPs; (6) QC tests.

Part B — Liquid forms

B1. Preformulation checklist (liquids)

中文
必须检查：溶解度随pH变化、pKa、logP/logD、化学稳定性（氧化/水解/光）、物理稳定性（沉降/絮凝/破乳）、微生物风险与防腐剂、包装相容性。EN: Check: solubility vs pH, pKa, logP/logD, chemical stability (oxidation/hydrolysis/light), physical stability, microbiology & preservatives, container compatibility.
中文
溶解度不足的‘六联招’（闭卷背）：调pH/盐型/共溶剂/表活胶束/络合(环糊精)/改为混悬或干粉复溶。EN: 6 solubility levers: pH, salt, cosolvent, surfactant micelles, complexation (cyclodextrin), switch to suspension or dry powder for reconstitution.

B2. pKa and ionization (short Q writing)

中文
必背句：弱酸 pH > pKa 更电离；弱碱 pH < pKa 更电离。电离↑→水溶性↑；但电离↑→膜渗透性↓。EN: Memorize: weak acids ionize at pH > pKa; weak bases ionize at pH < pKa. More ionization → higher solubility but lower permeability.
中文
题型：给 pH 与 pKa，让你判断主要形态，并解释为什么调 pH 能提高溶解度（因为增加电离比例）。EN: Typical: given pH and pKa, decide dominant form; explain why pH adjustment increases solubility (increases ionized fraction).

B3. Tonicity (isotonicity) — calculation template

中文
等渗目的：减少注射/滴眼刺激，避免溶血；参照 0.9% NaCl。EN: Isotonicity: reduce irritation; avoid hemolysis; reference 0.9% NaCl.
中文
E-value算法模板：先算 API 的 NaCl 当量贡献（C_API×E），再用 0.9%−该贡献 = 需要补加的 NaCl 当量；再按等渗剂当量换算质量。EN: E-value template: isotonic contribution of API (C_API×E), remainder = 0.9% minus contribution, then convert using tonicity agent equivalent.

B4. Suspensions — Stokes and redispersibility

中文
Stokes：V = 2r^2(ρ_p−ρ_m)g/(9η)。让沉降慢：r↓、η↑、密度差↓。EN: Stokes: V = 2r^2(ρ_p−ρ_m)g/(9η). Reduce V by r↓, η↑, density difference↓.
中文
Flocculated vs deflocculated：絮凝沉降快但松散易再分散；非絮凝沉降慢但易结硬沉淀(caking)。EN: Flocculated settles faster but redisperses; deflocculated settles slower but may cake.
中文
综合题写法：先写‘控制粒径 + 提高黏度 + 控制絮凝 + 防腐’四件套，再写 QC：粒径分布、黏度、沉降体积比、再分散性、含量、微生物限度。EN: Problem writing: control size + increase viscosity + control flocculation + preservation; QC: size distribution, viscosity, sedimentation volume, redispersibility, assay, microbial limits.

B5. Emulsions — O/W vs W/O, HLB, and instability

中文
O/W：水为连续相；W/O：油为连续相。Bancroft rule：表活更溶于哪相，哪相更可能为连续相。EN: O/W water continuous; W/O oil continuous. Bancroft: phase where surfactant is more soluble becomes continuous.
中文
HLB：用于选择乳化剂（高HLB偏O/W，低HLB偏W/O）。混合：HLB_mix = Σ(f_i·HLB_i)。EN: HLB selects emulsifiers (higher HLB favors O/W; lower favors W/O). Blend rule: HLB_mix = Σ(f_i·HLB_i).
中文
失稳四件套：creaming、flocculation、coalescence、breaking。对策模板：粒径↓、黏度↑、合适表活（类型+浓度）、避免温度循环。EN: Instability: creaming, flocculation, coalescence, breaking. Fix template: smaller droplets, higher viscosity, right surfactant type/concentration, avoid thermal cycling.
中文
界面张力测量（可能作为加分题）：Vonnegut（旋转滴）γ = ω^2Δρ d^3 / 32（认识即可，重点是‘表活降低γ→更易形成乳滴’）。EN: Interfacial tension bonus: Vonnegut (spinning drop) γ = ω^2Δρ d^3 / 32; key idea: surfactants lower γ → easier droplet formation.

B6. Microemulsion vs nanoemulsion (short Q)

中文
Microemulsion：热力学稳定、透明/各向同性、可自发形成，但需高表活/助表活；Nanoemulsion：动力学稳定，通常高能量制备（高压均质）。EN: Microemulsion: thermodynamically stable, transparent/isotropic, often spontaneous but needs high surfactant/co-surfactant; Nanoemulsion: kinetically stable, high-energy prep (HPH).

Liquid-forms 'problem' universal template

中文
写作顺序：1) 选剂型（solution/suspension/emulsion）+理由；2) API性质（solubility vs pH, pKa, stability）；3) 处方：溶剂/表活/增稠/缓冲/抗氧/防腐；Prescription: solvent/surfactant/thickener/buffer/antioxidant/preservative; 4) 工艺：乳化/均质/脱气/过滤；Process: emulsification/homogenization/degassing/filtration;5) 关键参数：粒径、黏度、pH、等渗；Key parameters: particle size, viscosity, pH, isotonicity;6) QC：外观、含量、pH、黏度、粒径、微生物/无菌、稳定性。QC: appearance, content, pH, viscosity, particle size, microbiology/sterility, stability.

One-page formula sheet (memorize)

中文
溶出：dC/dt = (D·A/h)(Cs−Ct)；抗张强度：σ=2F/(πDe)；脆碎度：F%=(m0−m)/m0×100；Stokes：V=2r^2(ρp−ρm)g/(9η)；Heckel：ln(1/(1−D))=KP+A；S*=V_solution/V_interparticle。EN: Dissolution: dC/dt=(D·A/h)(Cs−Ct); tensile: σ=2F/(πDe); friability: F%=(m0−m)/m0×100; Stokes: V=2r^2(ρp−ρm)g/(9η); Heckel: ln(1/(1−D))=KP+A; S*=V_solution/V_interparticle.

生物药剂学与制剂策略 Biopharmaceutics and Formulation Strategies

1
药物在体内的基本过程及速率常数

Fate of Drugs in the Body & Associated Rate Constants

药物从服用到起效主要经历溶解、吸收、分布和消除阶段，各阶段由不同的速率常数表征。 The journey of a drug from administration to action involves dissolution, absorption, distribution, and elimination, each defined by different rate constants.

片剂服用后首先溶解（KdKd​），溶解产物被吸收进入血液（KaKa​），随后进入分布/消除阶段（KK）。 For tablets: First, dissolution (KdKd​) → absorption into blood (KaKa​) → distribution/elimination (KK).

通道如下
药片 Tablet --(KdKd​)→ 消化道 Digestive tract --(KaKa​)→ 血液 Bloodstream --(KK)→ 清除。 The route: Tablet --(KdKd​)→ Digestive tract --(KaKa​)→ Bloodstream --(KK)→ clearance.

公式：Dn=ttransittdissolutionDn​=tdissolution​ttransit​​，衡量穿过消化道所需时间与药物溶解时间的比值。 Dn=ttransittdissolutionDn​=tdissolution​ttransit​​ measures the ratio between gastrointestinal transit time and drug dissolution time.

课堂补充
老师强调速率常数对于药效起始和持续的关键意义。 Class note: The teacher stressed the critical importance of rate constants for onset and duration of drug action. 4
2
改善药物活性的制剂思路

Formulation Strategies to Improve Drug Activity

保护药物免于胃酸和酶的破坏（如肠溶包衣）。 Protect the drug from gastric acid and enzyme degradation (e.g., enteric coating).

减少胃肠道丧失，通过控制释放或改工艺提升溶出/吸收。 Reduce gastrointestinal loss by controlled release or improved dissolution/absorption.

采用缓释释放（sustained/prolonged release），降低给药频率、提升患者依从性。 Sustained/prolonged release decreases dosing frequency and improves patient compliance.

改变给药途径（如直肠/舌下/注射）避免首过效应。 Change route of administration (e.g., rectal, sublingual, injection) to bypass first-pass metabolism.

控制释放位置（速释、延释、定点释放）。 Control release location (immediate, delayed, or targeted release).

课堂补充
老师举例肠溶剂型/缓释剂型和药物吸收部位相关。 Class: The instructor exemplified enteric and sustained-release forms related to absorption sites. 2 4 5
3
常规和创新制剂策略的核心目标

Key Objectives of Conventional and Innovative Formulation Strategies

最大化药物吸收率和速度。 Maximize drug absorption rate and extent.

控制API（活性成分）的释放速度与模式。 Control API (Active Pharmaceutical Ingredient) release rate and pattern.

控制药代动力学参数（PK）（如延长半衰期、降低个体差异）。 Control pharmacokinetic (PK) parameters such as prolonging half-life and reducing variability.

课堂补充
老师补充PK和药效学（PD）均需关注。 Note: Both PK and pharmacodynamics (PD) are important as mentioned by the teacher. 6 22
4
影响药物吸收的药学属性

Pharmaceutical Properties Affecting Drug Absorption

粒径越小，比表面积增大，溶出更快，吸收更好（尤其对于难溶药物） Smaller particle size increases surface area, enhances dissolution, and improves absorption (critical for poorly soluble drugs).

药物及辅料的溶解度越高，越易吸收。 Higher solubility of drugs and excipients promotes absorption.

多晶型影响溶解速率和稳定性，同一成分不同晶型可能药效差异明显。 Polymorphism affects dissolution rate and stability; different crystal forms of the same drug may have distinct efficacy.

药物极性、pKa和logP决定其在胃肠道环境下的离子化和脂溶/水溶比例，进而影响吸收能力。 Drug polarity, pKa, and logP determine its ionization and distribution between lipophilic/hydrophilic environments, thus influencing absorption.

药物可经被动扩散或蛋白介导主动转运吸收，不同机制吸收速率差异大。 Drugs are absorbed via passive diffusion or carrier-mediated transport, greatly affecting absorption speed.

课堂补充
老师反复提到“物理化学参数决定处方设计”，要学会分析分子结构与吸收性能的关系。 Class highlight: "Physicochemical parameters dictate formulation design; learn the link between molecular structure and performance," the teacher repeatedly stressed. 7 8 19
5
药物递送系统类型及释药动力学

Drug Delivery System Types & Release Kinetics

按释药机制分
By release mechanism:

扩散控制型（如膜控、基质控）：药物通过膜或聚合物网络缓慢扩散。 Diffusion-controlled (e.g., reservoir or matrix): Drug diffuses slowly through membranes/polymers.

溶蚀/降解控制型
药物随载体降解/溶蚀而释放。 Erosion/degradation-controlled: Drug is released as carrier erodes/degrades.
渗透泵型
利用渗透压推动药物定量释放。 Osmotic pump: Drug is released at a controlled rate by osmotic pressure.

释药动力学常见模型有零级、一级、Higuchi等。 Common release kinetics: zero-order, first-order, and Higuchi models.

课堂补充
老师特别举例膜控、基质控系统在新型制剂开发中的应用。 Class addition: The teacher cited use of membrane and matrix systems in advanced formulations. 14 22 26

Class note
The instructor added that efficacy and safety must be addressed early; AMD and the “fate” of the molecule in vivo define the core for formulation design. 20

药物吸收影响因素与BCS分类系统

Factors Influencing Drug Absorption and the BCS Classification System

药物被吸收前，必须处于溶解状态，否则很难通过生物膜。 A drug must be in a dissolved state before absorption; otherwise, it is difficult to cross biological membranes.

在肠道上皮层，大多数小分子通过被动扩散跨膜，极性分子需通过转运蛋白协助。 In the intestinal epithelium, most small molecules cross membranes via passive diffusion, while polar molecules require transporters.

BCS系统根据药物的水溶性和膜透性将药物分为四类。 The BCS system classifies drugs into four categories according to solubility and membrane permeability.

I型药物水溶性高、透膜性高，体内吸收好，开发难度低。 Class I drugs have high solubility and permeability, are well absorbed, and are easy to develop.

II型药物水溶性低但透膜性高，吸收往往受溶出速率限制，需优化溶解度。 Class II drugs have low solubility but high permeability; absorption is limited by dissolution rate, so solubility enhancement is necessary.

III型药物水溶性高但透膜性低，吸收瓶颈在于跨膜效率。 Class III drugs have high solubility but low permeability; membrane crossing is the limiting factor for absorption.

IV型药物水溶性与透膜性都低，开发难度最大，吸收效果差。 Class IV drugs have both low solubility and low permeability, representing the greatest challenge in development with poor absorption.

BCS分类标准
高溶解度指最大剂量可在250 mL的水中、pH 1–7.5完全溶解；高透膜性指体内吸收率≥90%。 BCS criteria: high solubility means the maximal dose dissolves in 250 mL water (pH 1–7.5); high permeability is defined as ≥90% in vivo absorption.

药物分配系数及吸收关系

Drug Partition Coefficient and Absorption Relationship

分配系数（Partition coefficient, PP）反映药物亲脂性，常用对数（logP）表示。 The partition coefficient (PP) reflects drug lipophilicity and is usually expressed as logP.

药物logP适中时，穿过细胞膜能力最强，吸收效果好。 Drug permeability through membranes is optimal at a moderate logP, resulting in good absorption.

分配系数过高，药物可能过度脂溶，不易溶于水，吸收减少。 Excessively high partition coefficient leads to overly lipophilic drugs, decreasing water solubility and absorption.

分配系数过低，药物水溶性过强，难以穿膜，同样影响吸收。 Excessively low partition coefficient means the drug is too hydrophilic and cannot efficiently pass through membranes, also reducing absorption.

以巴比妥酸类为例，分配系数增加，结肠吸收比例明显提升。 For barbiturates, increased partition coefficient results in a significant increase in colonic absorption proportion.

药物溶解度、溶出速率与体内吸收

Drug Solubility, Dissolution Rate and In Vivo Absorption

溶解度及溶出速率决定药物吸收的快慢和程度。 Solubility and dissolution rate determine the speed and extent of drug absorption.

制剂晶型（如form 1与form 2）不同，溶解速度不同，导致血药浓度-时间曲线明显变化。 Different drug polymorphs (e.g., form 1 vs. form 2) exhibit distinct dissolution rates, leading to marked differences in plasma concentration-time profiles.

溶出速度快的药型可更快达到更高的血药浓度峰值。 A formulation with rapid dissolution will reach a higher and quicker plasma concentration peak.

溶解/溶出速率慢的药型，起效慢、吸收差。 Formulations with slower dissolution lead to delayed onset and poorer absorption.

总结与应用方法

Summary and Application Approach

解题时首先判别药物BCS分类，再结合logP判断透膜性，最后结合溶解度/溶出曲线综合预测吸收特点。 When analyzing, first determine the BCS class, then evaluate logP for permeability, and finally combine solubility/dissolution data for comprehensive absorption prediction.

教师特别强调
只有药物处于溶解状态，才能被有效吸收。 The instructor emphasizes: A drug must be in a dissolved state for efficient absorption.

吸收机制示例Absorption Mechanisms: Example (Case 2)

1
溶出行为对比

Dissolution Behavior Comparison

Product B（红色方块）比Product A（蓝色三角）溶出速度更快，溶出百分比更高。 Product B (red squares) dissolves faster and reaches a higher percentage dissolved than Product A (blue triangles).

在2小时内，Product B基本完全溶解，而Product A溶解速率较慢，8小时后仍未达最大。 Within 2 hours, Product B is almost completely dissolved, while Product A dissolves more slowly and does not reach maximum even at 8 hours.

2
生物利用度对比

Bioavailability Comparison

两种产品的血药浓度-时间曲线（C vs. Time）趋势相似，均在2~3小时左右达到峰值后下降。 Both products show similar plasma concentration-time profiles, peaking at around 2–3 hours, then declining.

Product B血药峰值略高且出现略早，但与Product A总体差异不大。 Product B has a slightly higher and earlier peak than Product A, although the overall differences are minimal.

3
解析与结论

Analysis and Conclusion

尽管溶出速率相差明显，但对于该药物，生物利用度并未呈现显著差异。 Despite clear differences in dissolution rate, the bioavailability is not significantly different for this drug.

说明溶出已不是吸收的限制步骤，可能药物吸收受制于其他因素，如跨膜转运或代谢。 This indicates that dissolution is no longer the rate-limiting step for absorption; factors like membrane transport or metabolism may be limiting instead.

案例启示
当药物的溶出速率足够快或不是瓶颈时，优化溶解度对提升生物利用度帮助有限，应关注制剂的其他因素。 Takeaway: When dissolution is no longer limiting, improving solubility may not enhance bioavailability; attention should shift to other formulation aspects.

溶解度与透膜性的关系Solubility vs Permeability

1
概念与机制

Concepts and Mechanisms

药物吸收分为两个主要过程
先溶解（由KDKD​控制），再跨膜吸收（由KAKA​控制）。 Drug absorption includes two major steps: dissolution (governed by KDKD​) followed by membrane permeation (governed by KAKA​).
2
BCS分类标准

BCS Classification Criteria

高溶解性
药物最大剂量可在250 mL水（pH 1–7.5）中完全溶解。 High solubility: The drug's highest dose strength is soluble in 250 mL (or less) of aqueous medium over pH 1–7.5.
高透膜性
体内吸收率达到90%或以上。 High permeability: The drug shows at least 90% absorption of the administered dose.
3
不同类别药物的分布（以BCS分类，按溶解度和透膜性）

Distribution of Different Classes (Based on BCS Categories: Solubility & Permeability)

市场上市药物 Marketed Drugs：

1类
高溶解、高透膜——占35%。 Class 1: High solubility, high permeability — 35%.
2类
低溶解、高透膜——占30%。 Class 2: Low solubility, high permeability — 30%.
3类
高溶解、低透膜——占25%。 Class 3: High solubility, low permeability — 25%.
4类
低溶解、低透膜——占10%。 Class 4: Low solubility, low permeability — 10%.

新分子实体 New Molecular Entities：

1类
高溶解、高透膜——占5%。 Class 1: High solubility, high permeability — 5%.
2类
低溶解、高透膜——占70%。 Class 2: Low solubility, high permeability — 70%.
3类
高溶解、低透膜——占5%。 Class 3: High solubility, low permeability — 5%.
4类
低溶解、低透膜——占20%。 Class 4: Low solubility, low permeability — 20%.
4
现象与实际意义

Observations & Practical Significance

目前上市药物以1类（高溶解高透膜）和2类（高透膜低溶解）为主。 The majority of marketed drugs are Class 1 or Class 2.

新药分子主要分布在2类和4类（高比例为低溶解，高透膜或低透膜）。 Most new drug entities are either Class 2 or Class 4 (mostly low solubility with varying permeability).

说明药物开发难度增加，溶解性差成为新分子开发瓶颈，需要重点关注增溶技术与透膜性提升措施。 This indicates increasing formulary challenges for new drugs, emphasizing the need for solubility enhancement and permeability improvement strategies.

难溶性药物的制剂工艺方法

Formulation Approaches for Poorly Water Soluble Drugs

主要制剂和工艺策略

Main Formulation and Process Strategies

共溶剂法 Co-solvents 通过将药物溶解于两种以上互溶溶剂体系中，提高溶解度。 Drugs are dissolved in a mixture of two or more miscible solvents to enhance solubility.

表面活性剂 Surfactants 利用表面活性剂形成胶束，将药物包裹以增加溶解度。 Surfactants form micelles that encapsulate drugs, increasing solubility.

脂质基制剂 Lipid-based systems 药物溶解或分散于脂溶体系（如油/脂肪、乳剂），提高体内溶解和吸收。 Drugs are dissolved/dispersed in lipid systems (oils, emulsions) to enhance dissolution and absorption.

无定形固体分散体 Amorphous solid dispersions 利用无定形状态提高药物分子间分散及溶解度。 Amorphous dispersions increase molecular dispersion and improve solubility.

盐制剂 Salts 通过离子交换将原药物制成盐，提高可溶性。 Conversion to salt forms improves solubility.

环糊精包合物 Cyclodextrins 利用环糊精包埋药物，增强其水溶性。 Cyclodextrin inclusion complexes enhance drug solubility in water.

微粒化/粒径减小 Particle size reduction

减小药物粒径增加比表面积，加速溶解。 Smaller particle size increases surface area and dissolution rate.

共晶体 Co-crystals 药物与共晶形成新固体结构，改善溶解性。 Co-crystal formation improves solubility by altering solid structure.

晶型多态选择 Polymorphs 选择溶解度更高的晶型，提高药物溶解速率。 Selecting more soluble crystal forms can enhance dissolution.

总结与用途

Summary and Application

针对难溶性药物，可采用上述多种策略单独或组合提升溶解度和体内吸收。 For poorly water-soluble drugs, these approaches can be used alone or in combination to improve solubility and bioavailability.

具体选用何种策略，要根据药物的理化性质、剂型、患者需求等综合考虑。 The final strategy should be determined based on drug properties, dosage form, and patient requirements.

药物增溶——化学法

Drug Solubilisation — Chemical Approach

1
制剂及工艺方法

Formulation and Process Approaches

药物盐（Drug salts） 将药物转化为盐类（如：盐酸盐、钠盐、硫酸盐等）能显著提高其水溶性。 Converting drugs into salts (such as hydrochloride, sodium, sulfate, etc.) can greatly enhance water solubility.

前药（Prodrug） 设计药物化学结构，体内转化为活性药物，以改善溶解度和吸收性。 Prodrugs are structurally modified drugs that are transformed into active forms in the body to improve solubility and absorption.

2
药物盐在医药中的应用

Application of Drug Salts in Medicine

超过50%的药物以盐形式存在，最常见为盐酸盐、钠盐和硫酸盐。 Over 50% of all drug molecules are used as salts, most frequently hydrochloride, sodium, and sulfate.

药物多为弱酸或弱碱，直接用游离体易溶解度低，影响吸收。 Drugs are often weak acids or bases, but their free forms may be suboptimal for dissolution and absorption.

通过加入惰性盐类，可提升药物的吸收和疗效。 Adding an inactive salt helps enhance drug absorption and effectiveness.

3
药用盐的常见种类及比例

Common Pharmaceutical Salts and Their Frequencies

最常用阴离子（Anion）：

盐酸根（Hydrochloride）：39.96%

硫酸根（Sulphate）：10.58%

醋酸根（Acetate）：6.70%

磷酸根（Phosphate）：4.97%

氯离子（Chloride）：4.54%

最常用阳离子（Cation）：

钠（Sodium）：62.79%

钾（Potassium）：11.05%

钙（Calcium）：8.72%

铝（Aluminium）：4.65%

4
总结要点

Key Takeaways

盐型选择和前药设计是提升难溶性药物体内可用性的重要化学策略。 Salt selection and prodrug design are major chemical strategies for improving bioavailability of poorly soluble drugs.

药物增溶
前药实例

Drug Solubilisation: Example of Prodrug

1
例子说明

Example Explanation

Benorilate 是一种前药，其化学名：4-乙酰氨基苯基 2-乙酰氧基苯甲酸酯（又称阿司匹林-对乙酰氨基酚酯）。 Benorilate is a prodrug: its chemical name is 4-acetamidophenyl 2-acetoxybenzoate (aspirin-acetaminophen ester).

2
代谢与释放机制

Metabolism & Release Mechanism

经体内酶促水解，Benorilate释放：

阿司匹林（aspirin）

对乙酰氨基酚（acetaminophen） Benorilate is hydrolyzed enzymatically in the body to release aspirin and acetaminophen.

反应类型为
酯键水解反应（Hydrolysis of ester bond） Enzymatic hydrolysis splits the ester bond, yielding two active drugs.
3
结构对应解析

Structural Relationships

Benorilate（左图）： 同时含有阿司匹林和对乙酰氨基酚的结构基团，通过酯键相连。 Left: Benorilate structure combines both aspirin and acetaminophen via an ester bond.

Aspirin（中图）： 释放的阿司匹林分子结构。 Middle: Released aspirin structure.

Acetaminophen（右图）： 释放的对乙酰氨基酚结构。 Right: Released acetaminophen structure.

4
作用机制与增溶意义

Mode of Action & Solubilisation Significance

前药设计优势：

1
改善原有药物的溶解性与吸收性。
2
提高复方制剂依从性，减少刺激性。
3
体内代谢生成有效成分，实现治疗作用。 Prodrug design can improve the solubility and bioavailability of poorly soluble drugs. Upon metabolism, active drugs are released.
5
小结

Summary

药物前体（前药）通过体内酶解，释放原有效成分，是提升难溶性药物可用性的重要化学策略。 Prodrugs are an important chemical approach in drug solubilisation, as they release active ingredients upon enzymatic hydrolysis in the body.

药物增溶——制剂及工艺方法

Drug Solubilisation — Formulation and Process Approaches

1
化学与工艺方法总览

Overview of Approaches

药物盐（Drug salts） 通过将药物转化成盐类，提高其水溶性和体内吸收率。 Conversion to salt form increases solubility and absorption.

前药（Prodrug） 改造药物结构，利用体内酶转变为活性成分，以改善溶解性/吸收性。 Prodrugs are metabolized in vivo to active forms, improving solubility and absorption.

粒径减小（Particle Size Reduction, Grinding） 通过粉碎等方式减小药物颗粒大小，增加比表面积，提高溶解速率。 Grinding decreases particle size, increases surface area, and improves dissolution rate.

2
粒径减小实例
Example
Particle Size Reduction

苯乙胺（Phenacetin）实验：

在 20°C 下，苯乙胺水中溶解度极低（<1mg/mL）。

通过调整颗粒粒径，增溶效果显著提升：

0.11-0.15 mm 颗粒溶解最快，溶出量最高；

0.21-0.30 mm 次之；

0.50-0.71 mm 颗粒溶出最慢，量最低。

图示显示，颗粒越小——溶出速率和溶出总量都明显升高（其他条件不变）。 Smaller particle size (0.11-0.15 mm) greatly increases dissolution rate and amount, compared to larger sizes (e.g., 0.50-0.71 mm), as demonstrated in phenacetin.

3
机理解析

Mechanism Explanation

原理：

粒径减小后，比表面积增加，与溶剂的接触面更大，促进溶解。

适用于难溶性药物的增溶，是工业界常用技术之一。 Smaller particles have more surface area for solvent interaction, leading to faster dissolution—widely used for poorly soluble drugs.

4
要点小结

Key Takeaways

盐型、前药设计、粒径减小（物理粉碎）是提升药物溶解度的常见方法。

粒径越小，药物越容易、越快溶解，有利于提高生物利用度。

实际工艺常结合多种方法，最优提升药物体内可用性。

药物增溶Drug solubilisation——配方与工艺方法及环糊精Formulation and process approaches & Cyclodextrins

1
配方与工艺增溶方法总结

Formulation and process approaches for drug solubilisation

药物盐（Drug salts） 将药物制成盐形式以提升溶解度。 Drugs can be formulated as salts to improve solubility.

前药（Prodrug） 设计为体内可转化为活性药物的前体分子。 Design prodrugs which are inactive until metabolized into the active drug in vivo.

粒径减小（研磨，Particle size reduction, milling） 通过减小颗粒大小提高溶解速率。 Reducing particle size by milling increases the dissolution rate.

环糊精↑（Cyclodextrins） 利用环糊精包合，提升疏水性药物在水中的溶解度。 Use cyclodextrins to form inclusion complexes and enhance the aqueous solubility of hydrophobic drugs.

2
环糊精的结构与原理

Cyclodextrin structure and mechanism

环糊精为环状低聚糖，包括α-CD（6个葡萄糖单元）、β-CD（7个）、γ-CD（8个）。 Cyclodextrins are cyclic oligosaccharides: α-CD (6 glucose units), β-CD (7 units), γ-CD (8 units).

它们拥有疏水性空腔和亲水性外表面。 They have a hydrophobic internal cavity and a hydrophilic outer surface.

疏水性药物分子（API）可被包裹进空腔，从而提升其水溶解度。 Hydrophobic API molecules can be encapsulated in the cyclodextrin cavity, enhancing their water solubility.

3
环糊精的种类与应用

Types and applications of cyclodextrins

常见有α-环糊精、β-环糊精、γ-环糊精，可天然存在或经修饰（如HPβCD, SBEβCD, MeβCD等）。 Common types include α-, β-, and γ-cyclodextrins, either natural or modified (such as HPβCD, SBEβCD, MeβCD).

它们常用于提升疏水性药物的溶出速率和生物利用度。 Cyclodextrins are commonly used to improve the dissolution rate and bioavailability of poorly water-soluble drugs.

4
结构对比说明

Structural comparison

α-, β-, γ-CD空腔大小逐级增大，适用不同分子大小的药物。 α-, β-, and γ-CD have increasing cavity sizes, suitable for drugs of different molecular dimensions.

右侧图示显示环糊精如何通过疏水性空腔捕获疏水药物分子，外部的亲水基团保证整体分子的水溶性。 The figure illustrates how cyclodextrins capture hydrophobic drugs in their cavity, while hydrophilic groups on the exterior confer overall aqueous solubility.

5
课堂音频要点补充

Key points from lecture recordings

老师强调，药物增溶方法多样，环糊精是一种重要且高效的增溶辅料；其选择需根据API的理化性质。 The lecturer emphasized that there are multiple strategies for drug solubilisation, with cyclodextrins being an important and effective excipient; their use is determined by the physicochemical properties of the API.

讲述了环糊精复杂合物的结构原理及工业应用（如通过外源添加、选择合适类型），实际操作中可与粒径减小等方法配合使用。 The lecturer discussed the principle and industrial application of cyclodextrin inclusion complexes (including exogenous addition and type selection), and highlighted that they are often used in combination with other methods such as particle size reduction.

药物增溶Drug solubilisation——配方与工艺方法及表面活性剂（胶束增溶）Formulation and process approaches & Surfactants (micellar solubilisation)

1
药物增溶的配方与工艺方法 Formulation and process approaches for drug solubilisation

药物盐 | Drug salts 通过将药物转化为盐增加水溶性。 Drug salts increase aqueous solubility by converting the drug into its salt form.

前药 | Prodrug 设计成体内转化为活性成分的前体以提高溶解。 Prodrugs are designed to be converted into the active drug in vivo to improve solubility.

粒径减小（研磨）| Particle size reduction (milling) 降低颗粒大小以增加比表面积和溶解速率。 Reducing particle size increases the surface area and speeds up dissolution.

环糊精 | Cyclodextrins 环糊精包合可以提升难溶药物的溶解性。 Cyclodextrins form inclusion complexes that enhance the solubility of poorly soluble drugs.

表面活性剂（胶束增溶）| Surfactants (micellar solubilisation) 利用表面活性剂形成胶束来包裹疏水药物，显著提升溶解度。 Surfactants form micelles that encapsulate hydrophobic drugs, markedly increasing their solubility 39.

2
表面活性剂类型与机理 Types and mechanisms of surfactants

可为小分子表面活性剂也可为聚合物表面活性剂。 Surfactants can be small molecules or polymer surfactants.

典型结构
如聚合物表面活性剂PEO-PPO-PEO，亲水和疏水基团共存。 Example: Polymer surfactant PEO–PPO–PEO contains both hydrophilic and hydrophobic segments.

在水中低浓度下，表面活性剂首先吸附于界面，降低表面张力； At low concentrations, surfactants first adsorb at the interface, reducing surface tension.

当浓度超过临界胶束浓度（CMC）后，自发形成胶束包裹药物分子；药物以胶束为载体被包埋在溶液中。 When above the critical micelle concentration (CMC), surfactants spontaneously form micelles that encapsulate insoluble drug molecules, allowing them to stay dispersed in solution 39.

3
老师课堂讲解补充要点 Additional key points from the lecture

老师重点强调，表面活性剂通过降低水的界面张力，在药物增溶中起到关键作用。 The lecturer emphasized that surfactants play a vital role in solubilisation by lowering water’s surface tension.

胶束在水中形成内部疏水核心，能有效包裹不溶性药物，从而提升溶解与吸收。 Micelles provide a hydrophobic core that can solubilize otherwise insoluble drugs, enhancing both dissolution and absorption.

绘图解释了随表面活性剂浓度增加，表面张力下降及胶束形成过程。 The diagram explains how, as surfactant concentration increases, surface tension drops and micelle formation occurs.

该方法在药物工业实际中十分常见，特别适用于液体制剂和难溶性药物。 This approach is widely used in the pharmaceutical industry, especially for liquid formulations and poorly soluble drugs 39, 12.

2
水溶性溶剂三元相图讲解 Ternary phase diagram for water miscible solvents

通过不同配比的水、乙醇和丙二醇，可以调节整体体系的极性，从而提升API的溶解性。 By varying the ratios of water, ethanol, and propylene glycol, the polarity of the system can be tuned, enhancing API solubility.

三元图中，API的溶解度随各组分比例变化而变化。例：当API为2%时，最佳溶液约为水12.9%、乙醇67.8%、丙二醇19.3%；而API为5%时，最佳配比为水12-16%、乙醇33%、丙二醇51-55%。 In the ternary diagram, API solubility depends on the formulation. For 2% API: about water 12.9%, ethanol 67.8%, propylene glycol (PG) 19.3%. For 5% API: water 12-16%, ethanol 33%, PG 51-55%.

3
重要物理参数——介电常数 Key physical parameter: Dielectric constant
不同溶剂的介电常数决定其极性与溶解性
水ε=80，丙二醇ε=36，乙醇ε=24。 Dielectric constants indicate polarity and solubilisation ability: water ε=80, PG ε=36, ethanol ε=24.

利用不同极性的溶剂组合，可以针对性地优化API的溶解环境。 By mixing solvents of different polarity, the solubilising environment for the API can be optimized.

4
课堂讲解要点补充 Additional key points from the lecture

老师强调，通过调配溶剂及调节极性，能显著提高难溶API在液体制剂中的溶解极限。 The lecturer emphasized that adjusting solvent composition and polarity can dramatically improve the maximum solubility of poorly soluble APIs in liquid formulations.

三元相图直观表现了各种溶剂组合下API的最大溶解度，为实际药物配方提供理论依据。 The ternary diagram visually presents the maximum solubility of API in various solvent combinations, offering a practical reference for pharmaceutical formulation.

使用有机溶剂时需兼顾安全性、毒性以及法规限制。 When using organic solvents, safety, toxicity, and regulatory issues should all be considered.

1
亲水性高分子的增溶应用 Hydrophilic polymers for solubilisation

亲水性高分子是一类能显著提高难溶性药物在水中分散性和表观溶解度的辅料。 Hydrophilic polymers are excipients that can significantly enhance the dispersibility and apparent solubility of poorly water-soluble drugs33。

常用做法为将疏水药物与亲水高分子以及一定量溶剂混合，得到药物-高分子溶液。 The typical method is mixing the hydrophobic drug with a hydrophilic polymer and a solvent to obtain a drug-polymer solution33。

随后对该溶液进行干燥，形成固体药物-聚合物干块。 Then, the solution is dried to form a solid drug-polymer pellet33。

最后将干块研磨，得到水溶性的药物-高分子微粒，有利于后续分散及口服吸收。 Finally, the dry pellet is ground to yield water-soluble drug-polymer particles, facilitating further dispersion and oral absorption33。

2
老师讲解重点 Highlights from the lecture

老师强调，亲水高分子的引入能帮助难溶性API实现固体分散体，整体提升溶解速率。 The lecturer emphasized that introducing hydrophilic polymers can assist poorly soluble APIs by forming solid dispersions, greatly improving the dissolution rate1233。

工艺的核心在于药物与高分子的充分接触与均匀混合，以及干燥和研磨过程的参数优化。 The process core is adequate drug-polymer contact and uniform mixing, as well as optimal drying and grinding parameters12。

聚合物的具体种类和比例影响API的增溶效果，常选用PVP、HPMC等水溶性聚合物。 The specific type and proportion of polymer affect the solubilising effect, with PVP and HPMC being common water-soluble choices33。

3
课堂内容补充 Additional content from class

通过药物-高分子复合，API分子被均一分散于亲水基体中，减少团聚，便于水化。 By drug-polymer complexing, API molecules are homogeneously dispersed in a hydrophilic matrix, reducing aggregation and facilitating hydration33。

这种方法特别适用于难溶药物的固体口服制剂开发。 This method is especially suitable for developing solid oral formulations of poorly soluble drugs33。

在实验和工业制备中，需要优化混合、干燥和磨粉条件以获得最佳增溶效果。 In both experimental and industrial preparation, mixing, drying, and milling conditions must be optimized for the best solubilisation outcome

药物增溶的制剂和工艺方法 Formulation and process approaches for drug solubilisation

制备药物盐可以提高药物溶解度。 Preparing drug salts can improve drug solubility.

前药策略通过化学修饰提升溶解性或穿透性。 The prodrug strategy utilizes chemical modification to enhance solubility or permeability.

通过研磨等方式减小颗粒尺寸，可以增大比表面积，加快溶解速率。 Particle size reduction (milling) increases specific surface area, accelerating dissolution rate.

环糊精能通过包合疏水性药物提升其溶解度。 Cyclodextrins can form inclusion complexes with hydrophobic drugs to increase solubility.

表面活性剂通过胶束增溶，提高难溶性药物的溶解度。 Surfactants achieve micellar solubilisation, enhancing the solubility of poorly soluble drugs.

添加水溶性有机溶剂，可获得液体制剂以提高药物溶解度。 Adding water-miscible organic solvents allows liquid formulations for enhanced drug solubility.

亲水性聚合物作为辅料可促进药物溶解或分散。 Hydrophilic polymers as excipients can promote drug dissolution or dispersion.

脂质基系统（如油/水乳剂）适用于难溶性药物，将进一步讲解。 Lipid-based systems (such as oil/water emulsion) are suitable for poorly water-soluble drugs and will be discussed in detail later.

下方图片为油包水乳剂（o/w emulsion）的显微照片，展示了其典型结构。 The image below shows a microscopic view of an oil-in-water (o/w) emulsion, illustrating its typical structure.

目标2
活性药物成分的控释 | Objective 2: Controlled release of the API

药物开发中的一个重要目标是通过配方设计实现活性药物成分（API）的受控释放。 One important goal in drug development is to achieve controlled release of the active pharmaceutical ingredient (API) through formulation design.

受控释放可以帮助维持体内稳定的药物浓度，从而延长疗效，减少给药次数，并降低副作用风险。 Controlled release helps to maintain a stable drug concentration in the body, thus prolonging efficacy, reducing dosing frequency, and minimizing side effects.

实现控释的方法通常包括改变制剂结构（如片剂骨架、包衣、微粒、胶束、缓释/肠溶涂层等），以调节API在给定时间和部位的释放速度。 Methods to achieve controlled release typically involve altering the formulation structure (such as matrix tablets, coatings, microparticles, micelles, sustained/enteric coatings, etc.) to modulate the release rate of API at specific times and locations.

控释策略可以针对药物在胃肠道的释放、吸收、代谢等多环节进行优化，最终目标是改善药物的疗效和安全性。 Controlled release strategies can optimize drug release, absorption, and metabolism in the gastrointestinal tract, with the ultimate goal of improving drug efficacy and safety.

设计合适的控释制剂需要根据药物的理化性质、应用需求和患者依从性综合考量。 The design of suitable controlled release formulations requires comprehensive consideration of the drug’s physicochemical properties, application requirements, and patient compliance.

控释制剂系统（Controlled release DDS）可以通过改变药物的生物药剂学特性和药代动力学，实现药物在体内受控释放。 Controlled release drug delivery systems (DDS) can modulate drug biopharmaceutics and pharmacokinetics, enabling controlled drug release in the body.

通过控释制剂，药物血浆浓度能维持在治疗窗口（即最大耐受浓度MTC与最低有效浓度MEC之间），从而提升疗效并减少副作用。 By using controlled release formulations, plasma drug concentration can be maintained within the therapeutic window—between the maximum tolerated concentration (MTC) and the minimum effective concentration (MEC)—thus improving efficacy and reducing side effects.

图中展示了不同制剂类型的血药浓度-时间曲线，包括未修饰（蓝色）、加速释放（绿色）、持续释放（红色）和延迟释放（棕色）制剂。 The figure shows plasma concentration-time profiles for various formulation types: unmodified (blue), accelerated release (green), sustained release (red), and delayed release (brown).

未修饰制剂（Unmodified）释放快，血药浓度迅速上升后下降，易导致峰谷波动和副作用。 Unmodified formulations release the drug rapidly; plasma concentration rises quickly and then drops, resulting in fluctuating peaks and troughs and potential side effects.

持续释放（Sustained）和延迟释放（Delayed）制剂能显著延长药效，保持稳定血药浓度，减少给药频率，提高患者依从性。 Sustained and delayed release formulations notably prolong drug action, maintain a steady plasma concentration, reduce dosing frequency, and improve patient compliance.

控释制剂的优化需考虑药物本身的理化性质、治疗需求和预期的吸收部位，以实现理想的治疗效果。 Optimization of controlled release systems requires considering the drug’s physicochemical properties, therapeutic needs, and intended absorption sites to achieve desired therapeutic outcomes.

Accelerated release 加速释放

加速释放旨在调节药物的吸收速度，提升药物在体内的起效速度。 Accelerated release aims to modulate the absorption rate of drugs, enhancing the onset of action in the body.

加速释放片剂（Accelerated release tablets）主要通过优化片剂崩解和溶出过程实现。 Accelerated release tablets mainly achieve this by optimizing tablet disintegration and dissolution processes.

常规的片剂崩解采用“膨胀及外拉”模式，速度有限，导致血药浓度-时间曲线上升较慢（见绿线）。 Conventional tablet disintegration uses a "swelling and drawing out" mode, which is relatively slow, causing a slower rise in the plasma concentration-time profile (see green curve).

加速释放方案如可分散片（dispersible tablets）、泡腾片（effervescent tablets）、快速崩解（flash disintegration）可显著加快药物释放，血药浓度更快达到峰值（见蓝线）。 Accelerated methods such as dispersible tablets, effervescent tablets, and flash disintegration can significantly speed up drug release, resulting in a faster peak plasma concentration (see blue curve).

泡腾片通过酸碱反应释放CO₂，产生气泡促使药片迅速崩解并溶解。 Effervescent tablets utilize acid-base reactions to release CO₂, producing bubbles that rapidly disintegrate and dissolve the tablet.

快速崩解片通常含有特定赋形剂（如RXCIPIENTS™FM 1000），能够迅速吸水膨胀并碎裂为细微颗粒，极大提升初始溶解速度。 Flash disintegration tablets often contain specific excipients (such as RXCIPIENTS™FM 1000), which can rapidly absorb water, swell, and break up into fine particles, dramatically increasing the initial dissolution rate.

老师补充，采用加速释放策略可以使药物快速起效，适用于急性止疼、退烧等场景，但需注意峰浓度过高时的安全性问题。 The instructor added that accelerated release strategies enable drugs to take effect quickly, suitable for rapid pain relief or fever reduction, but attention should be paid to potential safety concerns from high peak concentrations.

持续释放制剂系统（sustained release DDS）

持续释放（Sustained release）的目标是使药物在体内释放维持数小时至数年，根据系统不同可实现不同时间尺度。 The objective of sustained release is to maintain drug release in the body from several hours to several years, depending on the drug delivery system.

持续释放制剂有助于血药浓度在治疗窗口内保持稳定，减少浓度峰谷波动，从而减少副作用并提高治疗效果和依从性。 Sustained release dosage forms help keep plasma drug concentrations within the therapeutic window, reducing peaks and troughs, thus minimizing side effects and improving therapeutic outcomes and patient compliance.

图中红线代表持续释放制剂
药物浓度较为平稳，在24小时内逐渐下降。虚线则代表普通（多次）给药方式：血药浓度多个峰谷波动，容易出现超出治疗范围的情况。 The red curve in the diagram represents sustained release: drug concentration remains relatively stable and gradually decreases within 24 hours. The dashed line represents conventional (multiple dosing) modes: plasma concentration fluctuates with peaks and valleys, easily exceeding the therapeutic range.

持续释放DDS的扩散机制 | Diffusion mechanisms in sustained release DDS

持续释放的常见实现方式是通过扩散机制，调节药物在体内的释放速率。 Sustained release is commonly achieved by diffusion mechanisms that control the rate of drug release in the body.

储库型（reservoir-based）制剂：药物通过膜控扩散释放，分为恒定活性（C0>CsC0​>Cs​）和非恒定活性（C0<CsC0​<Cs​）两类。 Reservoir-based DDS: drug diffuses through a membrane, with either constant activity (C0>CsC0​>Cs​) or nonconstant activity (C0<CsC0​<Cs​).

矩阵型（matrix-based）制剂：药物通过不可膨胀聚合物或无机材料扩散，可分为未饱和（C0<CsC0​<Cs​）和饱和（C0>CsC0​>Cs​）矩阵系统。 Matrix-based DDS: drug diffuses through a nonswelling polymer or inorganic matrix, either as an unsaturated matrix system (C0<CsC0​<Cs​) or saturated matrix system (C0>CsC0​>Cs​).

另一类为通过高分子材料网状结构（swollen polymer network）扩散释放。 Another type is matrix-based DDS: diffusion through a swollen polymer network.

生物降解型制剂（bio-erodible DDS）则是通过基质的缓慢降解实现药物的持续释放。 Bio-erodible DDS: drug is released by matrix erosion as the material slowly degrades.

补充讲解 | Supplement from the lecture

老师强调，持续释放系统的设计可极大提高患者依从性，适用于需要长效维持血药浓度的疾病（如慢性病）。 The lecturer emphasized that sustained release systems greatly improve patient compliance and are especially suitable for diseases requiring long-term maintenance of therapeutic drug levels (e.g., chronic diseases).

持续释放的关键技术包括
选择合适的膜材料与聚合物，合理设计药物与基体的比例与分布，实现稳定、可控的体内释放曲线。 Key technologies include selecting appropriate membranes and polymers, rationally designing drug-to-matrix ratios and distribution, to achieve a stable and controlled in vivo release profile.

缓控释DDS及其机制 DDS for Sustained and Controlled Release

膜控型缓释DDS
通过膜扩散实现药物释放 Membrane-controlled sustained release DDS: Release by diffusion through a membrane

膜控型贮库系统通常采用聚合物膜包裹药物溶液或分散体，药物通过膜扩散释放，可以实现恒定或非恒定活性。 Reservoir-based (membrane-controlled) systems typically encapsulate a drug solution or dispersion in a polymer membrane, allowing the drug to diffuse out; this can lead to either constant or nonconstant drug activity.

水分渗透进入体系，药物经膜以受控速率向外扩散，经典如渗透泵（如OROS®系统）。 Water penetrates the system and the drug diffuses outward through the membrane at a controlled rate; a classic example is the osmotic pump (e.g., OROS® system).

基质型缓释DDS
通过不可膨胀基质扩散 Matrix-based sustained release DDS: Diffusion through a nonswelling matrix

药物分散于不可膨胀聚合物或无机基质中，水分渗透后药物分子经扩散释放。 The drug is dispersed in a nonswelling polymer or inorganic matrix; after water permeates, the drug molecules are released via diffusion.

这种机制可实现药物的稳定、持续释放，见常规含药基质片。 This mechanism enables a stable, prolonged drug release, as often seen in standard matrix tablets.

基质型缓释DDS
通过膨胀型高分子网络扩散 Matrix-based sustained release DDS: Diffusion through a swollen polymer network

药物分布于可吸水膨胀的高分子三维网络中，吸水后聚合物膨胀，药物溶出经扩散前沿逐步释出。 The drug is dispersed in a hydrophilic, swellable polymer network; upon water uptake, the polymer swells and the drug is released progressively through the diffusion front.

具有典型的“膨胀前沿—扩散前沿—侵蚀前沿”结构，适合递送难溶性药物。 This has characteristic “swelling front – diffusion front – erosion front” zones, suitable for delivering poorly soluble drugs.

生物降解型缓释DDS
由基质侵蚀控制 Bio-erodible controlled release DDS: Release by matrix erosion

可降解基质包埋药物，体液进入后基质逐渐侵蚀，药物随基质降解而持续释放。 The drug is embedded in a biodegradable matrix; as body fluids penetrate, the matrix erodes and the drug is steadily released with the degradation.

特点是基质最终完全降解，适用于需植入且无需取出的系统。 The matrix is eventually fully degraded, suitable for implantable systems where removal is not needed.

5
各种缓控释机制总结 Summary of controlled release mechanisms

膜控型、基质控型、膨胀控型与侵蚀控型是主要持续释放机制，具体应用取决于药物性质与治疗需求。 Membrane-controlled, matrix-controlled, swelling-controlled, and erosion-controlled mechanisms are the main sustained/controlled release routes; specific application depends on drug properties and therapeutic needs.

这些系统可实现血药浓度平稳，减少给药次数，提高患者依从性与安全性。 These systems help maintain stable drug levels, reduce dosing frequency, and improve patient compliance and safety.

 释放动力学总结

1
Release Kinetics Summary

三种主要释药动力学模型对应不同缓控释系统。 Three main drug release kinetic models correspond to different controlled/sustained release systems.

1
零级动力学 Mt∼tMt​∼t
2
Zero-order kinetics Mt∼tMt​∼t

药物释放速率恒定，与剩余药物浓度无关。 Drug is released at a constant rate, independent of remaining drug amount.

典型系统为储库型DDS（恒定活性）、平面可降解系统和简单渗透泵片剂。 Typical systems: reservoir-based DDS (constant activity), planar erodible systems, simple osmotic tablets.

3
Higuchi扩散模型 Mt∼tMt​∼t​
4
Higuchi (diffusion) model Mt∼tMt​∼t​

药物释放受扩散控制，速率随时间减慢。 Release is diffusion-controlled; rate decreases with time.

主要见于短时间下的不饱和或饱和平板型基质。 Seen in unsaturated planar matrices at short times or saturated planar matrices (Higuchi model).

5
一级动力学 Mt∼A−Bexp⁡(−Ct)Mt​∼A−Bexp(−Ct)
6
First-order kinetics Mt∼A−Bexp⁡(−Ct)Mt​∼A−Bexp(−Ct)

药物释放速率与剩余未释出药量呈指数衰减关系。 Release rate decays exponentially with unreleased drug amount.

适用于非恒定活性的平面储库系统，以及球形/平面基质长期释放。 Applies to planar reservoir-based DDS (non-constant activity), spherical/planar matrices at long time.

2
曲线理解与释药机制
2
Curve Interpretation and Release Mechanisms

曲线图中直线为零级释放，斜率恒定；平方根型逐渐减缓；指数型则迅速趋于平台。 In the graph: straight line is zero-order (constant slope), square-root curve flattens with time, and exponential form rapidly reaches a plateau.

可通过选择不同辅料与工艺，实现目标释放曲线。 By selecting appropriate excipients and manufacturing technology, target release profiles can be achieved.

3
课程总结与实际意义
3
Course Summary & Practical Implications

利用合适的材料（如辅料）和技术，可以有效控制药物的释放速率和过程，以满足临床需求。 Using suitable materials (excipients) and technology, one can effectively control the rate and process of drug release to meet clinical requirements.

控释/缓释设计可优化血药浓度、降低给药频率、改善患者依从性。 Controlled/sustained release design can optimize blood concentration, reduce dosing frequency, and improve patient compliance.

老师强调每个动模背后的释药机制要理解（扩散控制、基质侵蚀、膜控等），理论曲线与实际应用紧密相关。 The instructor emphasized the need to understand the release mechanism behind each kinetic model (diffusion-, matrix erosion-, membrane-controlled, etc.), as theoretical curves are tightly linked to practical application.

补充注
老师讲到只要合理选配辅料/工艺，理论上几乎都能实现需要的释放类型。
Additional note
The teacher mentioned that with rational selection of excipients/processes, almost any desired release profile can be achieved in theory.
1
药物吸收前及肝代谢导致的药物损失

Loss of drug before absorption and by hepatic metabolism

需要对药物进行保护，防止在胃肠道中被降解或在吸收前因酶作用而丢失。 Drug protection is needed to prevent degradation in the GI tract or loss due to enzymes before absorption.

对于高价值的API（有效成分），常常需要较大的剂量才能弥补提前损失。 For high-value APIs (active ingredients), higher doses are often needed to compensate for pre-absorption losses.

但剂量越大，潜在的药物毒性风险也越大，因此必须权衡。 However, higher doses increase the risk of drug toxicity, so a balance must be reached.

2
高价值或高毒性药物必须以低剂量给药
2
High-value or highly toxic drugs must be administered at low dosage

对于特别贵重或毒性大的药物，低剂量给药尤为重要。 For especially valuable or highly toxic drugs, low dosage administration is particularly important.

但用常规生产工艺很难精准控制低剂量，容易造成过量或剂量不足。 It is difficult to precisely control low dosages with standard manufacturing processes, increasing the risk of overdose or underdose.

这类药物往往偏向于静脉注射（IV）以获得更精准的剂量和更快的起效，减少代谢路径上的损失。 Such drugs often favor intravenous (IV) administration to achieve precise dosing and rapid onset, minimizing loss via metabolic pathways.

举例
地高辛（digoxin）等治疗窗窄的药物，标准制剂难以准确控制剂量，过量危险极大。 Example: Digoxin and similar drugs with a narrow therapeutic window; standard formulations struggle with accurate dosing, and overdosing can be very dangerous.
3
补充老师口头讲解要点
3
Teacher’s additional oral explanations

老师提到药物吸收前的损失主要包括肝脏首过效应、胃肠道酶降解等。 The teacher mentioned that loss before absorption mainly includes hepatic first-pass effect and enzymatic degradation in the GI tract.

保护药物措施包括包衣（如肠溶衣）、改变给药途径（如避免口服）、及配方优化。 Drug protection strategies include coating (e.g., enteric coating), changing administration route (e.g., avoid oral), and formulation optimization.

对于高毒性药物，静脉途径可以直接进入血液循环，减少首过效应和剂量误差。 For highly toxic drugs, IV provides direct entry into systemic circulation, reducing first-pass effect and dosing inaccuracies.

但也有组内互动、讨论分组等方面的提醒，不过本页重点为药代动力学控制与给药策略。 There was also mention of group interaction and organization, but this page’s focus is on PK control and administration strategies.

4
总结
4
Summary

控制药物的药代动力学（PK）至关重要，既要减少前期损失，又要规避毒性风险。 Controlling drug pharmacokinetics (PK) is crucial—both minimizing early losses and avoiding toxicity risks.

需针对不同药物特性采用相应的保护及给药策略。 Appropriate protection and administration strategies should be tailored to the properties of the drug.

1
需要控制药物药代动力学

Hence the need to control drug pharmacokinetics

为达到更好疗效，需要对药物在体内的分布和清除速度进行调控。 To achieve better therapeutic effect, it is necessary to regulate the drug’s distribution and clearance in the body.

老师强调，药动学控制不仅影响药效，还决定药物毒性与副作用出现的范围与程度。 The teacher stressed that PK control not only impacts efficacy, but also determines the range and extent of drug toxicity and side effects.

2
控制药物药代动力学的主要方法
2
Main approaches to control drug pharmacokinetics
改变给药途径
可采用局部给药或通过非首过效应的全身给药路径，减少首过代谢，提升有效药物进入体内的比例。 Change of administration route: Use local routes or systemic routes that avoid first-pass metabolism to increase the proportion of effective drug entering the body.
控制药物分布靶向性
设法让药物更集中地到达病变组织，减少对健康组织的影响。 Control drug distribution/tissue targeting: Ensure the drug accumulates more in diseased tissues and less in healthy ones.
概念转变
药物+辅料=新实体
Paradigm shift
Drug + excipient = new entity

老师特别提出一个观念转变——药物和辅料的结合形成一个具备自身生物利用度和药代动力学的新实体（例如胶体型药物递送系统）。 The teacher highlighted a paradigm shift—combining drug and excipient creates a new entity with its own bioavailability and pharmacokinetics (e.g., colloidal drug delivery systems).

这意味着不仅仅是单纯改变药物结构，用合适的载体或辅料可以全方位调控药物体内行为。 This means that instead of only modifying the drug molecule, using the right carrier/excipient enables comprehensive control over the drug’s in vivo behavior.

4
示例（胶体DDS）
4
Example (colloidal DDS)

胶体药物递送系统（如脂质体、微乳等）能改变药物的分布、生物利用度及清除速率，实现更有效的疗效和更低的毒副作用。 Colloidal drug delivery systems (e.g., liposomes, microemulsions) can alter the distribution, bioavailability, and clearance of drugs, leading to more effective therapy and reduced side effects.

5
小结
5
Summary
控制药物药代动力学的核心在于
The core of PK control involves:

选择合适给药途径，避免首过代谢 Choosing appropriate administration routes to avoid first-pass metabolism

靶向分布药物至患病组织 Targeting drug distribution to diseased tissues

利用辅料与载体创造新型药物递送实体 Creating new drug delivery entities by combining drug and excipients

胶体药物递送系统

Colloidal drug delivery systems

胶体药物递送系统是一种利用纳米级载体（如脂质体、聚合物胶束、纳米粒等）将药物递送到靶组织或细胞的新方法。 Colloidal drug delivery systems use nanoscale carriers (such as liposomes, polymeric micelles, nanoparticles) to deliver drugs to target tissues or cells.

这种系统属于“新型生物药剂学”和“新型药代动力学”，能够显著影响药物在体内的分布、代谢和清除。 This belongs to "new biopharmaceutics" and "new pharmacokinetics", significantly affecting drug distribution, metabolism, and clearance in the body.

工作原理与优势

Mechanism and Advantages

胶体载体一般粒径为70-120纳米，可以封装小分子药物、蛋白、核酸等。 Colloidal carriers usually have a size of 70–120 nm, and can encapsulate small molecules, proteins, nucleic acids, etc.

通过表面修饰，可以负载靶向配体，使药物优先富集于病变部位，减少对健康组织的影响。 By surface functionalization with targeting ligands, the drug is enriched at the disease site, minimizing impact on healthy tissue.

胶体递送可逃避单核吞噬系统，延长血液循环时间，提高生物利用度。 Colloidal delivery allows escape from the mononuclear phagocyte system, prolongs circulation time, and improves bioavailability.

结构类型包括脂质体、聚合物胶束、固体脂质纳米粒等。如图所示，不同显微图片展示了胶体纳米载体的形态特征。 Structure types include liposomes, polymeric micelles, solid lipid nanoparticles, etc. The images show microscopic features of these colloidal nanocarriers.

代表性应用

Representative Applications

图A
胶体纳米颗粒可通过修饰实现多功能，如表面包覆、定位配体、成像基团、治疗性药物、放射性或磁性标签等。 Figure A: Colloidal nanoparticles can be engineered for multifunctionality: surface coating, targeting ligand, imaging marker, therapeutic payload, radioactive or magnetic labels, etc.
图B
纳米颗粒进入细胞，递送药物或基因，实现高效靶向治疗。 Figure B: Nanoparticles enter cells to deliver drugs/genes, enabling efficient targeted therapy.

胶体系统是纳米医学（Nanomedicine）研究的热点，推动了针对肿瘤等疾病的精准治疗。 Colloidal systems are the focus of nanomedicine research, enabling precision treatment for diseases like cancer.

课堂与补充讲解要点

Key Points from Lecture & Additional Explanation

老师强调，新型胶体递送系统可以显著优化传统药物的体内过程，针对难治性疾病或特殊组织的靶向治疗尤为有效。 The teacher emphasized that novel colloidal delivery systems can greatly optimize the fate of traditional drugs in vivo, especially effective for targeting refractory diseases or special tissues.

胶体系统结合生物兼容性、可调控性、靶向性和多功能性，是药物递送未来发展方向之一。 With biocompatibility, tunability, targeting, and multifunctionality, colloidal systems represent a promising direction for future drug delivery.

总结 Summary

胶体药物递送平台推动了新药物剂型和新药物体内行为的研究，有助于提高疗效、降低副作用。 Colloidal drug delivery platforms foster research on new drug formulations and pharmacokinetics, enhancing efficacy and reducing side effects.

纳米医学是现代药剂学和药物递送领域的重要前沿。 Nanomedicine is a cutting-edge field in pharmaceutics and drug delivery.

022025-10-17

液体中的相互作用——电双层

Liquid interactions – Electric double-layer

当颗粒表面带有净电荷时，会在其表面形成一层带相反电荷的离子。 When a particle develops a net surface charge, ions with opposite charge accumulate around its surface.

这样的离子排列会在颗粒周围形成“电双层”，包括内层的Stern层和外层的弥散层。 This arrangement forms an "electrical double layer" around the particle, consisting of an inner Stern layer and an outer diffuse layer.

Stern层中的离子与颗粒表面结合非常紧密，而弥散层中的离子结合得比较松散。 Ions in the Stern layer are strongly bound to the particle, while those in the diffuse layer are less strongly associated.

电双层结构可以用来解释带电颗粒在液体中的许多行为，如稳定性、聚集和分散。 The structure of the electric double layer explains many behaviors of charged particles in liquids, such as stability, aggregation, and dispersion.

实际上，我们可以通过测量颗粒表面的电势，来估算这个双电层的厚度和特性。 In practice, we can measure the electrostatic potential at the particle surface to estimate the double layer's thickness and properties.

图中所示，离颗粒越近，电势越高，随着距离增加电势逐步降低，并有表面电势（surface potential）、Stern电势（Stern potential）和zeta电势（zeta potential）三个关键指标。 As shown in the diagram, the closer to the particle, the higher the potential; as distance increases, the potential drops, with three key points: surface potential, Stern potential, and zeta potential.

zeta电势是颗粒与其外部溶液中带相反电荷离子的液层之间的电位差，这一指标常用于表征颗粒稳定性。 Zeta potential is the potential difference between the particle and the surrounding fluid layer containing oppositely charged ions, and is often used to characterize particle stability.

在老师讲解中还提到，无论是固体颗粒还是液滴，只要表面带电，都有电双层，并且距离足够远时，体系就可以近似看作电中性。 The teacher also emphasized that both solid and liquid particles with charged surfaces possess an electrical double layer, and at sufficient distance, the system can be regarded as approximately electrically neutral.

此外，老师特别提醒，电双层原理不仅适用于颗粒溶液，还适用于所有带电表面的液体系统。 Furthermore, the teacher stressed that the principle of the electric double layer applies not only to particle suspensions but also to any liquid system with a charged surface.

其它与电荷相关的相互作用Other interactions involving charges

偶极与极化现象 Dipoles and polarization phenomena

偶极是由于分子内部电子密度分布不对称而产生的。 A dipole results from the existence of an asymmetric distribution of electron density within a molecule.

偶极的特征可用偶极矩来描述。 The characteristic of a dipole is described by its dipole moment.

偶极矩的定义为
分离的两电荷之间的距离（l）与电荷量（q）的乘积。 The dipole moment is defined as the product of the distance (l) between two separated charges and the amount of charge (q).

公式：μ = ql Equation: μ = ql

图中显示偶极矩的方向和分子的极化情况，μ通常用箭头表示，箭头从负电荷指向正电荷。 The diagram shows the direction of dipole moments and the polarization situation in the molecule, where μ is generally indicated by an arrow pointing from the negative to the positive charge.

老师强调，偶极可以是永久性的（如某些具有极性基团的分子），也可以是在外部电场作用下被诱导产生。 The teacher emphasized that dipoles can be permanent (as in some molecules with polar groups) or can be induced by an external electric field.

极化现象指的是在外部电场或相邻分子的作用下，一个原本非极性的分子也可瞬时具有偶极性。 Polarization refers to the phenomenon where a non-polar molecule acquires instantaneous dipole character due to an external electric field or neighboring molecules.

这些现象在液体体系、自组装膜、胶体等结构中非常重要，影响分子的相互作用和宏观物理化学性质。 These phenomena are very important in liquid systems, self-assembling films, and colloids, influencing molecular interactions and macroscopic physicochemical properties

总结：

偶极和极化现象源于分子内或分子间电子分布的不均匀，是描述分子间物理相互作用的基本概念。

Summary:

Dipole and polarization phenomena arise from non-uniform electron distributions within or between molecules, forming a fundamental concept for describing physical interactions between molecules

带电相互作用的其它表现——偶极与极化现象

Other interactions involving charges—Dipoles and polarization phenomena

偶极现象指分子内部因电子密度分布不对称而在分子内形成正负两极。 A dipole phenomenon refers to the formation of positive and negative poles within a molecule due to an asymmetric electron density distribution.

偶极可以是永久的，也可以是外加电场作用下诱导产生的。 Dipoles can be permanent or induced by the application of an external electric field.

课上老师强调，当有极性基团或分子被外电场影响时，分子表现出偶极特性，并且这对药物体系中的相互作用极其重要。 During class, the teacher emphasized that when polar groups are present or when molecules are influenced by an external field, they exhibit dipole characteristics, and this is extremely important for interactions in pharmaceutical systems.

课件举例，磷脂分子属于两性离子（zwitterion），它们既有正电荷也有负电荷。 As shown in the slide, phospholipid molecules are zwitterions, possessing both positive and negative charges.

两性离子是很好的偶极例子，因为它们分子整体电中性，但在空间结构中存在分布明显的正负电荷。 Zwitterions are good dipole examples because they are electrically neutral as a whole, but their spatial structure has distinct regions of positive and negative charge.

旁边的甘氨酸分子随pH变化有不同电荷状态：在等电点（pHi = 6）时整体为中性，两侧pKa值分别为2.3和9.6。 The glycine example shows that at different pH, it has varying charge states: at the isoelectric point (pHi = 6), it is overall neutral, and has pKa values of 2.3 and 9.6.

老师补充，只有在pH等于等电点时分子才整体为电中性，偏离等电点则会分别呈现带正电或带负电状态，影响其与环境的库仑（电荷）相互作用。 The teacher added that the molecule is only electrically neutral at the isoelectric point; at pH values above or below this, it will be positively or negatively charged, affecting its Coulombic (charge) interactions with the environment.

总结
偶极和极化现象的存在，使得分子间能形成多种特殊物理相互作用，这对于蛋白、磷脂、药物分子的聚集、膜形成及溶液稳定性等起核心作用。 In summary: The existence of dipole and polarization phenomena enables molecules to form various special physical interactions, which are central to aggregation, membrane formation, and solution stability in proteins, phospholipids, and pharmaceutical molecules.

偶极与极化现象Dipoles and polarization phenomena

界面上的相互作用Interactions at the interfaces

两性离子在溶液中的等电点
Zwitterion in solution at the isoelectric point:

分子整体为电中性。 The molecule is electrically neutral.

但两性离子内部相反的电荷产生了偶极矩，这种偶极会决定分子的作用方式。 But the opposite charges within the zwitterion create a dipole, which can govern the nature of the interactions the molecule undergoes.

当环境pH高于或低于等电点（pHi）时，该分子就会带整体电荷，表现出库仑（静电）作用。 At pH above or below the isoelectric point (pHi), the molecule becomes formally charged and undergoes Coulombic interactions.

老师补充指出，等电点是所有两性分子分子内正负电荷平衡时的特殊pH值，此时分子对外电中性，但因内部分布可形成显著偶极。 The teacher further explained that the isoelectric point is a special pH at which all internal positive and negative charges of the zwitterion balance out, leading to electrical neutrality externally, but a notable dipole exists due to internal charge distribution.

磷脂分子的界面相互作用
Interactions of phospholipid molecules at interfaces:

磷脂属于两性离子，分子一端带正电，另一端带负电，在界面上排列时形成分明的带电区域。 Phospholipids are zwitterions, with one end positively charged and the other negatively charged, creating distinct charge regions when aligned at the interface.

图示1区为界面扰动带
界面附近的水分子排列受到磷脂极性头部影响，形成有序或扰动层。 Zone 1 in the diagram (layer of water disturbed by the interface): Water molecules near the interface are arranged under the influence of the phospholipid polar head, forming an ordered or disturbed layer.

2区为界面本身，带有极性基团，是电荷分布的关键区域。 Zone 2 is the interface itself, containing polar groups and representing the crucial area for charge distribution.

3区为疏水链密集带，自由体积很小，有利于双层结构的紧密性和稳定性。 Zone 3 is characterized by a high density of hydrophobic chains and very low free volume, contributing to the compactness and stability of the bilayer structure.

4区为双层中心，疏水性极强，水分子无法进入。 Zone 4 is the bilayer center, which is highly hydrophobic and inaccessible to water molecules.

老师特别提到，界面现象对于药物递送、脂质体制备、细胞膜模拟等都有重要意义，双层结构的极性分布决定了器件的吸附、包埋以及靶向特性。 The teacher particularly mentioned that interfacial phenomena are crucial in drug delivery, liposome formulation, and cell membrane simulation; the polarity distribution within the bilayer determines adsorption, encapsulation, and targeting characteristics of such systems.

小结：

两性离子的偶极性质及其在界面形成的极性分布，是理解生物膜、药物递送体系等复杂液体结构的基础。

Summary:

The dipole properties of zwitterions and their polarity distribution at interfaces are fundamental for understanding biological membranes, drug delivery systems, and other complex liquid structures.

分子水合与界面有序Molecular Hydration and Interfacial Organization

两性离子的水合与界面有序 Zwitterion hydration and interfacial organization

不同类型的磷脂分子由于头部基团结构不同，表现出不同的水合能力和分子间有序排列。 Different types of phospholipid molecules show varying hydration abilities and intermolecular ordering at interfaces due to differences in their head group structures.

左图PC（磷脂酰胆碱）分子，其极性头部能与25-30个水分子形成水合层。 In the left panel, PC (phosphatidylcholine) has a polar head capable of binding to 25-30 water molecules in its hydration shell.

右图PE（磷脂酰乙醇胺）分子的头部仅能与10-12个水分子结合。 In the right panel, PE (phosphatidylethanolamine) head group can bind only 10-12 water molecules.

PC的分子头部更大，空间占据更宽，因此水分子可以以更多方式进行有序排列。 The head group of PC is larger and more spacious, allowing water molecules to arrange themselves in a more orderly and extensive fashion.

PE分子因为头部更紧凑，水合水分子的数目显著减少，界面出现更加密集和局限的水合结构。 For PE, the head group is more compact, resulting in fewer hydrated water molecules and denser, more localized hydration at the interface.

老师在讲解中指出，水合水分子的数量和排列对磷脂双层的物理性质有重大影响，如膜的流动性、厚度和屏障功能。 The teacher pointed out that the quantity and organization of hydration water molecules significantly influence the physical properties of phospholipid bilayers, such as membrane fluidity, thickness, and barrier functions.

此外，水合能力不同还会影响药物分子或蛋白在膜界面上的吸附和结合行为。 Additionally, differences in hydration affect the adsorption and binding behavior of drug molecules or proteins at membrane interfaces.

小结：

磷脂分子头部化学结构决定其水合能力，水合结构直接影响生物膜的界面性质和功能。

Summary:

The chemical structure of phospholipid head groups determines their hydration capacity, and this hydration pattern directly affects the interfacial properties and functions of biological membranes.

分散作用Dispersive interactions

范德华力（分散作用）是物质中普遍存在的一类分子间作用力，属于弱物理作用。 Van der Waals forces (dispersive interactions) are a type of intermolecular interaction universally present in matter and are considered weak physical forces.

范德华力具有较长的作用距离，可达10纳米甚至更远。 Van der Waals forces are long-range interactions, extending to 10 nm or more.

其相互作用能量范围一般为1到4 kJ/mol。 The interaction energy typically ranges from 1 to 4 kJ/mol.

但范德华力会随着距离的增加迅速衰减。 However, Van der Waals forces decrease rapidly with increasing distance.

课上老师特别强调，两分子之间的范德华相互作用可能会被周围其他分子的存在所影响，因此实际体系中相互作用会更加复杂。 The teacher emphasized that the interaction between two molecules can be affected by the presence of surrounding molecules, making the actual interactions in a system more complex.

范德华力包括诱导偶极、瞬时偶极和取向偶极等多种类型，这些因素共同决定了液体和生物体系分子的相互作用特性。 Van der Waals forces include induced dipole, instantaneous dipole, and orientation dipole types, jointly determining the interaction characteristics of molecules in liquids and biological systems.

总结
分散作用在溶液、药物分子和生物膜系统中广泛存在，虽能量较弱，但对分子组织结构和稳定性具有重要影响。 Summary: Dispersive interactions are widespread in solutions, drug molecules, and biomembrane systems; although individually weak, they play an important role in molecular organization and stability.

偶极相互作用Dipole interactions

偶极-偶极相互作用（Keesom） Dipole-dipole interactions (Keesom)

两个具有永久偶极的分子之间存在作用力，这类分子具有部分永久电荷和偶极矩μ。 Forces exist between two molecules with permanent dipoles, which possess partial permanent charges and a dipole moment μ.

偶极-偶极作用通常见于极性分子（如HCl、H2O等），相互排列取向对作用强度有影响。 Dipole-dipole interactions typically occur between polar molecules (such as HCl, H2O), and the relative orientation affects interaction strength.

课上老师补充道，偶极-偶极相互作用能量与两分子间距离r的六次方成反比，即E∝1r6E∝r61​。 The teacher added that the energy of dipole-dipole interactions is inversely proportional to the sixth power of the distance between two molecules, E∝1r6E∝r61​.

永久偶极-诱导偶极相互作用（Debye） Permanent dipole-induced dipole interactions (Debye)

永久偶极分子可通过电场影响，诱导附近非极性分子的瞬时偶极，从而产生吸引力。 A permanent dipole molecule can induce a temporary dipole in a neighboring nonpolar molecule via its electric field, leading to an attractive force.

例如水分子的极性可以诱导碘分子（I2）产生偶极。 For example, the polar nature of a water molecule can induce a dipole in an iodine (I2) molecule.

形成机制为永久偶极极化临近分子的电子云，表现为诱导偶极。 The mechanism involves the permanent dipole polarizing the electron cloud of a nearby molecule, resulting in an induced dipole.

老师强调Debye型作用也是距离的六次方反比递减E∝1r6E∝r61​，但其大小还取决于分子的极化率。 The teacher emphasized that Debye-type interactions also decrease with the sixth power of distance E∝1r6E∝r61​, and the magnitude depends on the polarizability of the molecule.

瞬时偶极-诱导偶极相互作用
伦敦力（London-van der Waals） Instantaneous dipole-induced dipole interactions: London forces (London-van der Waals)

所有分子（包括非极性分子）之间都存在伦敦色散力，这是由分子电子云瞬时不均匀分布导致的瞬时偶极产生的。 London dispersion forces exist between all molecules (including nonpolar ones), resulting from transient inhomogeneity in electron distribution and the creation of instantaneous dipoles.

任何时刻的电子云分布不均都可形成瞬时偶极，并进而在邻近分子中诱导偶极，产生吸引作用。 Any instant uneven electronic distribution can form an instantaneous dipole, which induces a dipole in a neighboring molecule, resulting in attraction.

伦敦力是吸引性（粘合性）的，其强度随分子距离减小而显著增强。 London forces are attractive (cohesive); their intensity increases rapidly as molecules get closer.

课上老师补充，色散力随分子（或粒子）体积变大而增强，在颗粒性物质中效果更明显。 The teacher added that dispersion forces increase with the volume of the molecule (or particle), and the effect is more pronounced in large particles.

范德华力吸引能的表达式如下
The attractive energy of van der Waals forces is expressed as:

μi​ 表示分子的永久偶极矩（如有电荷） μi​ represents the permanent dipole moments of the molecules (if charged).

αi​ 表示分子的极化率（用于描述诱导偶极的能力） αi​ represents the polarizability of the interacting and polarizable molecules (induction).

νi​ 表示分子的电离度或相关频率 νi​ represents the degrees of ionization (or relevant vibration frequency).

小结

偶极相互作用分为永久偶极-偶极、永久偶极-诱导偶极和瞬时偶极-诱导偶极三类，能量均随距离增加呈六次方衰减。这些作用对液体与生物体系分子的吸引、组织结构和功能有重要影响。

Summary

Dipole interactions comprise permanent dipole-dipole, permanent dipole-induced dipole, and instantaneous dipole-induced dipole types; all energies decay with the sixth power of distance. These interactions are crucial for molecular attraction, structural organization, and function in liquids and biological systems.

分子间相互作用能Intermolecular interaction energy

分子间相互作用能是所有相互作用能的总和。 Intermolecular interaction energy results from the sum of all interaction energies.

常用Lennard-Jones势能模型来描述分子间作用的总能量。 The Lennard-Jones potential model is commonly used to describe the total energy between molecules.

总自由能变化ΔGΔG包括排斥作用（Born力）和吸引作用（van der Waals力）两部分。 The total free energy change ΔGΔG consists of a repulsive component (Born force) and an attractive component (van der Waals force).

随着距离减小，排斥力迅速增强，防止分子过度接近；而吸引力则在较远距离起主导作用。 As the distance decreases, the repulsive force rises sharply, preventing excessive approach, while the attractive force dominates at longer distances.

Lennard-Jones曲线表现为在特定距离存在能量极小点，对应分子间稳定的平衡距离。 The Lennard-Jones curve shows an energy minimum at a specific distance, corresponding to a stable equilibrium distance between molecules.

课上老师强调，排斥项与距离的十二次方成反比，是源自分子间电子云层重叠的排斥作用；吸引项（1/d6）描述了色散（范德华）力。 The teacher emphasized that the repulsive term is inversely proportional to the twelfth power of the distance, arising from repulsion between overlapping electron clouds, while the attractive term (1/d6) describes dispersion (van der Waals) forces.

最终的“resulting intermolecular potential”在两项共同作用下，决定了分子间的实际相互作用特性。 The resulting intermolecular potential, as a balance of these two terms, determines the actual interaction characteristics between molecules.

水介质中的特殊相互作用Specific interactions in aqueous media

1
氢键、疏水和亲水相互作用的类型 The types of interactions: hydrogen bonds, hydrophobic interactions, and hydrophilic interactions

氢键、疏水相互作用和亲水相互作用是水介质中特有的重要分子相互作用。 Hydrogen bonds, hydrophobic interactions, and hydrophilic interactions are important and specific molecular interactions in aqueous media.

水分子之间、及与其他溶质之间的相互作用，影响着溶液的结构与性质。 The interactions between water molecules, and between water and other solutes, affect the structure and properties of solutions.

2
氢键在水中特殊性及其物理数据 The special properties of hydrogen bonds in water and their physical data

水具有特殊的氢键网络结构，不仅冰熔化后仍可保持较强分子间氢键。 Water has a special hydrogen bond network structure and can preserve strong intermolecular hydrogen bonds even after melting of ice.

这些氢键的形成依赖于水分子的取向。 The formation of these bonds depends on the orientation of water molecules.

共价O-H键长为0.1纳米；分子间O-H作用距离为0.176纳米，远小于理论空间距离（0.26纳米），表明氢键强烈收缩水分子间距。 The covalent O-H bond distance is 0.1 nm; the intermolecular O-H interaction distance is 0.176 nm, much less than the expected 0.26 nm, indicating hydrogen bonds strongly shorten the spacing between water molecules.

3
氢键强度与分子装配作用 Strength of hydrogen bonds and their role in molecular assembly

氢键键能为10-40千焦/摩尔，强于范德华力（1千焦/摩尔），显著弱于共价键或离子键（约500千焦/摩尔）。 The bond energy of hydrogen bonds is 10-40 kJ/mol, stronger than van der Waals forces (1 kJ/mol), but much weaker than covalent or ionic bonds (about 500 kJ/mol).

氢键对分子及大分子组装有决定性作用，包括分子精细结构、多肽、核酸、胶束及生物膜等的稳定与构象。 Hydrogen bonds play a decisive role in certain molecular and macromolecular assemblies, such as the fine structure of molecules, polypeptides, polynucleotides, micelles, and biological membranes.

4
纯水与水分子团聚 Pure water and water molecular clusters

纯水中每个水分子平均参与3~3.5个氢键，在20°C时常以6个聚集成团。 Each water molecule in pure water forms on average 3 to 3.5 hydrogen bonds, and at 20°C, the water molecules assemble in groups of 6.

5
疏水效应 Hydrophobic effect

当水中加入非极性分子时，水分子需重组、打破原有氢键并重新生成新的氢键。 When a nonpolar molecule is added to water, the water molecules reorganize, breaking some original hydrogen bonds and forming new ones.

在包围疏水分子时，每个水分子可形成4个氢键，水分子排列更有序，体系熵降低，热力学上不利。 In surrounding the hydrophobic molecule, each water molecule forms 4 hydrogen bonds and aligns more orderly, leading to decreased entropy, which is energetically unfavorable.

疏水效应在表面现象、分子自组装和蛋白质构象等领域起关键作用。 The hydrophobic effect is crucial in surface phenomena, molecular self-assembly, and protein conformation.

6
亲水相互作用 Hydrophilic interaction

水与亲水分子作用时，会竞争氢键形成，导致水分子结构紊乱，严重时促使物质溶解。 When water interacts with hydrophilic molecules, there is competition for hydrogen bond formation, causing disorganization of the water structure and, when severe, leading to dissolution.

对其他溶质也有重要影响，如尿素可通过影响水结构导致蛋白质变性。 It also affects other dissolved molecules, for example, urea denatures proteins by disrupting water structure.

【老师补充讲解】

【Supplementary points from the teacher】

氢键是水的独特性质来源，决定溶液行为如蛋白质变性和自组装。 Hydrogen bonding is the source of water's unique properties, determining solution behaviors such as protein denaturation and self-assembly.

在水与溶质相互作用过程中，主导因素是氢键及其对水分子取向/排列的影响。 During water-solute interactions, the dominant factor is hydrogen bonding and its effect on water molecule orientation and arrangement.

疏水和亲水效应决定溶质在水中的溶解性和分子间作用。 Hydrophobic and hydrophilic effects determine solubility of solutes and intermolecular interactions in water.

用尿素变性等例子说明水结构的变化会显著影响生物大分子的稳定性和功能。 Examples like urea-induced denaturation show that changes in water structure significantly affect macromolecular stability and function.

药用液体剂型Liquids in pharmacy

1
活性成分与辅料的定义 Definition of Active Ingredient and Excipient

活性成分指药物中的主要有效物质，例如：甲基水杨酸酯、精油。 The active ingredient refers to the main effective substance in the drug, e.g., methyl salicylate, essential oils.

辅料是用于配制和稳定制剂的非活性添加剂，例如：水、乙醇、甘油、油类。 An excipient is a non-active additive used to formulate and stabilize preparations, e.g., water, ethanol, glycerol, oils.

2
不同药用液体剂型与举例 Types of Liquid Pharmaceutical Forms and Examples

药品可以制成多种液体制剂，主要包括以下类别： Medicines can be made into various liquid forms, mainly including the following categories:

口服液体制剂
如糖浆、药酒、溶液、口服溶液安瓿。 Oral liquid forms: e.g., syrups, potions, solutions, ampoules of drinkable solutions.
注射用液体制剂
如溶液、乳剂、分散液等。 Liquid forms for the parenteral route: e.g., solutions, emulsions, dispersions, etc.
吸入用液体制剂
如雾化吸入用液体或分散液。 Liquid forms for the airborne route: e.g., liquid preparations for inhalation, dispersed liquids by nebulizers.
眼用液体制剂
如滴眼液。 Liquid forms for the ophthalmic route: e.g., eye drops.
经皮用液体制剂
如软膏、乳膏、凝胶、膏剂。 Liquid forms for the percutaneous route: e.g., ointments, creams, gels, pastes.
3
药物剂型的分布比例 Distribution of Dosage Forms

临床和药房中常见的药物剂型有如下分布频率（%）： The common frequency (%) distribution of pharmaceutical dosage forms in clinics and pharmacies is as follows:

片剂
46% Tablets: 46%
胶囊剂
13% Capsules: 13%
口服液体制剂
16% Oral liquid forms: 16%
注射用剂型
15% Parenteral forms: 15%
外用制剂
3% Topical forms: 3%
眼用制剂
2% Ophthalmic forms: 2%
栓剂
3% Suppositories: 3%
其他类别
2% Other: 2%
4
溶液的定义与机理 Definition and Mechanism of Solutions

溶液定义为溶质在分子层面分散在溶剂中的体系。 A solution is defined as a system where the solute is dispersed at the molecular scale in the solvent.

离子晶体溶解后形成离子分散在溶液中的电解质溶液。 An ionic crystal, upon dissolution, forms ions dispersed in the solvent, which is called an electrolyte solution.

非离子分子溶解后形成非电解质溶液。 A non-ionic molecule, upon dissolution, forms a non-electrolyte solution.

溶质和溶剂之间的结合称为溶剂化过程。 The bonding between solute and solvent is called solvation.

溶解过程实质上是溶质在溶液中分子层面的分散，其特点包括： Dissolution is essentially the dispersion of the solute at a molecular level in the solvent, with the following features:

无化学反应发生 Absence of chemical reaction

可逆性 Reversibility

溶液的均一性 Homogeneity of the solution

5
补充讲解要点 Key Supplementary Points from the Teacher

老师强调，液体剂型不仅满足不同临床需求，也是药物吸收和配伍的重要考虑。 The teacher emphasized that liquid forms not only meet different clinical needs but are also important for drug absorption and formulation.

在设计液体药品时，优选溶解状态，因为分子分散有利于药物穿越生物屏障。 When designing a liquid pharmaceutical, the dissolved state is preferred, as molecular dispersion aids drug passage through biological barriers.

注重溶液的均匀性、透明度和无可见杂质。 Emphasis is placed on the homogeneity, clarity, and absence of visible impurities in solutions.

溶解度Solubility

1
基本概念与实验步骤 Basic concepts and experimental steps
