在恒定温度下，向溶剂1中逐步加入A，直到达到A的饱和溶液，剩余A为过量。 At constant temperature, increasing quantities of A are added to solvent 1 until a saturated solution of A is reached; the remainder is excess A.

若A为固体，可为纯A或A的溶合物；最终形成A的饱和溶液并有固体A剩余。 If A is solid, it can be pure A or in the form of solvates; finally, a saturated solution of A is formed with excess solid A left.

若A为液体，将有两种饱和状态：溶剂1被A饱和，以及A中溶有饱和的溶剂1。 If A is liquid, there are two saturated states: solvent 1 saturated with A, and liquid A saturated with solvent 1.

2
溶解度的定义及特点 Definition and features of solubility
溶解度定义为
在特定温度下，某一化学物质能溶解在一定体积溶剂中的最大量，即饱和浓度(Cs)。 Solubility is defined as the maximum amount of a given chemical species that can be dissolved in a given volume of solvent: saturation concentration (Cs), at a given temperature.

溶解度不受时间、搅拌或固体表面积影响。 Solubility does not depend on time, agitation, or the surface of the solid.

常用单位为每升克数（g/L）或每升摩尔数（mol/L）。 The usual units are grams per litre or moles per litre.

3
溶解度的分级 Solubility classification
高度溶解
每份物质用不满1份溶剂即可溶解。 Highly soluble: less than a part of solvent per part of solute.
易溶
1~10份溶剂 Easily soluble: 1 to 10 parts of solvent.
可溶
10~30份溶剂 Soluble: 10 to 30 parts of solvent.
较易溶
30~100份溶剂 Fairly soluble: 30 to 100 parts of solvent.
难溶
100~1000份溶剂 Poorly soluble: 100 to 1000 parts of solvent.
极难溶
1000~10000份溶剂 Very poorly soluble: 1000 to 10000 parts of solvent.
几乎不溶
大于10000份溶剂 Virtually insoluble: more than 10000 parts of solvent.
4
溶解度的实验测定方法 Experimental determination of solubility
常用定量方法
先加入过量溶质(X > Cs)，搅拌至平衡，过滤后测定随时间变化的浓度f(t)。 Quantitative method: Add excess solute (X > Cs), agitate to equilibrium, filter, then measure the concentration f(t) as a function of time.

浓度随时间上升后趋于Cs，绘制溶解度达平衡的曲线。 Concentration increases with time and plateaus at Cs; the resulting curve illustrates reaching solubility equilibrium.

【老师补充讲解要点】

【Supplementary points from the teacher】

溶解度是药物剂型设计的核心参数，决定活性成分能否高效释放。 Solubility is a key parameter in drug formulation, determining whether the active ingredient can be efficiently released.

一定温度下的溶解度为常数，实验需保证恒温且搅拌充分达到真实饱和。 At a given temperature, solubility is constant; experiments must ensure constant temperature and sufficient agitation to reach true saturation.

临床和制剂中常用分级可指导制剂方法的选择。 The classification of solubility guides formulation strategies in clinics and preparation.

溶解度与pKa Solubility and pKa

1
pKa的定义与意义 Definition and significance of pKa

pKa是指弱酸或弱碱电离度达到50%时的pH值。 The pKa is the pH at which a weak acid or weak base will be ionized to 50%.

这是判断分子在不同pH环境中电离状态的关键参数。 This is a key parameter for determining the ionization state of a molecule in different pH environments.

2
酸的电离规律 Ionization behavior of acids

弱酸（如AH）在pH高于其pKa时主要以电离态(A^-)存在。 Acids are ionized above their pKa, meaning when pH > pKa, the acid mainly exists in the ionized form (A^-).

酸的pH与电离度关系如图所示
pH等于pKa时，电离度为50%；pH高于pKa越多，电离程度越高。 The relationship between pH and ionization for acids is shown in the graph: at pH = pKa, 50% is ionized; higher pH increases ionization.
3
碱的电离规律 Ionization behavior of bases

弱碱（如BH^+）在pH低于其pKa时主要以电离态(BH^+)存在。 Bases are ionized below their pKa, meaning when pH < pKa, the base mainly exists in the ionized form (BH^+).

碱的pH与电离度关系如图所示
pH等于pKa时，电离度为50%；pH越低于pKa，电离程度越高。 The relationship between pH and ionization for bases is shown: at pH = pKa, 50% is ionized; lower pH increases ionization.
4
电离与溶解度的联系 Relationship between ionization and solubility

在药物研发中，溶解度与药物的电离状态密切相关。 In drug development, solubility is closely related to the ionization state of the drug.

各种API的pKa值决定了其在不同pH下的电离比例，这直接影响其在水等溶剂中的溶解度。 The pKa value of various APIs determines their proportion of ionized forms at different pH, directly affecting their solubility in water or other solvents.

5
图像解读与知识点总结 Interpretation of graphs and main points

两个图分别展示了弱酸和弱碱的未电离百分比与pH的关系曲线。 The two graphs show the percentage of unionized form versus pH for a weak acid and a weak base, respectively.

对于酸，当pH = pKa时，有50%为未电离形式；当pH增加1单位（pKa+1），未电离比例降至约10%。 For acids, at pH = pKa, 50% is unionized; at pKa + 1, unionized form drops to about 10%.

对于碱，当pH = pKa时，有50%为未电离形式；当pH降低1单位（pKa-1），未电离比例降至约10%。 For bases, at pH = pKa, 50% is unionized; at pKa - 1, unionized form drops to about 10%.

这种电离变化规律对药物的吸收和体内分布具有决定性影响。 These ionization rules have a decisive influence on drug absorption and distribution in the body.

6
课上补充与举例 Additional points and examples from lecture

老师强调，在实际药物开发和配方设计时，必须比较API的pKa与目标环境（如胃液、血浆等）的pH，判断药物的电离比例。 The teacher emphasized that in real drug development and formulation, one must compare the API's pKa to the target environment's pH (e.g., gastric juice, plasma) to determine ionization proportion.

酸类API在胃内（低pH）以非电离态为主，有利于吸收；碱类API在小肠（中性略碱pH）以非电离态为主，也有利于吸收。 Acidic APIs are mainly non-ionized in the stomach (low pH), favoring absorption; basic APIs are mainly non-ionized in the small intestine (neutral to slightly alkaline pH), also favoring absorption.

确认溶解度测定时需参考药物的pKa和测试介质的pH，否则结果无法反映实际生理环境下的溶解情况。 When measuring solubility, the drug’s pKa and the pH of the testing medium must be considered, otherwise results may not reflect real physiological conditions.>

溶解度与pKa、表观溶解度 Solubility, pKa and Apparent Solubility

1
溶解度的影响因素 Solubility influencing factors

溶解度受温度和pH强烈影响，尤其是对于酸性和碱性API。 Solubility is strongly affected by temperature and pH, especially for acidic and basic APIs.

酸碱性药物的电离比例随着pH变化而改变。 The proportion of ionized and unionized forms of acidic and basic drugs varies with pH.

2
电离型与未电离型浓度关系式 Relationship between ionized and unionized concentration

在特定pH条件下，总溶解度为电离型和未电离型的浓度之和： At a certain pH, total solubility equals the sum of ionized and unionized concentrations:

Cs = Cs（电离型）+ Cs（未电离型） Cs = Cs (ionized form) + Cs (unionized form)

3
酸碱药物在不同pH下电离比例 Ionization percentage of acids and bases at different pH

酸性药物在pKa-3、-2、-1、pKa、pKa+1、+2、+3时，未电离型分别为99.9%、99%、90%、50%、10%、1%、0.1%。 For acidic drugs, the unionized state is 99.9%, 99%, 90%, 50%, 10%, 1%, and 0.1% at pKa-3, -2, -1, pKa, +1, +2, +3 respectively.

碱性药物在相同pH点的未电离型分别为0.1%、1%、10%、50%、90%、99%、99.9%。 For basic drugs, the unionized state is 0.1%, 1%, 10%, 50%, 90%, 99%, and 99.9% at these pH points.

这种规律决定药物在不同pH环境中的溶解度和主要存在形式。 This pattern determines drug solubility and dominant molecular state at different pH values.

4
体液pH及常见药物pKa举例 Body fluid pH values and typical API pKa examples
常见酸性体液pH
胃液、尿液、汗液为1.56.8，血液、脑脊液、胆汁为7.08.5。 Common acidic body fluids: gastric juice, urine, sweat (pH 1.5–6.8); blood, cerebrospinal fluid, bile (pH 7.0–8.5).
常见弱酸API的pKa有
左旋多巴2.3，阿莫西林2.4，阿司匹林3.5，头孢氨苄3.6，呋塞米3.9，华法林5.0，乙酰唑胺7.2，苯妥英8.4，茶碱8.8。 Common acidic APIs and their pKa: Levodopa 2.3, Amoxycillin 2.4, Aspirin 3.5, Cephalexin 3.6, Frusemide 3.9, Warfarin 5.0, Acetazolamide 7.2, Phenytoin 8.4, Theophylline 8.8.
常见弱碱API的pKa有
地西泮3.0，利多卡因7.9，可待因8.2，可卡因8.5，肾上腺素8.7，阿托品9.7，苯丙胺9.8，美托洛尔9.8，甲基多巴10.6。 Common basic APIs and their pKa: Diazepam 3.0, Lignocaine 7.9, Codeine 8.2, Cocaine 8.5, Adrenaline 8.7, Atropine 9.7, Amphetamine 9.8, Metoprolol 9.8, Methyldopa 10.6.
5
溶解度与pH的临床相关举例 Clinical examples of solubility and pH

在胃（pH约1.5），酸性药物主要为未电离型，有利于胃吸收。 In the stomach (pH about 1.5), acidic drugs are mainly unionized, favoring gastric absorption.

在小肠和血液等碱性环境，碱性药物未电离比例变高，更有利于吸收。 In the intestine and blood (alkaline), basic drugs have more unionized form, which better supports absorption.

6
表观溶解度的影响因素 Influencing factors of apparent solubility

API的物理状态（如晶型、颗粒粒径等）会改变表观溶解度。 The physical state of API (like polymorphism, particle size) changes apparent solubility.

例如苯乙氨基酚不同粒径，其溶解度曲线差异明显，粒径越小，溶解越快，最终释放量更大。 For example, phenacetin with smaller particles dissolves faster with higher final release; different particle sizes give different dissolution curves.

体系中若有表面活性剂、环糊精等辅料，也能明显提高API的溶解度和稳定性。 Presence of surfactants, cyclodextrins or other excipients can significantly increase API solubility and stability.

7
课上重点补充和总结 Key supplementary points from class

溶解度与药物在各pH下的离子型比例相关，需结合API pKa和体液pH具体分析。 Solubility is tied to the drug’s ionization ratio at various pH; one must analyze it with API pKa and the specific body fluid pH.

老师强调
测定药物溶解度和开发液体制剂时，要同时关注生理pH和API的所有剂型、表观溶解度影响因素。 Teacher emphasized: When measuring drug solubility or developing liquid formulations, consider both physiological pH and all factors affecting apparent solubility of the API.

药物的溶解与溶解度 Dissolution and Solubility of Drugs

1
固体制剂的溶解特点 Dissolution of solid forms

固体制剂的药物，溶解发生在吸收步骤前，通常在体内给药后进行。 For solid drug forms, dissolution occurs before the absorption step, most often in vivo after administration.

上课补充，固体药物进入体内被体液润湿，经过溶解后才能以分子状态通过生理屏障被吸收。 The teacher added that after administration, solids are wetted by body fluids, dissolution proceeds, and only then can drugs cross physiological barriers in a molecular state.

2
液体制剂的溶解特点 Dissolution of liquid forms

液体制剂在药物配制过程中已完成溶解。 For liquid forms, dissolution has already taken place during the preparation of the pharmaceutical form.

上课补充，液体制剂中API已是均匀溶解状态，制备时确保均一与澄清。 The teacher emphasized that the API in liquid forms is already homogeneously dissolved, and uniformity/clarity is ensured during preparation.

3
溶解度在制剂前研究及给药途径选择中的作用 Importance of solubility in preformulation and route choice

溶解度是处方前研究的关键参数，决定给药途径和剂型的选择。 Solubility is a critical preformulation parameter, conditioning the choice of the administration route and pharmaceutical form.

口服固体剂型关注胃肠道(pH 1-8)的溶解度。 Oral solid forms focus on dissolution in gastrointestinal tract (GIT) fluids at pH 1-8.

注射液等液体制剂关注所有pH及不同溶剂体系（如水、醇、油、PEG等）的溶解度。 Liquid/parenteral forms must consider solubility across all pH and multiple vehicles (e.g., water, alcohols, oils, PEG).
4
溶解度不足引发的实际制剂难题 Problems arising from poor solubility

大约75%在研分子和40%上市药物为低溶解度，严重影响注射剂、口服液、滴眼剂等剂型的开发。 About 75% of molecules in development and 40% of marketed drugs have low solubility, posing challenges for injectables, liquid orals, and eye drops.

溶解度低于所需剂量对应浓度时，必须提升溶解度。 If solubility is below the needed concentration for dosing, it must be improved.

选用的提升策略需结合API的化学性质和目标剂型。 The chosen strategy must consider the API's chemical character and the desired dosage form.

5
提高低溶解度药物溶解度的策略 Strategies to enhance solubility of poorly soluble APIs
API物理改性
非晶或晶型选择（水溶解度、稳定性），水合/无水型选择，粒径减小（微米化/纳米化）。 Physical modification of API: amorphous/crystalline form (solubility and stability), anhydrous/hydrate form, particle size reduction (micronization, nanonization).
API化学改性
制盐、前药设计。 Chemical modification: salt formation, prodrug design.

pH调整来优化溶解度。 pH adjustment to improve solubility.

处方策略
Formulation strategies: a. 使用水混溶性溶剂提升溶解度。 Use of water-miscible solvents to increase solubility. b. 使用水不混溶溶剂（如脂类）提升溶解度。 Use of water-immiscible (e.g., lipid) solvents. c. 添加表面活性剂改善溶解度。 Use of surfactants to improve solubility. d. 环糊精包合提升溶解度和/或溶出速率。 Cyclodextrin complexation for improving solubility/dissolution. e. 制备固体分散体或脂质体包埋API。 Solid dispersion or liposome encapsulation for enhanced solubility.

多种提升溶解度方法可组合使用以获得最佳效果。 Multiple methods may be combined for maximum effect.

溶解度与溶解策略Solubility and Strategy of Dissolution

1
API适用对象总结 Summary of API Candidates

存在于结晶状态的API。 API exists in crystalline form.

溶解度小于10 mg/mL。 Solubility is less than 10 mg/mL.

生物利用度低，或者易受患者进食/空腹状态影响。 Low bioavailability or significantly influenced by the patient’s fed/fasted state.

溶出速率受限。 Limiting dissolution rate.

老师强调，这些分子需要专门溶解度提升策略，否则难以达到体内有效浓度。 The teacher emphasized that such molecules require dedicated solubility enhancement strategies, otherwise they may not reach effective in vivo concentrations.

2
溶出速率对体内药物浓度的影响 Effect of dissolution rate on drug plasma concentration

Nanocrystal制剂相比传统分散可显著提高体内药物峰浓度与暴露。 Nanocrystal formulations significantly increase peak and total plasma drug levels compared to conventional dispersions.

曲线显示相同API，纳米晶体制剂能更快、更高效地释放药物。 The graph shows that for the same API, nanocrystal preparations release the drug faster and more efficiently.

3
药物溶解策略决策流程 Chart for strategy of dissolution

首先判断API是否水溶。 First, check if the API is water-soluble.

若水溶，则直接开发即可。 If yes, direct development is possible.

若不水溶，判断能否制备成盐。 If not, assess whether salt formation is feasible.

可制备盐，采用pH调整或盐制备提升溶解度。 If salt formable, use pH adjustment or salt formation to increase solubility.

不可制备盐，则选择其他策略。 If not, move to other strategies.

4
配伍策略选择的递进原则 Principles for choosing formulation strategies

若LogP低且剂量高，适合包合物（如环糊精）或共溶剂辅助。 If logP is low and dose is high, inclusion complexes (like cyclodextrins) or co-solvents are preferred.

若LogP高且熔点低，使用共溶剂、乳剂、胶束等脂质体系。 If logP is high and melting point is low, use co-solvents, emulsions, micelles, or other lipid-based systems.

若LogP高且熔点高，优先采用纳米悬浮或固体分散体等提高粒径分散的手段。 If logP and melting point are both high, choose nanosuspensions or solid dispersions to enhance dispersibility.

5
特殊分子结构和剂量的考量 Special consideration for molecular shape and dose

分子结构特殊时，判断能否包被或形成包含物。 For special molecular shapes, check if inclusion complexation (e.g., cyclodextrin) is possible.

剂量过高时，不建议使用部分策略如纳米体系，可考虑其他协同方法。 For high doses, some approaches (e.g., nanosystems) may be unsuitable; alternative or combined methods should be considered.

6
增加极性与协同提升 Strategies follow the polarity-increasing order; combination is encouraged

各种方法从提升API极性出发，逐级递进，可联合运用。 Strategies are arranged in order of increasing drug polarity and can be combined when needed.

课堂提醒
结合API物性参数（溶解度、logP、熔点、结构）制定最佳策略。 Teacher's reminder: optimize strategy based on API physicochemical attributes (solubility, logP, melting point, molecular structure).

共溶剂及其应用Co-solvents and Their Application

1
溶剂的选择原则 Principle of Solvent Selection
问应选择何种溶剂？Which solvent to use?
答

“相似相溶”原则：“Like dissolves like” principle.

溶解离子化合物的能力与溶剂的介电常数密切相关。The ability to solubilize an ionic compound is closely related to the dielectric constant of the solvent.

溶剂介电常数越大，离子成分的溶解度越大。The higher the dielectric constant, the greater the solubility of an ionic constituent.

非极性倾向溶剂：ε = 2 到 15。Solvents with nonpolar tendency: ε = 2 to 15.

极性倾向溶剂：ε = 15 到 80。Solvents with polar tendency: ε = 15 to 80.

老师强调，“相似相溶”不仅适用于极性/非极性分类，还必须关注具体API的特性与所选溶剂参数的匹配。The teacher emphasized that "like dissolves like" not only refers to polarity classes, but also requires matching solvent parameters to the specific API.

2
API与溶剂介电常数匹配 Matching Dielectric Requirement of API and Solvent

API具有特定的介电需求值。API has a characteristic dielectric requirement value.

只有当所用溶剂或溶剂混合物的介电常数与API的介电需求相等或接近时，才能获得最佳溶解度。Solubility is maximized when the dielectric constant of the solvent or mixture closely equals the dielectric requirement of the API.

曲线说明：API溶解度随溶剂介电常数变化呈峰型分布，峰值对应最佳匹配。The graph shows that API solubility peaks when the solvent's dielectric constant matches the dielectric requirement of the API.

3
共溶剂法的实际应用 Methodology of Co-solvent Use

以水-二恶烷体系为例，通过实验确定API的介电需求。Using water-dioxane systems, experimentally determine the dielectric requirement of the API.

公式：ε_1 = φ_d ε_d + φ_w ε_w， 其中φ代表体积分数，ε表示各自溶剂的介电常数。Formula: ε_1 = φ_d ε_d + φ_w ε_w, where φ is volume fraction and ε is the dielectric constant of each solvent.

实际选择时，应选择与API介电需求最接近的溶剂或溶剂混合物，并结合给药途径。Select the solvent/mixture close to API's dielectric requirement and appropriate to the administration route.

4
共溶剂用量与选择的综合考量 Criteria for Solvent and Amount Choice

需要综合生理和药物技术标准。Consider physiological and pharmaceutical technical criteria.

主要涉及因素：给药途径（口服、注射、皮肤等）；给药条件（输注还是单次注射）；API总剂量；目标人群（儿童、孕妇、酒精过敏者等）；治疗时长。Major influencing factors: route of administration (oral, parenteral, cutaneous, etc.); administration condition (infusion, single injection, etc.); total API dose; target population (child, pregnant woman, alcoholic, etc.); treatment duration.

老师补充：在实际处方设计时，安全性、耐受性和法规也是共溶剂选择的重要依据。The teacher added that in formulation design, safety, tolerability, and regulatory criteria are equally critical in solvent selection.

共溶剂及其应用Co-solvents and Their Application

1
注射剂中常用的水溶性非水共溶剂 Water-miscible Non-aqueous Co-solvents for Injectables

市场上注射剂中常见约6种水溶性非水共溶剂。 About six water-miscible non-aqueous co-solvents are commonly used in marketed injectables.

主要共溶剂按使用频率高低依次为：96%乙醇（ε=24），丙二醇（ε=30），苯甲醇（ε=9.3），甘油（ε=42），PEG 300或400（ε=16），N,N-二甲基乙酰胺（ε=38）。 The major co-solvents (in order of usage frequency) are: ethanol 96% (ε=24), propylene glycol (ε=30), benzyl alcohol (ε=9.3), glycerol (ε=42), PEG 300 or 400 (ε=16), and N,N-dimethylacetamide (ε=38).

多种共溶剂的联合应用很常见。 It is very common to combine several co-solvents in formulations.

老师补充：以水为主溶剂，辅以一种或多种共溶剂提升溶解度，关键在于满足API介电常数需求的同时保证安全有效。 Teacher’s note: Water is always the main solvent, co-solvents are added to achieve the required dielectric constant for solubilizing API while ensuring safety and efficacy.

2
共溶剂对API溶解度的影响 Effect of Co-solvents on API Solubility

对于某一API，溶解度和使用的共溶剂种类密切相关。 For a given API, solubility is highly dependent on the co-solvent used.

曲线显示，不同共溶剂/水体系中，API溶解度均随混合体系介电常数（ε）变化，达到API特征介电需求值时溶解度最大。 Graphs illustrate that solubility in different co-solvent/water mixtures peaks when the system's dielectric constant matches the API’s characteristic dielectric requirement.

特定API的这种介电需求值是固定的，各种共溶剂上表现一致。 Each API has a specific dielectric requirement—a fixed value that’s independent of the co-solvent itself.

溶解曲线呈现“峰型”，选用使体系介电常数和API需求值最接近的组合，可优化溶解度。 Solubility curves show a “peak” pattern; optimal solubility is reached when the solvent system’s dielectric constant approaches that of the API.

3
口服液剂中常用水溶性非水溶剂 Water-miscible Non-aqueous Solvents for Oral Liquids

市场口服液剂常用5种主要水溶性非水共溶剂：PEG 400、乙醇、丙二醇、甘油、山梨醇溶液。 Five common water-miscible non-aqueous co-solvents are used for oral liquid forms: PEG 400, ethanol, propylene glycol, glycerol, and sorbitol solution.

配方选择需根据API物理化学性质及适应症安全标准综合权衡。 Formulation choices must consider API physicochemical properties and relevant safety standards.

4
共溶剂实际应用举例 Examples of Co-solvent Application
注射用苯妥英钠溶液（Dilantin solution injectable）：常用辅料有氢氧化钠、纯化水、丙二醇及乙醇。 Dilantin injectable contains sodium hydroxide, purified water, propylene glycol and ethanol as excipients.

含可待因的止咳糖浆（Sirop Euphon)：每勺含320 mg绝对乙醇。 Codeine anti-cough syrup contains 320 mg absolute alcohol per tablespoon.

爱托泊苷软胶囊（Vepesid 50 mg）：辅料有柠檬酸、甘油、纯化水、PEG 400。 Vepesid (etoposide) softgels: excipients include citric acid, glycerol, purified water, and polyethylene glycol 400.

老师补充：不同用途和剂型的共溶剂选用会依据药品靶标、剂量、人群等灵活调整。 Teacher’s note: Choice of co-solvents in different products adapts to target indication, dose and population.

5
水不混溶共溶剂及应用 Water-immiscible Co-solvents and Application

主要类型有：长链甘油三酯（植物油）、中链甘油三酯（Miglyols）、蜂蜡、α-生育酚、脂肪酸（如油酸）。 Main classes are: long-chain triglycerides (vegetable oils), medium-chain triglycerides (Miglyols), beeswax, α-tocopherol, fatty acids (oleic acid).

多用于硬胶囊/软胶囊包裹难溶API，如异维A酸、孕激素、布洛芬、美维林等。 These are mostly used for hard or soft capsule formulations of poorly soluble APIs (e.g., isotretinoin, progesterone, ibuprofen, mebeverine).

老师强调：对于极疏水API及大剂量应用，水不混溶溶剂为必要选择。 Teacher emphasized: For highly lipophilic APIs and high-dose products, water-immiscible solvents are required.

胶束增溶Micellar solubilization

1
表面活性剂分子结构及其作用 Surfactant Structure and Function

表面活性剂是一种兼具亲水性和疏水性的两亲分子。 A surfactant is an amphiphilic molecule, possessing both hydrophilic and hydrophobic parts.

分子结构包括亲水头部和疏水尾部。 It has a hydrophilic head and a hydrophobic tail.

常见表面活性剂：吐温80（Tween 80）、聚氧乙烯蓖麻油（Cremophor EL）、大豆磷脂（Soy lecithin）、脱氧胆酸钠（Sodium deoxycholate）、泊洛沙姆F68（Poloxamer F68）。 Common surfactants include Tween 80 (polysorbate 80), Cremophor EL, soy lecithin, sodium deoxycholate, and Poloxamer F68.

老师强调，这些表面活性剂的选择受剂型和给药途径安全性等多因素影响，需谨慎评估毒性。 The teacher emphasized that selection of these surfactants requires careful evaluation of toxicity due to differences in safety profiles across formulations and administration routes.

2
胶束的形成与增溶原理 Micelle Formation and Solubilization Mechanism

当表面活性剂浓度达到临界胶束浓度（CMC）时，自发形成胶束。 When the surfactant concentration reaches the critical micellar concentration (CMC), micelles spontaneously form.

疏水性API可被包埋在胶束的疏水核心，形成胶束溶液。 Hydrophobic APIs can be entrapped in the hydrophobic core of the micelle, resulting in a micellar solution.

增溶机制：疏水药物分子被胶束包埋，从而增加其在水中的表观溶解度。 The solubilization mechanism is that hydrophobic drug molecules are encapsulated in micelles, increasing apparent solubility in water.

老师提示：需警惕表面活性剂毒性（部分药物类别禁用），并注意给药途径的适用性。 Teacher’s note: Beware of surfactant toxicity (some classes are forbidden for certain drugs) and route-appropriateness.

3
药物稀释现象需关注 Attention to Dilution Phenomena

稀释时胶束可能解离，导致API析出。 Upon dilution, micelles can dissociate, leading to precipitation of the API.

老师反复提醒，临床配伍及给药时要关注稀释现象，否则药物易失效或产生不良反应。 Teacher repeatedly warned that attention must be paid to dilution, or the drug may precipitate and cause loss of efficacy or adverse reactions.

环糊精Cyclodextrins

1
环糊精的来源及性质 Origin and Nature of Cyclodextrins

环糊精由淀粉通过酶降解反应（环糊精-葡萄糖基转移酶，CGT）获得。 Cyclodextrins are derived from starch by enzymatic degradation using cyclodextrine-glucosyl transferase (CGT).

它们属于环状低聚糖（环状寡糖）。 They are classified as cyclic oligosaccharides.

老师补充，因来源为多糖，安全性高，易被人体代谢或排泄。 Teacher added that since they are polysaccharide-derived, cyclodextrins are generally safe and easily metabolized or excreted.

2
α、β、γ-环糊精的分子结构及特点 Structure and Properties of α-, β-, γ-cyclodextrins

α、β、γ-环糊精分别含有6、7、8个葡萄糖单元。 Alpha, beta, and gamma cyclodextrins contain 6, 7, and 8 glucopyranose units, respectively.

分别对应不同的分子腔直径：α为14.6 Å/4.9 Å，β为15.4 Å/6.2 Å，γ为17.5 Å/7.9 Å；腔高均为7.9 Å。 Each has a characteristic internal diameter: α-CD (14.6 Å/4.9 Å), β-CD (15.4 Å/6.2 Å), γ-CD (17.5 Å/7.9 Å), and all have a height of 7.9 Å.

常温水溶性（g/L）：α-CD为145，β-CD为18.5，γ-CD为233。 Water solubility at 25℃ (g/L): α-CD 145, β-CD 18.5, γ-CD 233.

不同尺寸的内腔允许包埋不同分子大小和形状的疏水性API。 The different cavity sizes allow encapsulation of hydrophobic APIs with various dimensions and shapes.

3
功能化环糊精及其特性 Functionalized Cyclodextrins and Their Properties

常用修饰类型有：甲基-β-环糊精（Methyl-β-CD）、羟丙基-β-环糊精（Hydroxypropyl-β-CD, HPβCD）、磺丁基醚-β-环糊精（Sulfobutylether-β-CD, SBE7CD）。 Common modifications include methyl-β-CD (Dimeb, Rameb), hydroxypropyl-β-CD (HPβCD), and sulfobutylether-β-CD (SBE7CD).

这些修饰提升了水溶性，使其更适合于药物的增溶和包合作用。 Such modifications greatly increase water solubility, making them more suitable for drug solubilization and inclusion.

某些衍生型（如磺丁基醚-β-环糊精）为阴离子型，适用于特殊药物体系。 Some derivatives (e.g., sulfobutylether-β-CD) are anionic, fit for specific pharmaceutical environments.

老师强调，通过功能化可调节环糊精的包合能力和药物相容性。 The teacher emphasized that chemical modifications enable tuning of inclusion capacity and drug compatibility.

环糊精Cyclodextrins

1
环糊精的结构与包合作用 Cyclodextrin Structure and Inclusion

环糊精是由 6、7、8 个葡萄糖单元环状连接而成的α-、β-、γ-环糊精。 Cyclodextrins are cyclic compounds composed of 6, 7, or 8 glucopyranose units—α-, β-, and γ-CD.

分子结构类似外部亲水、内部疏水的中空圆台。 They have a structure like a hollow conical trunk with a hydrophilic outside and a lipophilic central cavity.

内部疏水腔可以容纳脂溶性客体分子，形成包合物。 The hydrophobic cavity can host lipophilic guest molecules to form inclusion complexes.

环糊精能够在水环境中包埋疏水性药物分子。 Cyclodextrins can encapsulate hydrophobic drugs in aqueous environments.

包合后，环糊精能够掩盖被包分子的性质，使其更易于在水中分散和运输。 After inclusion, cyclodextrins mask the properties of the hosted molecule, enhancing water compatibility and transportability.

老师强调，环糊精可作为“分子传输体”，帮助难溶药物进入体液环境。 The teacher emphasized that cyclodextrins act as “molecule transporters,” aiding poorly soluble drugs in biological fluids.

2
包合机制机理及动力学机制 Inclusion Mechanism and Affinity

API与环糊精以可逆方式形成包合物，平衡受亲和常数控制。 APIs form reversible inclusion complexes with cyclodextrins, governed by an affinity constant.

体系可表示为：API（溶解态）＋CD ⇌ 包合物（溶解态）。 The system can be represented as: API (dissolved) + CD ⇌ Inclusion Complex (dissolved).

API存在固体-溶液动态平衡，包合物同理。 There is a solid–liquid equilibrium for both the free API and the inclusion complex.

老师补充，包合反应是动力学可逆的，影响包合效率的主要因素是亲和常数。 The teacher added that inclusion is kinetically reversible; the main factor for efficiency is the affinity constant.

3
包合相溶度图与亲和常数 Phase Solubility Diagram and Affinity Constant

相溶度图绘制API总溶度随环糊精浓度变化。 The phase solubility diagram shows total API solubility as a function of cyclodextrin concentration.

直线型曲线表示1:1可溶复合物生成，斜率越大增溶能力越强。 A linear curve indicates 1:1 soluble complex formation; the steeper the slope, the greater the solubilizing ability.

S型（B型）曲线说明生成不溶或部分溶解包合物。 A B-type curve suggests formation of insoluble or partially soluble inclusion complexes.

1:1包合常数计算公式：K₁:₁ = slope/S₀(1-slope) The 1:1 inclusion constant is calculated as: K₁:₁ = slope / (S₀ * (1 – slope))

老师强调，选择合适的环糊精和浓度对于溶解度提升和药效至关重要。 The teacher emphasized that selecting an appropriate type and concentration of cyclodextrin is crucial for optimal solubilization and efficacy.

4
案例：环糊精增溶效果 Cyclodextrin Example: Solubilization Enhancement

举例：2-羟丙基-β-环糊精（2-HPβCD）能大幅提升甲基睾酮在水中的表观溶解度。 For example, 2-hydroxypropyl-β-cyclodextrin (2-HPβCD) can significantly increase the apparent solubility of methyltestosterone in water.

同时对比苯脱氧胆酸钠、1,2-丙二醇等增溶剂，2-HPβCD表现出最强增溶效果。 Compared to sodium deoxycholate and 1,2-propylene glycol, 2-HPβCD shows the strongest solubilizing effect.

老师补充：实验中应根据药物特点优选最有效的增溶系统。 The teacher added that the most efficient solubilization system should be selected experimentally based on the characteristics of the drug.

>

注射途径=注射给药方式Parenteral route = administration by injection
1
注射途径的定义和类型 Definition and types of parenteral route
注射给药是指药物通过注射方式进入体内，不经过消化道。 Parenteral administration means delivering drugs into the body by injection, bypassing the digestive tract.

主要包括静脉注射（intravenous, IV）、皮下注射（subcutaneous, SC）、肌内注射（intramuscular, IM），以及皮内注射（intradermal）。 Main types include intravenous (IV), subcutaneous (SC), intramuscular (IM), and intradermal injection.

除此之外，还包括鞘内注射、关节腔注射、眼内注射、耳腔注射等，通常用于局部作用。 There are also intra-spinal, intra-articular, intra-ocular, and intra-auricular injections, usually for local action.

老师强调，不同注射途径选择取决于药物性质、给药目的及需要的局部或全身药效。 The teacher emphasized that route selection depends on drug properties, therapeutic goal, and whether local or systemic effects are needed.

注射制剂Parenteral preparations
2
注射制剂的类型和定义 Types and definitions of parenteral preparations
注射制剂是指为注射、输注或植入人体或动物体内而制备的无菌制剂。 Parenteral preparations are sterile products intended to be injected, infused, or implanted into the human or animal body.

常见包括：注射用液体、用于输注的制剂、注射用凝胶和植入剂。 Common forms include injectable preparations, preparations for perfusion, injectable gels, and implants.

还包括注射或输注用稀释液、注射或输注用粉末。 They also include preparations to dilute for injection/perfusion and powders for injection or perfusion.

老师补充，所有注射制剂要求严格无菌，部分需要配伍溶剂现场重建。 The teacher added that all parenteral preparations must meet strict sterility requirements, and some require reconstitution with a specific solvent before use.

注射途径与起效时间Parenteral route and time of action
3
注射途径与药效起效快慢关系 Time of action for different parenteral routes

不同注射途径起效速度不同，静脉注射起效最快，可立即达到有效血药浓度。 Different parenteral routes have different onset speeds; IV injection acts fastest, reaching effective plasma concentration immediately.

肌肉注射和皮下注射吸收速度较慢，血药浓度上升和下降均较缓。 Intramuscular and subcutaneous injections have slower absorption and declining curves for plasma concentration.

曲线显示静脉注射Cmax最早，肌肉和皮下注射分别次之；持续时间则相反。 The chart shows that IV reaches Cmax earliest, followed by IM and SC; the duration of effect is higher for IM and SC.

老师强调，给药速度和持续时间选择应根据临床需要调整。 The teacher emphasized that the choice of speed and duration should be tailored to clinical needs.

注射途径的剂型系统选择Parenteral route: choice of system
4
注射制剂的系统类型与特点 Types of injectable systems and their features
注射制剂包括单相系统和分散系统。 Injectable preparations comprise monophasic and dispersed systems.

单相系统包括溶液：API处于分子状态。可为水溶液、胶束溶液、水混溶非水溶液和油溶液。 Monophasic systems include solutions: API is in the molecular state. Can be aqueous, micellar, water-miscible non-aqueous, or oily solutions.

分散系统包括悬浮液和乳剂。悬浮液为固体/液体分散，分为水性、胶束、胶体或油性；乳剂则为分散相分子状态，有o/w（水包油）和w/o（油包水）型。 Dispersed systems include suspensions and emulsions. Suspensions are solid/liquid dispersions (aqueous, micellar, colloidal, oily); emulsions disperse API molecularly in o/w or w/o phases.

单相溶液需保持澄清，悬浮系统要求可再分散沉淀，乳剂要求不分层且滴径小于1微米。 Solutions must be clear; suspensions must have redispersible sediment; emulsions require no phase separation, with droplet size below 1 μm.

老师补充，选择剂型需结合API理化性质和目标使用场景。 The teacher added that dosage form selection should match API physicochemical properties and target application.

注射剂型预制剂型选择Preformulation: choice of pharmaceutical form
5
API对注射制剂剂型选择的影响 Influence of API properties on dosage form selection

API的水溶性决定首选的溶剂类型，可为水溶剂、水混溶非水溶剂或水不混溶溶剂。 API water solubility determines the preferred solvent: aqueous, water-miscible non-aqueous, or immiscible solvent may be used.

可根据需求添加表面活性剂或环糊精以增强溶解度。 Surfactants or cyclodextrins can be added to improve solubility if necessary.

老师强调，注射剂型的溶剂和增溶策略需兼顾安全性、稳定性与生物利用度。 The teacher emphasized that the choice of solvent and solubilization strategy for injectables must consider safety, stability, and bioavailability.

渗透压与溶液渗透浓度Osmotic pressure and osmolarity of a solution

1
渗透压定义 Definition of osmotic pressure

渗透压是指为阻止溶剂分子通过半透膜流动而需要施加的压力差。 Osmotic pressure is the pressure difference needed to stop the flow of solvent molecules across a semipermeable membrane. 渗透压是阻止溶剂分子流过半透膜所需的压力差。

半透膜只允许溶剂分子自由通过，但阻挡溶质分子。 A semipermeable membrane is hermetic to solute molecules but allows solvent molecules to pass. 半透膜对溶质分子来说是密封的，但允许

渗透压的本质体现了溶液和纯溶剂之间溶剂分子自发扩散的驱动力。 Osmotic pressure reflects the driving force of spontaneous diffusion of solvent molecules from pure solvent to solution. 渗透压反映了溶剂分子从纯溶剂自发扩散到溶液的驱动力。

2
渗透过程示意与物理含义 Schematic and physical meaning of osmosis

在左图中，两侧分别为纯溶剂和溶液，用半透膜隔开，只有溶剂分子能够通过。 In the left diagram, there is pure solvent on one side and solution on the other, separated by a semipermeable membrane that only allows solvent molecules to pass. 在左图中，一侧是纯溶剂，另一侧是溶液，中间由一层只允许溶剂分子通过的半透膜隔开。

溶剂分子会从低溶质浓度一侧（纯溶剂）向高溶质浓度一侧（溶液）迁移。 Solvent molecules migrate from the side with low solute concentration (pure solvent) to the side with high solute concentration (solution). 溶剂分子从溶质浓度低的一侧（纯溶剂）迁移到溶质浓度高的一侧（溶液）。

该过程称为渗透作用，导致溶液液面升高。 This process is called osmosis, causing the liquid level of the solution side to rise. 这个过程称为渗透，导致溶液侧的液面上升。

如果向溶液侧施加外部压力，使净流动为零，这时所需的压力即为渗透压。 If external pressure is applied to the solution side to counteract osmosis so that no net flow occurs, the required pressure is the osmotic pressure. 如果在溶液侧施加外部压力以抵消渗透作用，从而不发生净流，则所需的压力就是渗透压。

3
渗透压与溶质摩尔浓度的关系 Relationship with molar concentration

溶液的渗透压正比于溶液中溶质粒子的摩尔浓度。 The osmotic pressure of a solution is proportional to the molar concentration of solute particles in solution. 溶液的渗透压与溶液中溶质颗粒的摩尔浓度成正比。

公式：π=cRTπ，其中π为渗透压，c为溶质摩尔浓度，R为气体常数，T为热力学温度。 Equation: π=cRTπ, where π is the osmotic pressure, c the molar concentration, R the gas constant, and T the temperature. 方程： π=cRTπ ，其中 π 是渗透压， c 是摩尔浓度， R 是气体常数， T 是温度。

老师强调，渗透压对于注射液等体内用药尤为重要，要与体液保持等渗，防止溶血或组织损伤。 The teacher emphasized that osmotic pressure is crucial for injectable solutions to ensure isotonicity with body fluids and prevent hemolysis or tissue damage. 老师强调，渗透压对于注射液至关重要，以确保与体液等渗，防止溶血或组织损伤。

注射液体制剂必须与血浆等渗Injectable liquid forms must be isotonic to blood plasma
1
等渗性的重要性 Importance of isotonicity
注射液体制剂必须与血浆等渗，否则会对红细胞造成损伤。 Injectable liquid forms must be isotonic with blood plasma, otherwise they will damage erythrocytes.

等渗性指溶液的渗透压与体液（血浆）保持一致。 Isotonicity means the solution’s osmotic pressure equals that of body fluids (plasma).

2
红细胞在不同渗透环境中的变化 Erythrocytes in different osmotic environments

在高渗环境中，红细胞失水收缩，出现质壁分离，此过程是可逆的。 In hypertonic environments, erythrocytes lose water and shrink, leading to plasmolysis (this process is reversible).

在低渗环境中，红细胞吸水膨胀并发生溶血，此过程是不可逆的。 In hypotonic environments, erythrocytes absorb water, swell, and undergo hemolysis (this process is irreversible).

老师强调，注射溶液若为低渗，将引起红细胞破裂，严重危害患者安全。 The teacher emphasized that if the injectable is hypotonic, it will cause red blood cell lysis and pose a serious risk to patient safety.

等渗环境下，红细胞保持正常形态和功能。 Under isotonic conditions, erythrocytes maintain normal shape and function.

3
人体血浆渗透压范围 Osmolarity range of human blood plasma

人体血浆的渗透压（渗透浓度）一般为 270-300 mosmol/L。 The osmolarity of human blood plasma is generally 270-300 mosmol/L.

注射用液体制剂应调整至该渗透浓度范围，确保安全。 Injectable liquid preparations should be adjusted to this osmolarity range to ensure safety.

老师补充，等渗性是注射剂型注册和管理的硬性标准。 The teacher added that isotonicity is a mandatory requirement for registration and approval of injectables.

等渗性Isotonicity

1
注射液的等渗性要求 Injectable liquid forms must be isotonic
注射液必须与血浆等渗，否则会对红细胞等组织造成损伤。 Injectable liquid forms must be isotonic with blood plasma; otherwise, they can damage erythrocytes and tissues.

等渗是指溶液的渗透压与人体血浆渗透压保持一致。 Isotonicity means the osmotic pressure of the solution matches that of human blood plasma.

2
等渗性计算与调节 Calculation and adjustment of isotonicity

通过测定溶液的渗透压（渗透浓度），可以计算出需加入的氯化钠质量使配方达到等渗。 By measuring the solution’s osmolarity, we can calculate the amount of sodium chloride to add to achieve isotonicity.

计算公式中，ΔTiso 代表等渗降温值，ΔTexp 代表样品实际测得的降温值，ΔTNaCl1% 是 1% NaCl 溶液的降温值。 In the calculation, ΔTiso indicates the freezing point depression for isotonicity, ΔTexp is the measured freezing point depression of the sample, and ΔTNaCl1% is the reference for 1% NaCl.

3
人体血浆的渗透压范围 Osmolarity range of human plasma

人体血浆的渗透压范围为270-300 mosmol/L，是调整注射液等渗的标准依据。 The osmolarity of human plasma is 270-300 mosmol/L, which serves as the standard for isotonic adjustment of injectables.

常用的等渗溶液有0.9%（质量/质量）NaCl和5%（质量/质量）葡萄糖溶液。 Commonly used isotonic solutions include 0.9% w/w NaCl and 5% w/w glucose solutions.

4
实验和调配注意事项 Experimental and formulation notes

实际制备中可通过冷却点下降法测得渗透压，并用公式计算需补加NaCl的量。 In practice, osmolarity can be measured using freezing point depression, and the required amount of NaCl is calculated accordingly.

老师强调，只有调整到等渗状态的注射液才能安全用于静脉等体内给药。 The teacher emphasized that only injectables properly adjusted to isotonicity are safe for intravenous and other systemic administration.

其他预制参数Other preformulation parameters

1
pH 调节 pH adjustment
注射液体制剂的pH需尽量接近7.4。 The pH of injectable liquid forms should be as close to 7.4 as possible.

可以通过加入酸或碱来调节pH值。 pH can be adjusted by adding an acid or a base.

若活性成分的化学稳定性区间非常狭窄，则需添加缓冲剂。 If the API has a narrow chemical stability range, a buffer should be added.

老师强调，pH调节不仅影响药物稳定性，还关系到注射时的生物相容性和无痛性。 The teacher emphasized that pH adjustment affects not only drug stability, but also biocompatibility and painlessness upon injection.

2
抗氧化剂 Antioxidants

为防止氧化降解，可添加抗氧化剂，如硫代硫酸钠（Na2S2O3）、维生素C、亚硫酸氢钠（NaHSO3）和亚硫酸氢钠（Na2S2O5）。 To prevent oxidative degradation, antioxidants such as sodium thiosulfate (Na2S2O3), vitamin C, sodium bisulfite (NaHSO3), and sodium metabisulfite (Na2S2O5) can be added.

老师补充，抗氧化剂优先消耗氧化剂，保护主药成分的稳定性。 The teacher added that antioxidants preferentially react with oxidants, thus protecting the main active ingredient’s stability.

注射制剂的必须要求Required specifications for parenteral forms
1
澄明性（无可见颗粒）Limpidity (no visible particules in solutions)
注射液体制剂必须澄明，在溶液中不能有任何可见颗粒。 Parenteral solutions must be clear, with no visible particles in the solution.

教师强调，若溶液混浊或有颗粒，则质量不合格，不能用于注射。 The teacher emphasized that turbid or particulate-containing solutions are unacceptable for injection.

2
等渗性Isotonicity
注射液渗透压必须与血浆等渗（270-300 mosmol/l）。 The osmolarity of the injectable form must be isotonic with blood plasma (270-300 mosmol/l).
注射溶液如非等渗，会导致红细胞损伤甚至溶血，危及患者安全。 Non-isotonic injectable solutions can damage erythrocytes and may cause hemolysis, endangering patient safety.
3
中性pH（接近7.4）Neutrality (pH close to 7.4)
注射液pH值必须接近7.4，以保证生物相容性和减少刺激。 The pH should be as close as possible to 7.4 to ensure biocompatibility and reduce irritation.

pH太偏离7.4会导致注射部位刺激或疼痛，不利于用药安全。 A pH far from 7.4 can cause irritation or pain at the injection site, affecting patient safety.

4
无痛Painless
注射液必须设计为无痛，避免注射过程引起患者不适。 The preparation should be painless to prevent discomfort during administration.

老师补充，无痛性不仅提升患者体验，也是质量要求之一。 The teacher added that painless administration improves patient experience and is a key quality standard.

5
无菌Sterility

必须保持绝对无菌，防止微生物污染。 Absolute sterility must be ensured to prevent microbial contamination.

每批次都需经过严格的无菌检测。 Every batch must undergo strict sterility testing.

6
无热原性Apyrogenous
注射液不得含有任何致热原物质。 The solution must not contain any pyrogenic (fever-causing) substances.

老师强调，热原污染会诱发高热反应，严重威胁患者健康。 The teacher emphasized that pyrogens can cause fever reactions and seriously threaten patient health.

小分子和生物大分子的创新制剂策略Current and innovative formulation strategies for small challenging API and biomacromolecules

1
从传统制剂与生产到创新药物递送系统From conventional formulation and manufacturing to innovative drug delivery systems

本课程内容包括混合、制粒、压片的原理，以及片剂的预制和制备，液体和半固体制剂的配制，以及冻干工艺。 This course covers principles of blending, granulation, tabletting, pre-formulation and formulation of tablets, formulation of liquid and semisolid forms, and freeze-drying.

老师强调创新型递送系统的开发需建立在对传统工艺的深入理解基础之上。 The teacher emphasizes that developing innovative delivery systems must be based on a solid understanding of conventional processes.

2
液体与半固体制剂Liquid and semi-solid formulations

液体及半固体制剂主要包括溶液和分散体。 Liquid and semi-solid formulations mainly include solutions and dispersions.

分散体包括悬浮剂、乳剂和霜剂。 Dispersions include suspensions, emulsions, and creams.

液体与半固体制剂Liquid and semi-solid formulations

1
液体制剂Liquid formulations
溶液
将活性药物成分（API）溶解于一种溶剂中。 Solution: Dissolving the API in a solvent.

常用溶剂包括水、水溶性非水溶剂和水不溶性非水溶剂。 Common solvents include water, water-miscible non-aqueous solvents, and water-immiscible non-aqueous solvents.

注射用溶液允许只用水或加用水溶性非水溶剂。 Injectable solutions allow water or water-miscible non-aqueous solvents.
2
具体溶剂类型Specific types of solvents

水溶性非水溶剂包括乙醇、苄醇、甘油、丙二醇、多元醇酯、聚氧乙烯醚和N,N-二甲基乙酰胺。 Water-miscible non-aqueous solvents include ethanol, benzyl alcohol, glycerol, propylene glycol, polyol esters, polyoxyethylene glycol, and N,N-dimethylacetamide.

水不溶性非水溶剂包括植物油（橄榄油、玉米油、蓖麻油、花生油）、矿物油、油酸乙酯、肉豆蔻酸乙酯和苄基苯甲酸酯。 Water-immiscible non-aqueous solvents include vegetable oils (olive, corn, castor, peanut), mineral oil, ethyl oleate, ethyl myristate, and benzyl benzoate.

3
老师补充说明Teacher’s additional notes

合理选择溶剂系统对API的溶解度、稳定性以及给药途径安全性均有显著影响。 Rational selection of solvent systems greatly affects the solubility, stability of the API, and safety of the administration route.

注射剂用非水溶剂需满足安全和生物相容性需求，通常限制使用类别和用量。 Non-aqueous solvents for injectables must meet safety and biocompatibility requirements, with restricted categories and quantities.

部分半固体制剂还需考虑分散体系的类型与物理稳定性。 Some semi-solid formulations also need to consider the type of dispersion and its physical stability.

液体与半固体制剂Liquid and semi-solid formulations

【注射用溶液关键组分与调节参数Summary of Key Components and Parameters for Injectable Solutions】

1
pH调节pH Adjustment

目标pH为7.4，常用NaOH或HCl进行调节，必要时加缓冲剂。 The target pH is 7.4, adjusted with NaOH or HCl, and buffer can be added if necessary.

在实际配制中，老师强调注射液pH尽量接近血液pH，保证生物相容性和患者舒适。 In practice, the teacher stressed that the pH of injectables should be as close as possible to blood pH for biocompatibility and patient comfort.

对于pH稳定性范围狭窄的API，则须用缓冲剂保持pH稳定。 If the API’s pH stability range is narrow, a buffer must be used to maintain stable pH.

2
抗氧化剂Antioxidant

常用维生素C、亚硫酸氢钠、亚硫酸钠、硫代硫酸钠，防止活性成分被氧化破坏。 Common antioxidants are ascorbic acid (vitamin C), sodium metabisulfite, sodium thiosulfate—added to prevent oxidative degradation of the active ingredient.

老师补充，抗氧化剂首先与氧化剂发生反应，从而优先保护主药的稳定性。 The teacher added that antioxidants preferentially react with oxidants, thus stabilizing the API.

3
螯合剂Chelating Agent

常用乙二胺四乙酸（EDTA）以结合痕量金属离子，进一步防止催化性氧化。 Ethylenediaminetetraacetic acid (EDTA) is frequently used to chelate trace metals and prevent catalyzed oxidation.

4
澄清度与无菌性要求Solution Clarity & Sterilization

所有成分必须完全溶解于液体中，成品溶液澄清透明。 All components must be fully dissolved—the final solution must be clear and transparent.

注射用溶液必须严格灭菌，确保无菌性。 Injectable solutions must be strictly sterilized to ensure sterility.

静脉或眼用溶液的制备关键点Key Points for IV-Injectable or Ocular Solutions

1
等渗剂Tonicity Agent

主要使用氯化钠调整渗透压，使制剂等渗。 Sodium chloride is mainly used to adjust osmotic pressure for isotonicity.

浓度不当会导致红细胞皱缩（高渗）或膨胀破裂（低渗），危及安全。 Improper tonicity may cause erythrocyte shrinkage (hypertonic) or lysis (hypotonic), compromising safety.

2
等渗性计算与操作Isotonicity Calculation & Operation

等渗性可用拉乌尔定律和Blagden定律说明，定量公式如下： Isotonicity can be described by Raoult’s and Blagden’s laws; quantitative formula: ΔT = K · i · b_solute ΔT = 冻点降低（温度） K = 冻点降低常数 i = 范特霍夫因子 b_solute = 溶质的质量摩尔浓度 ΔT = freezing point depression (temperature difference) K = cryoscopic constant i = van’t Hoff factor b_solute = solute molality

等渗剂所需质量的计算公式如下
Formula for calculating the required mass percentage of NaCl (tonicity agent): m_NaCl% = (ΔT_iso - ΔT_exp)/ΔT_NaCl1% m_NaCl%是氯化钠的质量百分比 ΔT_iso为理想等渗降温值（血液：-0.52°C） ΔT_exp为实验测得值 ΔT_NaCl1%为1%氯化钠的降温值（-0.58°C） m_NaCl% is NaCl mass% ΔT_iso is ideal isotonic depression for blood (-0.52°C) ΔT_exp is the experimental value ΔT_NaCl1% is 1% NaCl freezing-point depression (-0.58°C)

制剂等渗常用0.9%氯化钠溶液或5%葡萄糖溶液。 Standard isotonic solutions: 0.9% NaCl or 5% glucose.

3
课上强调重点In-Class Highlights

所有注射液必须严守澄明和无菌的质量底线，防止可见杂质和微生物。 Absolute clarity and sterility are mandatory for all injectables, preventing visible impurities and microbial contamination.

教师特别指出，等渗和pH调整同样关乎药物安全和疗效。 The teacher specifically pointed out that both isotonicity and pH adjustment are vital for drug safety and efficacy.

液体与半固体制剂Liquid and semi-solid formulations

溶液
表面活性剂和胶束溶液 Solutions: Surfactants and Micellar Solutions

利用表面活性剂可形成胶束溶液，用于增加难溶性API的溶解度。 Surfactants are used to form micellar solutions, which increase the solubility of poorly soluble APIs.

胶束能包埋不溶性API，通过这种机制促进API在水中的分散和溶解。 Micelles can encapsulate insoluble APIs, thus facilitating their dispersion and solubilization in water.

2
胶束临界浓度的测定 Determination of Critical Micellar Concentration (CMC)

必须确定表面活性剂的临界胶束浓度（CMC），即胶束开始形成的最低浓度。 It is crucial to determine the critical micellar concentration (CMC) of the surfactant, which is the lowest concentration at which micelles start to form.

常用表面张力法测定CMC，通过测量不同浓度下表面张力的变化获得。 The CMC is usually determined using surface tension measurements by recording surface tension changes at different surfactant concentrations.

具体实验方法为铂板法，通过如下公式计算表面张力： The typical experiment uses the platinum plate method, calculating surface tension as:

其中γ为表面张力，F为作用于铂板的拉力，L为板长。

Here, γ is the surface tension, F is the force on the platinum plate, and L is the length of the plate.

3
胶束化对API溶解性的影响 Effect of Micellization on API Solubility

随着表面活性剂浓度增加至CMC，表面张力急剧下降并趋于平稳，此时胶束大量形成。 As surfactant concentration increases to the CMC, surface tension drops sharply and then plateaus, indicating significant micelle formation.

老师强调，在实际应用中，胶束化策略经常被用作解决API水溶性不足的问题。 The lecturer emphasized that, in practice, micellization is a common strategy to solve the poor aqueous solubility of APIs.

4
胶束形成的动力学/热力学方程 Kinetics/Thermodynamics of Micelle Formation

胶束形成可表示为

​，S为表面活性剂分子，n为组成一个胶束所需分子数，Mn​为含n分子的胶束。 Micelle formation is represented as nS⇌MnnS⇌Mn​, where SS is a surfactant molecule, nn is the number of molecules per micelle, and MnMn​ is the micelle.

平衡常数公式如下
The equilibrium constant is:

其中k1​、k−1​为正、逆反应速率常数，[Mn​]为胶束浓度，[S]为游离表面活性剂浓度。

Where k1​, k−1​ are the forward and reverse rate constants, [Mn​] is the micelle concentration, and [S] is the free surfactant concentration.

5
课上补充说明 In-Class Additions

老师讲到，CMC值是设计和优化胶束制剂时的关键参数，过多或过少都影响胶束对API的包埋效果和制剂的安全性。 The teacher mentioned that CMC is a crucial parameter for designing and optimizing micellar formulations, with too high or too low concentrations affecting micellar encapsulation and safety.

胶束包埋还可一定程度保护API免受水解或降解，提高药物的稳定性。 Micellar encapsulation can also protect APIs from hydrolysis or degradation, enhancing drug stability.

液体与半固体制剂——胶束溶液与混悬液Liquid and semi-solid formulations—Micellar solutions and suspensions

溶液
API的胶束溶解Solutions: Solubilisation of the API by micellar solutions

难溶性API可通过表面活性剂形成胶束溶液来增溶。 Poorly soluble APIs can be solubilized by forming micellar solutions with surfactants.

胶束是一种由表面活性剂自组装形成的纳米结构，分为球形胶束和棒状（长条状）胶束。 Micelles are nano-structures self-assembled from surfactants, including spherical and rod (elongated) micelles.

球形胶束常在较低表面活性剂浓度和较高离子强度时形成，棒状胶束多出现在高表面活性剂浓度条件下。 Spherical micelles usually form at lower surfactant concentrations and higher ionic strength, while rod-shaped micelles appear at higher concentrations.

API分子可被包埋于胶束的疏水核心，从而在水中分散和增溶。 API molecules can be encapsulated in the hydrophobic core of micelles, aiding their dispersion and solubilization in water.

在显微镜下可观察到胶束的纳米结构形态。 The nano-structural shapes of micelles can be observed under microscopy.

2
课上补充In-class supplementation

老师强调，对于难溶性API，胶束溶液是一种常用且实用的药物递送策略。 The teacher emphasized that for poorly soluble APIs, micellar solutions are a common and practical drug delivery strategy.

胶束本身还能保护API，减弱降解速率。 Micelles may also protect APIs and reduce their degradation rate.

悬浮剂
粒子悬浮的决定因素Suspensions: Factors allowing particles to remain suspended

悬浮剂中API以微小固体颗粒状态分散在液体中。 In suspensions, APIs are dispersed as fine solid particles in the liquid phase.

颗粒能否长时间悬浮受多种因素影响
Several factors influence the ability of particles to remain suspended:   a. 颗粒对液体的亲和力（润湿性） The affinity of solid particles for the liquid (wettability)   b. 颗粒表面电荷（颗粒间排斥的静电作用） The surface charge of the particles (repulsive electrostatic interactions between particles)   c. 液相的粘度（通过增加粘度，补偿颗粒的密度和重力加速度带来的沉降） The viscosity of the liquid phase (higher viscosity compensates for the effect of density and gravitational acceleration, reducing sedimentation)

如果这些条件不充分，颗粒会由于重力作用下沉形成沉淀。 If these factors are insufficient, particles will settle due to gravity and form sediment.

4
课上补充In-class supplementation

老师指出，良好的悬浮稳定性对于剂型的均一性和药效至关重要，需综合调节以上因素。 The teacher noted that good suspension stability is critical for dose uniformity and efficacy, requiring comprehensive adjustment of the above factors.

液体与半固体制剂——悬浮剂的润湿性

Liquid and semi-solid formulations—Wettability of Suspensions

1
悬浮剂Suspensions

API以微小固体颗粒状态分散于液体载体中。 The active pharmaceutical ingredient (API) is dispersed as fine solid particles in a liquid vehicle.

2
润湿性Wettability

润湿性是指液体对固体表面的铺展能力。 Wettability refers to the ability of a liquid to spread over a solid surface.

良好的润湿性可以通过加入润湿剂（如表面活性剂）来实现，提高疏水性颗粒对水的亲和力。 Good wettability can be achieved by adding wetting agents (e.g., surfactants), which improve the affinity of hydrophobic particles for water.

3
润湿效果对比Comparison of Wetting Effects
坏润湿
水在疏水表面上的接触角θ较大，液滴呈球状，无法铺展，颗粒难以均匀分散。 Bad wetting: The contact angle θ of water on a hydrophobic surface is large, the droplet remains spherical and does not spread, making it difficult for particles to disperse uniformly.
好润湿
利用润湿剂后，水滴铺展，接触角θ减小，颗粒易被液体包裹和分散。 Good wetting: With wetting agents, the water droplet spreads out and the contact angle θ decreases, allowing particles to be more easily coated and dispersed by the liquid.
4
表面活性剂的作用Role of Surfactants

表面活性剂可自发吸附在疏水颗粒表面，降低水和颗粒之间的界面张力，提高润湿性。 Surfactants spontaneously adsorb onto hydrophobic particle surfaces, reducing interfacial tension between water and the particles, and thereby increasing wettability.

5
课堂补充In-class supplement

老师强调，评估润湿性的常用方法是测量接触角，润湿剂可显著降低接触角。 The teacher emphasized that wettability is commonly assessed by measuring the contact angle, which can be significantly reduced by wetting agents.

良好的润湿性对悬浮剂的稳定性和分散均匀性至关重要。 Good wettability is crucial for the stability and uniform dispersion of suspensions.

液体与半固体制剂——悬浮剂的沉降

Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations—Sedimentation of Suspensions

1
悬浮剂的沉降(Sedimentation in Suspensions)

1.1 悬浮剂中颗粒会受重力影响沉降，影响整个体系的稳定性。

In suspensions, particles tend to settle over time due to gravity, affecting system stability.

1.2 斯托克斯定律(Stockes Law)用于描述颗粒沉降速度V，其表达式为：

Stockes Law describes the sedimentation rate V of particles, expressed as:

V = (2 * r^2 * (ρ1 - ρ2) * g) / (9 * η)

其中：

Where:

V为沉降速度 (sedimentation rate)

r为颗粒半径 (particles radius)

ρ1-ρ2为固体与液体的密度差 (difference in density between solid and liquid phases)

g为重力加速度 (acceleration due to gravity)

η为液体的粘度 (viscosity of the liquid)

1.3 斯托克斯定律告诉我们，粒径越大、密度差越大则沉降越快，液体粘度越高沉降越慢。

According to Stockes law, larger particle size and greater density difference accelerate sedimentation, while higher liquid viscosity slows it down.

2
颗粒间作用力决定沉淀性状（沉淀物的类型）
2
Particle-particle interactions determine the characteristics of the sediment.

2.1 颗粒之间的吸引或排斥作用会影响悬浮剂中沉淀物的松散或致密程度。

Attractive or repulsive interactions between particles affect whether the sediment is loose (flocculated) or dense (deflocculated).

2.2 松散絮凝（flocculated）时，颗粒松散聚集，沉降快，但较易重悬。

In the flocculated state, particles loosely aggregate, settle quickly, but are easy to redisperse.

2.3 非絮凝（deflocculated）时，颗粒分散均匀，沉降慢，但底部沉淀紧密难以重悬。

In the deflocculated state, particles are evenly dispersed and settle slowly, but the sediment at the bottom is compact and hard to redisperse.

补充
老师讲解重点（根据录音）
Additional
Key teacher points (based on the recording).

3.1 粘度（viscosity）是主要影响沉降的三大因素之一。增加液体的粘度可以减少沉降速度。

Viscosity is one of the three main factors affecting sedimentation. Increasing the viscosity of the liquid phase reduces sedimentation rate.

3.2 颗粒的表面电荷(surface charge)和电性相互作用(electrostatic interactions)有助于颗粒维持悬浮状态，防止聚集。

Surface charge and electrostatic interactions among particles help to keep them suspended and prevent aggregation.

3.3 湿润性(wettability)同样重要，通过加入润湿剂（如表面活性剂）能改善疏水颗粒与水的亲和力。

Wettability is also important. Adding wetting agents (such as surfactants) can improve the affinity of hydrophobic particles for water.

3.4 实际中测定颗粒半径可以用光学显微镜，测量密度需要用比重瓶。

In practice, particle size is measured by optical microscopy, and density is measured with a pycnometer.

3.5 增加悬浮液的粘度通常可以通过加入如淀粉、聚合物等增稠剂实现。

Viscosity of suspensions can usually be increased by adding thickeners such as starch or polymers.

4
悬浮剂的不稳定性和使用建议
4
Instability of suspensions and usage recommendations.

4.1 悬浮剂普遍容易发生沉降，因此每次使用前应摇匀。

Suspensions are generally prone to sedimentation and should be shaken well before each use.

5
补充说明
5
Additional notes.

5.1 颗粒间的相互作用力主要包括范德华力和静电排斥力，这影响沉淀的絮凝或非絮凝状态。

Interparticle forces mainly include Van der Waals force and electrostatic repulsion, affecting flocculated or deflocculated states.

5.2 调控这些相互作用需要根据实际需求改变配方成分。

Regulating these interactions often requires adjusting the formulation components as needed.

悬浮剂的沉降控制与颗粒间相互作用

Sedimentation Control and Particle-Particle Interactions in Suspensions

1
悬浮剂沉降控制 Sedimentation Control of Suspensions

斯托克斯定律用于分析沉降速度，可通过工艺和处方进行调节。 Stokes law is used to analyze sedimentation rate, which can be adjusted by process and formulation.

通过研磨减小颗粒半径r，或添加赋形剂提高液相粘度来降低沉降速度。 Sedimentation rate can be reduced by grinding to decrease particle radius r, or adding excipients to increase liquid viscosity.

增加体系粘度常用的赋形剂包括黏土类或亲水性聚合物，如聚乙烯吡咯烷酮(PVP)、黄原胶、羧甲基纤维素钠(CMC)及缔合聚合物。 Common excipients for increasing viscosity include clays or hydrophilic polymers such as polyvinylpyrrolidone (PVP), xanthan gum, carboxymethylcellulose (CMC), and associative polymers.

体系中颗粒沉降的基本公式
The basic formula for sedimentation is: V = 2r^2(ρ1-ρ2)g / (9η) 其中V为沉降速度，r为颗粒半径，ρ1-ρ2为密度差，g为重力加速度，η为液体粘度。 Where V is the sedimentation rate, r is particle radius, ρ1-ρ2 is the density difference, g is gravitational acceleration, and η is liquid viscosity.

老师强调粘度是影响沉降的重要因素，增粘常采用增稠剂，如淀粉、聚合物等。 The teacher emphasized that viscosity is an important factor affecting sedimentation, with thickeners such as starch or polymers commonly used to increase viscosity.

2
颗粒间相互作用对沉降的影响 Effects of Particle-Particle Interactions on Sedimentation

颗粒间存在相互作用力，决定了沉淀的松散性或致密性。 There are particle-particle interactions that determine whether sediment is loose or compact.

若颗粒表面无电荷，则主要为范德华力（吸引），适用斯托克斯定律。 If particles have no surface charge, van der Waals attractions dominate, and Stokes law applies.

若颗粒带有电荷，则颗粒间存在排斥静电力，形成能障，有助于维持颗粒分散。此时需结合DLVO理论与斯托克斯定律分析。 If particles carry electrostatic charges, repulsive forces exist, forming an energy barrier that helps maintain dispersion. DLVO theory combined with Stokes law is used for analysis in this case.

老师补充
稳定悬浮的关键还包括通过调节颗粒表面电荷防止颗粒聚集。 Teacher's addition: Maintaining a stable suspension also relies on adjusting the surface charge to prevent aggregation.
3
悬浮剂处方设计建议 Formulation Suggestions for Suspensions

通过调节颗粒大小和液体粘度实现沉降控制。 Sedimentation control is achieved by adjusting particle size and liquid viscosity.

可以用光学显微镜测量颗粒尺寸，用比重瓶（picnometer）测定密度。 Particle size can be measured by optical microscopy and density by pycnometer.

实际用药时，悬浮剂需摇匀后使用。 In practice, suspensions should be shaken before use.

4
颗粒絮凝/非絮凝及其调控 Flocculation/Deflocculation and Its Regulation

颗粒间作用可导致絮凝（松散易重悬）或非絮凝（沉淀致密难重悬），配方时需综合考虑。 Particle interactions may result in flocculation (loose, easily redispersed) or deflocculation (tight, difficult to redisperse); formulation design should take this into account.

老师提示，调控颗粒间相互作用主要通过调整系统成分，包括表面活性剂、聚合物等。 Teacher's note: Regulation of particle-particle interactions is mainly achieved by modifying system components, such as surfactants or polymers.

悬浮剂的沉降与絮凝调控Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations: Sedimentation and Flocculation Control in Suspensions

1
沉降控制 Sedimentation Control

沉降控制涉及通过配方措施来调节颗粒在悬浮液中的沉降速率和稳定性。 Sedimentation control refers to formulation strategies to adjust the sedimentation rate and stability of particles in suspensions.

双电层屏障是常用的配方解决思路，可以通过调节颗粒间作用抑制颗粒快速沉降。 The double-layer barrier is a common formulation solution that suppresses rapid sedimentation by adjusting interparticle interactions.

加入絮凝剂（如离子型表面活性剂、电解质或聚电解质），能调节悬浮体系的颗粒电荷状态。 The addition of flocculating agents (such as ionic surfactants, electrolytes, or polyelectrolytes) regulates the surface charge state of suspended particles.

选择与粒子表面带电性相反的絮凝剂，有利于形成松散絮凝结构，易于二次分散。 Selecting flocculating agents with charges opposite to the particles promotes the formation of loose aggregates (flocs) that are easily redispersed.

如图示，聚电解质链缠绕在颗粒表面，形成网状结构，增加体系稳定性。 As illustrated, polyelectrolyte chains wrap around the particle surface forming a network structure, enhancing suspension stability.

2
絮凝控制 Flocculation Control

絮凝控制通过调节颗粒间相互作用，决定悬浮体中沉淀的松散或致密程度。 Flocculation control, by adjusting particle-particle interactions, determines whether sediment in a suspension is loose or compact.

增加絮凝剂浓度，电位（ζ）从负向或正向走向零点，引发颗粒聚集、絮凝，大颗粒沉降加快但易重悬。 Increasing flocculating agent concentration moves the zeta potential (ζ) toward zero, triggering particle aggregation and flocculation—leading to faster sedimentation of larger loose aggregates but easier redispersion.

图中显示絮凝剂浓度与沉降速率的关系
当ζ值接近零时，沉降速率达到最大；ζ离开零点时，沉降速率降低，体系趋于分散稳定。 The diagram shows how flocculating agent concentration relates to sedimentation rate: when ζ approaches zero, sedimentation rate peaks; as ζ deviates from zero, the rate drops and the system becomes more stably dispersed.
3
悬浮体系的宏观/微观评价与调控 Macroscopic/Microscopic Evaluation and Regulation of Suspensions

悬浮剂的稳定性常通过宏观观察（如分层、沉淀体积）和显微镜观察（颗粒分布、结构）进行评估。 Suspension stability is commonly assessed via macroscopic observation (layering, sediment volume) and microscopy (particle distribution, structure).

粘度测量也是评价和调控悬浮体系稳定性的关键参数。 Viscosity measurement is also a key parameter for evaluating and adjusting suspension stability.

4
录音补充重点（老师讲解总结）Additional Key Points from the Recording

老师强调，颗粒表面带电性及其调控对悬浮剂的絮凝和再分散性至关重要，实际配方应针对API和载体电性选择合适的絮凝剂。 The teacher emphasized that the regulation of surface charge on particles is vital for flocculation and redispersion in suspensions; the actual formulation should select flocculating agents according to the charge characteristics of APIs and carriers.

典型的絮凝剂包括一些聚电解质和带电表面活性剂，需注意剂量和体系pH等对ζ电位的调节影响。 Typical flocculating agents include certain polyelectrolytes and charged surfactants; dosage and system pH must be properly controlled to regulate zeta potential.

ζ电位的测量是评价悬浮体系稳定性的重要技术手段，如果ζ电位数值接近零，体系容易发生絮凝。 Zeta potential measurement is an important technique to evaluate suspension stability. When zeta values approach zero, flocculation is likely to occur.

制剂设计应兼顾悬浮剂的物理稳定性和临用时的可重悬性，选用适宜的助悬和絮凝系统。 Formulation design should balance the physical stability of suspensions and their ease of redispersion before use, by choosing appropriate suspending and flocculating systems.

液体与半固体制剂——乳剂的基本类型及热力学稳定性

Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations — Basic Types and Thermodynamic Stability of Emulsions

1
乳剂的类型 Types of Emulsions

乳剂分为油包水（O/W）、水包油（W/O）和多重乳剂（W/O/W）。 Emulsions are classified as oil-in-water (O/W), water-in-oil (W/O), and multiple emulsions (W/O/W).

O/W型乳剂以水为连续相，油为分散相。 In O/W emulsions, water is the continuous phase and oil is the dispersed phase.

W/O型乳剂以油为连续相，水为分散相。 In W/O emulsions, oil is the continuous phase and water is the dispersed phase.

W/O/W型为水包油再包水型，体系存在两个界面。 W/O/W emulsions are water-in-oil-in-water types, featuring two interfaces.

2
乳剂的热力学性质 Thermodynamic Nature of Emulsions

乳剂在热力学上是不稳定体系，总会发生分层或破乳。 Emulsions are thermodynamically unstable systems and will eventually separate or break down.

因此，乳剂的形成与稳定需要乳化剂（表面活性剂）辅助。 The formation and stabilization of emulsions require emulsifiers (surfactants).

即使有乳化剂，乳剂最终仍然不可避免地会失稳。 Even with the addition of emulsifiers, emulsions will inevitably become unstable over time.

3
乳剂的粒径分类与稳定性 Emulsion Size Classification and Stability

普通乳剂的粒径为5-30微米，热力学不稳定。 Conventional emulsions have a particle size of 5-30 micrometers and are thermodynamically unstable.

纳米乳剂的粒径小于1微米，同样热力学不稳定。 Nanoemulsions have a droplet size less than 1 micrometer and are also thermodynamically unstable.

微乳剂的粒径小于等于100纳米，为热力学稳定体系。 Microemulsions have a particle size of no more than 100 nanometers and are thermodynamically stable.

4
录音补充与老师讲解要点 Key Points from Audio and Teacher Explanation

乳剂体系的不稳定是因为界面自由能高，易向分层降低能量状态发展。 Emulsion instability is due to high interfacial free energy, leading to separation as the system seeks to lower its energy.

助乳剂/乳化剂通过降低界面张力，帮助形成和延缓乳剂分离。 Emulsifiers lower interfacial tension, assisting the formation of emulsions and delaying separation.

课堂强调，乳剂的实际应用中，需结合目标制剂所需特性选择合适类型和粒径范围。 In practice, selecting the appropriate emulsion type and droplet size depends on the required formulation characteristics.

微乳剂虽热力学稳定，但对制剂组分比例、温度与pH变化极为敏感。 Microemulsions are thermodynamically stable, but highly sensitive to the composition ratio, temperature, and pH changes.

乳剂的失稳过程Emulsion Destabilisation Processes

1
乳剂的失稳过程 Emulsion Destabilisation Processes

乳剂的失稳包括可逆过程（如乳漂与沉降）和不可逆过程（如奥斯特瓦尔德熟化和聚并）。 Emulsion destabilisation includes reversible processes (such as creaming and sedimentation) and irreversible processes (such as Ostwald ripening and coalescence).

可逆过程
O/W乳剂主要发生乳漂，W/O乳剂主要发生沉降，这两者在摇匀后能再次均匀分散。 Reversible processes: O/W emulsions mainly undergo creaming, W/O emulsions mainly undergo sedimentation; both can be redispersed by shaking.

乳漂和沉降均可通过减小分散相粒径（r）、减小密度差（Δρ）、提高连续相粘度等方式减缓。 Creaming and sedimentation can be slowed by reducing droplet size (r), decreasing density difference (Δρ), and increasing the viscosity of the continuous phase.

2
斯托克斯定律与影响因子 Stokes Law and Influencing Factors
斯托克斯定律用于描述乳漂与沉降速度
Stokes law describes the rate of creaming and sedimentation: V = 2r^2(ρ_d - ρ_c)g / 9η 其中，V为乳漂或沉降速度，r为分散相粒径，ρ_d为分散相密度，ρ_c为连续相密度，g为重力加速度，η为连续相粘度。 Where V is the speed of creaming/sedimentation, r is droplet size, ρ_d is density of dispersed phase, ρ_c is density of continuous phase, g is gravity, and η is continuous phase viscosity.

在实际配方中采用更小的粒径、更小的密度差和更高的粘度以提高乳剂物理稳定性。 In practical formulation, smaller droplet size, lower density difference, and higher viscosity are used to improve physical stability.

老师补充，乳剂静置时，若出现乳漂或沉降，往往通过摇匀可以简单恢复体系均匀性。 Teacher adds, if creaming or sedimentation occurs during storage, the emulsion can often be restored by shaking.

不可逆过程
聚并与奥斯特瓦尔德熟化 Irreversible Processes: Coalescence and Ostwald Ripening

聚并（Coalescence）：分散相粒子融合，最终导致分层，属于不可逆失稳。 Coalescence: droplets fuse, eventually leading to phase separation, which is irreversible.

奥斯特瓦尔德熟化
小液滴逐渐消失而变成大液滴，使体系趋于分层，也是不可逆的。 Ostwald ripening: small droplets dissolve and redeposit on larger ones, also leading to separation and is irreversible.

这两类过程一旦发生，乳剂均不可通过简单摇匀复原原状态。 Once either occurs, the emulsion cannot be restored by simple shaking.

老师强调，聚并和奥斯特瓦尔德熟化是乳剂稳定性的主要挑战，尤其是长期储存中。 The teacher emphasizes that coalescence and Ostwald ripening are key challenges for emulsion stability, especially during long-term storage.

4
实际配方优化措施 Practical Measures for Formulation Optimization

针对聚并问题，可选择性能更优的乳化剂，增强界面膜的弹性和稳定性。 For coalescence, use better emulsifiers to enhance elastic and stable interfacial films.

针对奥斯特瓦尔德熟化，应通过调整工艺和配方，使液滴粒径分布更窄，以延缓熟化。 For Ostwald ripening, narrow droplet size distributions by refining formulation and processing to delay this process.

老师补充，一些特殊应用可采用亲水-疏水性调控或固体粒子稳定的Pickering乳剂进一步增强稳定性。 Teacher further notes that for some applications, stability can be increased using hydrophilic-hydrophobic adjustment or solid particle-stabilized Pickering emulsions.

乳剂
乳化剂的作用与选择Emulsions: Roles and Selection of Emulsifiers
1
乳化剂的作用 Roles of the Emulsifier

乳化剂主要通过降低界面张力，促进乳剂形成。 Emulsifiers mainly promote emulsion formation by lowering interfacial tension.

乳化剂通常为表面活性剂，但不完全局限于此。 Emulsifiers are usually surfactants, but not always.

Bancroft法则
乳化剂更易溶的那一相成为连续相。 Bancroft rule: the phase in which the emulsifier is more soluble constitutes the continuous phase.

乳剂类型（O/W 或 W/O）的判定依赖于乳化剂的亲水/疏水平衡（HLB值）。 The type of emulsion (O/W or W/O) depends on the hydrophilic/lipophilic balance (HLB) of the emulsifier.

乳化剂还有助于乳剂的稳定性，具体表现为： Emulsifiers also help stabilize emulsions by:

增加分子的侧向作用力 Increasing lateral intermolecular interactions

形成能量屏障，防止液滴聚并 Forming an energy barrier to prevent droplet coalescence

提高体系粘度，减缓液滴运动 Increasing viscosity, thus slowing droplet movement

课上补充
通过选择合适的乳化剂体系，可以显著延缓乳剂的破乳、分层等失稳过程。 Additional from the lecture: by selecting suitable emulsifier systems, the processes of emulsion breakdown and phase separation can be significantly delayed.
乳化剂的选择
HLB系统 How to Choose the Emulsifier: HLB System

乳化剂的选择通常基于其亲水/疏水平衡（HLB）数值。 Emulsifier selection is typically based on hydrophilic/lipophilic balance (HLB) values.

Griffin法则
Griffin’s equation: HLB = (质量分数的亲水性基团) / 5 HLB = M_{hydrophilic groups} / 5
Davies法则
Davies’ equation: HLB = 7 + ∑n_h Z_h − ∑n_l Z_l 其中 n_h 为亲水基团数，n_l 为疏水基团数，Z 表示各自的特征值。 Where n_h is the number of hydrophilic groups, n_l is the number of hydrophobic groups, and Z denotes their respective group values.

HLB值可指示适用于O/W型或W/O型乳剂：亲水性高的乳化剂倾向形成O/W型，疏水性高的则形成W/O型。 HLB values indicate suitability: more hydrophilic emulsifiers tend to form O/W emulsions, more lipophilic emulsifiers give W/O types.

课上补充
老师强调，实际选择时应结合药物和赋形剂的理化性质，并通过实验调整HLB组合达到最佳效果。 Additional from the lecture: the teacher emphasized that in practice, emulsifier selection should consider the physicochemical properties of both API and excipients, and optimal HLB is fine-tuned experimentally.
3
其他要点补充 Additional Key Points

乳化剂不仅能改善乳剂稳定性，还能协同提高药物分散度和生物利用度。 Emulsifiers not only improve the stability of emulsions, but can also synergistically increase drug dispersibility and bioavailability.

乳化剂的种类及用量应避免过量导致相反效果，例如结构松散反而促使失稳。 The type and amount of emulsifier should be controlled; excess can lead to destabilisation, e.g. loose interfacial films.

液体与半固体制剂
乳剂中乳化剂HLB系统与选择

Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations: HLB System and Selection of Emulsifiers in Emulsions

1
HLB系统与乳化剂列表 HLB System and Surfactant Table

HLB值（亲水/疏水平衡）用于指导乳化剂的选择。 The HLB value (Hydrophilic/Lipophilic Balance) is used as a guide for emulsifier selection.

常用乳化剂的HLB值差异较大，低HLB（如1-5）乳化剂多为亲油性，高HLB（如10以上）乳化剂多为亲水性。 Common emulsifiers show a wide range of HLB values, with low HLB (e.g., 1–5) indicating lipophilicity, and high HLB (above 10) indicating hydrophilicity.

选择乳化剂时应关注其在水中的行为
如溶解性、是否易于分散等，稳定乳状液需要选择适宜的HLB与行为。 When selecting emulsifiers, consider their behavior in water: solubility, dispersibility, etc. Stable emulsions need suitable HLB values and properties.
举例如下
Some examples are:

蔗糖二硬脂酸酯HLB=7.4，不稳定乳状液。 Sucrose dipalmitate HLB=7.4, unstable milky solution.

吐温61 HLB=9.6，稳定乳状液，常作乳化剂。 Polyoxyethylene stearate 61 HLB=9.6, stable milky solution, commonly used as emulsifier.

十二烷基硫酸钠HLB=40，亲水性极强，易溶于水。 Sodium lauryl sulfate HLB=40, highly hydrophilic, easily soluble in water.

老师补充，实际配方往往组合不同HLB的乳化剂，以达到最佳性能。 Teacher adds, in practice, mixtures of emulsifiers with different HLB values are often used for optimal performance.

2
如何选择乳化剂 How to Choose the Emulsifier

通常建议联合使用两种表面活性剂，一种亲油一种亲水。 It is often recommended to use a combination of two surfactants: one hydrophobic and one hydrophilic.

乳化剂混合物的HLB值具有加和性，可据下式计算： The HLB value of a surfactant mixture is additive, calculated as:

其中f1f1​为1号乳化剂的质量分数或摩尔分数。 Where f1f1​ is the mass or molar fraction of surfactant 1.

通过调配不同比例，能微调整体HLB，满足不同类型乳剂对乳化剂特性的要求。 By adjusting the ratio, the overall HLB can be fine-tuned to meet the requirements of different emulsion types.

3
HLB系统的局限性 Limits of the HLB System

HLB系统未考虑乳化剂对溶液粘度的影响。 The HLB system does not take into account the effect of the surfactant on solution viscosity.

HLB体系主要适用于非离子型表面活性剂，对离子型表面活性剂适用性较差。 The HLB system applies mainly to nonionic surfactants and is less applicable to ionic ones.

课上补充
老师强调，实际配方开发还需结合实验优化，HLB仅作为初选辅助手段。 Additional from lecture: The teacher stressed that formulation optimization requires experimental adjustment; HLB is just a first-step guideline.
液体与半固体制剂
乳剂中乳化剂的界面张力测量与选择

Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations: Interfacial Tension Measurement and Emulsifier Selection in Emulsions

1
选择乳化剂的原理 Principle of Choosing Emulsifiers

选择乳化剂时，需测定不同乳化剂对界面张力（air-water interface或oil-water interface）的降低能力。 When choosing an emulsifier, it is necessary to measure how different emulsifiers reduce interfacial tension (air-water or oil-water interface).

优秀的乳化剂可以更显著地降低界面张力，促进乳剂形成并提高稳定性。 Good emulsifiers can significantly reduce interfacial tension, facilitating the formation and improving the stability of emulsions.

2
乳化剂种类对界面张力的影响 Influence of Different Emulsifiers on Interfacial Tension

亲水性表面活性剂（如Tween-20、Tween-80、Tween-85）能有效降低水-空气界面张力。 Hydrophilic surfactants (e.g., Tween-20, Tween-80, Tween-85) are effective at reducing water–air interfacial tension.

疏水性表面活性剂（如Span-80、Span-85）也同样通过降低界面张力促进乳剂形成，但作用与亲水性略有不同。 Hydrophobic surfactants (e.g., Span-80, Span-85) also lower interfacial tension to facilitate emulsion formation, but their effects differ from hydrophilic ones.

随着乳化剂浓度增加，界面张力逐步下降，直到达到临界胶束浓度（CMC），降低趋势趋于平缓。 As the emulsifier concentration increases, interfacial tension gradually decreases until reaching the critical micellar concentration (CMC), after which the decrease plateaus.

3
界面张力的测定方法与原理 Methods and Principle of Interfacial Tension Measurement

常用的测量方法包括悬滴法（Pendant drop method），可通过下式计算界面张力： Common measurement methods include the pendant drop method, with interfacial tension calculated as: Pin−Pout=rγ(1R1+1R2)Pin​−Pout​=rγ(R1​1​+R2​1​) 其中，PinPin​为液滴内压，PoutPout​为液滴外压，rr为半径，R1,R2R1​,R2​为主曲率半径，γγ为界面张力。 Where PinPin​ is the internal droplet pressure, PoutPout​ is the outside pressure, rr is the radius, R1R1​ and R2R2​ are principal radii of curvature, and γγ is interfacial tension.

课上补充
老师强调界面张力越低，体系越容易乳化，乳滴也越小越稳定。 Additional from lecture: The teacher emphasized that the lower the interfacial tension, the easier the emulsification and the smaller and more stable the droplets.
4
不同乳化剂联合使用的协同效应 Synergistic Effects of Combined Emulsifiers

不同乳化剂联合使用（如Abil EM 90与Synperonic F127）可出现协同效应，显著增强界面张力降低效果。 Combined use of different emulsifiers (e.g., Abil EM 90 with Synperonic F127) can produce synergistic effects, markedly improving the lowering of interfacial tension.

图中两种乳化剂的混合物在临界胶束浓度（cac）处能实现更优降低界面张力的效果。 The mixture of these two emulsifiers achieves better interfacial tension reduction at the critical aggregation concentration (cac).

课上补充
老师举例指出，选择乳化剂时要考虑协同作用，通过实验确定最优配比，兼顾界面张力降低和乳液物理稳定性。 Additional from lecture: The teacher gave examples stressing that emulsifier selection should consider synergy, with optimal ratios determined experimentally to balance interfacial tension reduction and emulsion stability.
5
总结 Summary

乳化剂选择应根据界面张力测定结果，优先选用能大幅降低张力者，并结合协同效应优化配方。 Emulsifiers should be chosen based on interfacial tension measurements, prioritizing those with strong tension-lowering ability and formulating to harness synergy.

悬滴法等实验是评价乳化剂性能和优化乳剂配方的重要手段。 Experimental methods like the pendant drop technique are vital for assessing emulsifier performance and optimizing emulsion formulation.

临界堆积参数与乳化剂选择Critical Packing Parameter (CPP) and Emulsifier Selection

1
临界堆积参数的定义 Definition of Critical Packing Parameter

临界堆积参数（CPP）用于预测表面活性剂分子在水溶液中的自组装结构。 The critical packing parameter (CPP) is used to predict the self-assembled structures of surfactant molecules in aqueous solution.

CPP的计算公式为
The formula for CPP is:

其中 v_h 为疏水链体积，a_0 为头基的平均表面积，l_c 为烷基链长度。 Where v_h is the volume of the hydrophobic chain, a_0 is the mean surface area per headgroup, and l_c is the chain length.

2
CPP值的结构含义 Structural Meaning of CPP Values

当 CPP < 1/3 时，分子倾向形成球状胶束结构。 When CPP < 1/3, molecules tend to form spherical micelle structures.

当 1/3 < CPP < 1/2 时，分子倾向形成柱状胶束或其他中间形态。 When 1/3 < CPP < 1/2, molecules may form cylindrical micelles or other intermediate structures.

当 CPP > 1 时，分子易形成倒构结构，如反相胶束或反相乳液。 When CPP > 1, molecules easily form inverted structures such as reverse micelles or water-in-oil emulsions.

3
HLB与CPP联合预测乳剂类型 Predicting Emulsion Type with HLB and CPP

选择乳化剂时，结合HLB与CPP可更准确判断体系类型。 When selecting emulsifiers, combining HLB and CPP provides a more accurate prediction of the system type.

如果 HLB < 9 且 CPP > 1，则易形成水包油（w/o）型乳剂。 If HLB < 9 and CPP > 1, a water-in-oil (w/o) emulsion is readily formed.

如果 HLB > 9 且 CPP < 1，则易形成油包水（o/w）型乳剂。 If HLB > 9 and CPP < 1, an oil-in-water (o/w) emulsion is readily formed.

课上补充
老师强调CPP是根据表面活性剂分子三维结构得出的，在开发新型乳化剂或复杂体系时非常有参考价值。 Additional from the lecture: The teacher emphasized that CPP is derived from the three-dimensional structure of surfactant molecules and is very valuable when developing novel emulsifiers or complex systems.
4
乳化体系结构类型 Representation of Emulsion System Structures

不同CPP值下表面活性剂自行组织成不同形态，包括锥形、球形胶束、反锥形和反相胶束等。 Depending on the CPP value, surfactants self-assemble into various morphologies including cone, spherical micelle, inverted truncated cone, or inverted micelle.

这些结构影响乳剂的稳定性和分散相类型。 These structures affect the stability of the emulsion and the type of dispersed phase.

5
实际应用说明 Practical Application Notes

通过调整乳化剂的结构参数，可以优化乳剂类型和稳定性。 By adjusting the structural parameters of the emulsifier, the type and stability of emulsion can be optimized.

课上补充
老师建议在实际配方开发中结合HLB法和CPP法，实验筛选最优乳化剂。 Additional from the lecture: The teacher suggested combining HLB and CPP methods alongside experimental screening for optimal emulsifier selection in real-world formulation development.

液体与半固体制剂——乳剂的稳定化方法Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations—Stabilisation Methods of Emulsions

一、乳剂的稳定化Stabilisation of the Emulsions

乳剂的稳定化依赖于表面活性剂分子在油水界面的侧向作用。 The stabilisation of emulsions relies on the lateral interaction between surfactant molecules adsorbed at the oil-water interface.

使用两种HLB值（低和高）的表面活性剂组合，可以形成更具弹性和抵抗力的界面膜。 The use of two surfactants with low and high HLB enables the formation of a more resistant (elastic) interfacial film.

课上补充
老师强调，两种不同HLB的表面活性剂组合有协同增效作用，能更好阻止乳滴聚并，提高体系物理稳定性。 Additional from the lecture: The instructor emphasized that a combination of surfactants with different HLB values produces a synergistic effect, better preventing droplet coalescence and improving physical stability.

二、能垒的形成与带电表面Formation of an Energy Barrier and Charged Surface

通过使用离子型表面活性剂，或者与非离子型表面活性剂的混合，可以在界面形成能垒。 By using an ionic surfactant, or a mixture with a nonionic surfactant, an energy barrier can be formed at the interface.

高电位（高ζ电位）有助于提高乳液的静电稳定性，防止乳滴聚集。 High zeta potential (high ζ) helps increase the electrostatic stability of emulsions and prevent droplet aggregation.

课上补充
老师补充道，ζ电位绝对值越高（如大于±30 mV），乳液体系的排斥作用越强，越不容易发生聚并。 Additional from the lecture: The instructor added that the greater the absolute value of the zeta potential (e.g. above ±30 mV), the stronger the repulsive force, and the less likely droplet coalescence will occur.

三、聚合物的乳剂稳定化Stabilisation of the Emulsions by Polymers

两亲性水溶性非离子型表面活性剂可作为空间位阻屏障，阻止乳滴靠近和融合。 Amphiphilic water-soluble nonionic surfactants can act as a steric barrier, preventing droplets from approaching and fusing.

亲水性聚合物可增加水相（o/w乳剂）粘度，从而提升分散相的稳定性。 Hydrophilic polymers can increase the viscosity of the aqueous phase (o/w emulsions), which promotes the stability of dispersed droplets.

若聚合物具有两亲性，还可直接稳定油水界面。 If the polymer is amphiphilic, it can also directly stabilize the oil-water interface.

课上补充
老师进一步指出，聚合物稳定乳剂不仅通过提升粘度减缓乳滴运动，还可形成吸附于界面上的保护层，进一步增强稳定性。 Additional from the lecture: The instructor further explained that polymers stabilize emulsions not only by increasing viscosity to slow down droplet movement, but also by forming a protective layer adsorbed at the interface, further enhancing stability.

液体与半固体制剂——乳剂的Pickering稳定与制备方法

Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations—Pickering Stabilization and Preparation of Emulsions

1
纳米颗粒（Pickering）乳剂的稳定化 Pickering Emulsion Stabilization by Nanoparticles

Pickering乳剂是通过固体颗粒（黏土、聚合物或金属等）稳定的，可以有无表面活性剂。 Pickering emulsions are stabilized by solid particles (such as clay, polymer or metallic particles), with or without additional surfactant.

固体颗粒在油水界面上的吸附是不可逆的，区别于表面活性剂的可逆吸附。 The adsorption of solid particles at the oil-water interface is irreversible, which is different from the reversible adsorption of surfactants.

这种不可逆吸附能极大提升乳剂的物理稳定性。 This irreversible adsorption greatly enhances the physical stability of the emulsion.

颗粒的润湿性决定能否在界面稳定乳滴
接触角小于90°（优先疏水一侧）形成o/w型乳剂，接触角大于90°则为w/o型乳剂。 The wettability of particles determines their ability to stabilize droplets: contact angle <90° (hydrophilic side) favors o/w emulsion, while >90° gives w/o emulsion.
课上补充
老师特别提到，Pickering乳剂的优势是不依赖大量表面活性剂，适用于需要提高生物相容性或减少刺激性的场景。 Instructor’s addition: The instructor emphasized that Pickering emulsions do not rely on large amounts of surfactant, making them suitable for applications requiring higher biocompatibility or reduced irritation.
2
乳剂的制备方法 Emulsion Preparation Methods

乳剂的制备可根据分散体系类型（如油包水、w/o型或双重乳剂）、分散相粒径和批量规模选择不同工艺。 Emulsion preparation protocols depend on the type of dispersion (o/w, w/o, or double emulsions), droplet size, and batch size.

常用制备设备有
Commonly used preparation devices include:

批量式Ultra-Turrax高速匀浆机 Ultra-Turrax (batch high-speed homogenizer)

连续式转子-定子分散机 Rotor-stator dispersing machines (continuous process)

连续式高压均质机 High-pressure homogenizer (HPH, continuous process)

课上补充
老师指出，不同方法对乳滴粒径分布影响显著，高压均质适合获得粒径更小、更均一的乳滴。 Instructor’s addition: The instructor pointed out that different methods greatly influence droplet size distribution, and high-pressure homogenization is suitable for producing smaller and more uniform droplets.

课上还补充，实际应用中常兼用多种技术，比如先粗混合后高压均质，以优化乳化和稳定性。 The instructor also added that in practice, multiple techniques are often combined, such as coarse mixing followed by high-pressure homogenization, for optimal emulsification and stability.

液体与半固体制剂——乳剂的制备与检测

Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations—Emulsion Preparation and Controls

一、乳剂的制备 Emulsion Preparation

表面活性剂溶解在其更易溶解的那一相中（亦可分散于另一相中）。 Surfactants are dissolved in the phase in which they are more soluble (they can also be dispersed in the other phase).

水相和油相通常需加热（60–65°C），但也有室温制备方法。 Both aqueous and oily phases are usually heated to 60–65°C, but processes at room temperature also exist.

水相需逐步加入油相（或反之），边加边搅拌。 The water phase is progressively poured into the oily phase (or the reverse), with continuous mixing.

所有水或油相加完后，搅拌要持续进行以保证乳液均匀。 Mixing continues after the last drop has been added to ensure uniformity of the emulsion.

课上补充
老师强调加热有助于降低各相黏度，提高混合和乳化效率，同时API热敏时要控制加热温度防止其降解。 Instructor’s addition: The instructor emphasized that heating helps reduce the viscosity of each phase, improving mixing and emulsification efficiency, but if the API is thermosensitive, heating temperature should be controlled to avoid degradation.

二、乳剂的检测与控制 Emulsion Controls

乳剂的各项检测应在室温下进行。 Controls are performed when emulsions are at room temperature.

检查乳液类型
判断o/w（油包水）或w/o（水包油）。 Check the emulsion type: determine if it is oil-in-water (o/w) or water-in-oil (w/o).
粒径检测方法
大于1μm用显微镜或激光衍射，小于1μm用动态光散射（DLS），必要时需稀释样本。 Droplet size measurement: for >1μm, use microscopy or laser diffraction; for <1μm, use dynamic light scattering (DLS). Dilution is necessary.

检测内容还包括：

离心稳定性 Stability to centrifugation

热稳定性（50°C, 30分钟） Stability to temperature (50°C, 30 min)

粘度测试 Viscosity measurement

课上补充
Instructor’s addition:

检查乳液类型可用染色法（如用水溶性色素标记水相，用亲油性色素标记油相），在显微镜下辨别连续相。 The emulsion type can be checked using staining methods (water-soluble dye for aqueous phase, lipophilic dye for oil phase). Observe under a microscope to distinguish the continuous phase.

热稳定性测试时加热有助于判别体系是否会因升温导致乳滴聚并或分层。 Thermal stability test helps identify whether phase separation or droplet coalescence occurs upon heating.

离心测试有助于模拟长期静置效果，加速乳液分层，用于快速评价乳液的物理稳定性。 Centrifugation simulates long-term storage and accelerates phase separation, serving as a rapid method to assess physical stability.

粘度测定一般在制备乳剂前对各自的水相与油相测量，乳液最终粘度受配比和搅拌影响较大。 Viscosity of each phase is commonly measured before emulsion preparation; the final emulsion viscosity depends on formulation proportion and mixing process.

液体与半固体制剂——纳米乳剂与微乳剂 Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations—Nanoemulsions and Microemulsions

1
纳米乳剂 Nanoemulsions

纳米乳剂是“微粒化的乳剂”，属于各向异性胶体体系。 Nanoemulsions are micronized emulsions and are classified as anisotropic colloidal systems.

纳米乳剂在稳定性方面与普通乳剂具有相同的约束条件。 Nanoemulsions have the same stability constraints as conventional emulsions.

纳米乳剂通常采用两步法制备
先用常规搅拌器初步混合，再经过高压均质机处理。 Generally, nanoemulsions are prepared by a two-step process: conventional mixer followed by high pressure homogenizer.
课上补充
老师强调纳米乳剂的典型粒径远小于微米级，能获得透明或半透明的体系，但物理不稳定性（如聚并、奥斯瓦尔德熟化）仍需关注。 Additional explanation from class: The instructor emphasized that nanoemulsions typically have droplet sizes well below one micron, leading to transparent or translucent systems, but physical instabilities such as coalescence and Ostwald ripening remain concerns.
2
微乳剂 Microemulsions

微乳剂为各向同性体系，可自发形成，且热力学稳定。 Microemulsions are isotropic systems with spontaneous formation and are thermodynamically stable.

与纳米乳剂相比，微乳剂具有自发性和热力学稳定性，是其显著区别。 Compared to nanoemulsions, microemulsions are notably spontaneous and thermodynamically stable.

课上补充
老师讲解微乳剂的形成依赖于极低的界面张力，能够在搅拌或轻微外部能量下快速形成透明或半透明体系。 Additional from the instructor: The instructor explained that microemulsion formation depends on extremely low interfacial tension, allowing rapid formation of transparent or translucent systems under stirring or mild external energy.
3
微乳剂的组成与界面张力测定 Components and Interfacial Tension Measurement of Microemulsions
微乳剂的典型组成为
油、水、一个亲水性表面活性剂和一个亲油性共表面活性剂。 The typical components of a microemulsion are oil, water, one hydrophilic surfactant, and one hydrophobic co-surfactant.

选择表面活性剂的目标是使油水界面张力降至0或接近0 mN/m。 Surfactants are chosen so that the interfacial tension between oil and water is reduced to 0 or nearly 0 mN/m.

老师补充，只有当界面张力极低时，微乳剂才能自发均匀且稳定地形成，并表现出热力学稳定性。 Instructor’s addition: Only with very low interfacial tension can microemulsions spontaneously and uniformly form, demonstrating thermodynamic stability.

微乳剂界面张力的测试常用旋转滴张力计，公式：

The interfacial tension of microemulsions is commonly measured by a spinning drop tensiometer, using the formula:

γ
界面张力（单位：N/m），表示油相和水相之间的张力。
ωω
圆筒旋转角速度（单位：rad/s），指装置旋转的速度。
ΔρΔρ
两相之间的密度差（单位：kg/m³），即油和水的密度之差。
dd
油滴的长度（单位：m），旋转后的最大直径。
3232
为常数。
γ
Interfacial tension (N/m), represents the tension between oil and water phases.
ωω
Angular rotation speed of the cylinder (rad/s), i.e., spinning speed of the apparatus.
ΔρΔρ
