Difference in density between the two phases (kg/m³), i.e., the difference between oil and water density.
dd
Length (maximum diameter) of the spinning oil droplet (m).
3232
Constant.
课上补充
老师讲明，通过测量界面张力可验证体系是否能形成微乳剂，只有极低界面张力时体系才能实现自发形成和热力学稳定。 Additional from class: The instructor clarified that measuring interfacial tension validates if a system can form a microemulsion; only extremely low interfacial tension allows for spontaneous formation and thermodynamic stability.

微乳剂的形成与控制Formation and Control of Microemulsions

1
微乳剂的三元相图 Microemulsion Phase Diagram

微乳剂的形成依赖于油、水、表面活性剂三组分的配比，通过三元相图可直观展示各种比例下的不同相态。 The formation of microemulsions depends on the ratio of oil, water, and surfactant; the ternary phase diagram visually shows the different phases at various proportions.

三元相图中标注了微乳剂存在的区域，以及其它相关的分散体系（如乳液、胶束溶液等）。 The ternary diagram marks the existence region of microemulsions and other related disperse systems (like emulsions, micellar solutions, etc.).

课上补充
老师强调，要获得微乳剂，需精确控制三组分比例，并降低界面张力至极低（接近0），否则无法实现自发形成和热力学稳定。 Class addition: The instructor highlighted that to obtain microemulsions, precise control of the three-component ratio and a very low interfacial tension (approaching zero) are necessary; otherwise, spontaneous formation and thermodynamic stability cannot be achieved.
2
微乳剂的控制参数 Control Parameters for Microemulsions
颗粒/液滴粒径
需测定以确保在微乳剂范围（一般<100nm）内。 Particle/droplet size: Must be measured to ensure it is within the microemulsion range (typically <100 nm).
ζ电位
反映体系表面电荷，可评估微乳剂的电稳定性。 Zeta potential: Indicates surface charge and can evaluate the electrostatic stability of the microemulsion.
电导率
有助于区分微乳剂类型（如油包水或水包油）。 Conductivity: Helps distinguish microemulsion type (oil-in-water or water-in-oil).
稳定性
通过离心、加热等手段加速评估其物理稳定性。 Stability: Physically assessed through methods like centrifugation or heating for rapid evaluation.
粘度
测量体系流变特性，微乳剂一般粘度较低且接近水。 Viscosity: Measures rheological properties; microemulsions usually have low viscosity, close to that of water.
热行为
使用差示扫描量热法（DSC）分析体系的热稳定性和相转变。 Thermal behavior: Assessed by Differential Scanning Calorimetry (DSC) to analyze thermal stability and phase transitions.
形态学
用扫描电镜（SEM）观察结构形貌。 Morphology: Structure observed by Scanning Electron Microscopy (SEM).
折射率
用于表征体系的均一性和透明度。 Refractive index: Characterizes uniformity and transparency of the system.
pH值
监测微乳剂的化学稳定性及适应性。 pH value: Monitors chemical stability and suitability of the microemulsion.
课上补充
老师说明，粒径与分布主要通过动态光散射（DLS）测定，电导率反映连续相变化，是区分水包油和油包水的常用手段。热行为与折射率高度相关，热稳定性提升能防止相分离。 Class addition: Instructor explained that particle size and distribution are mainly determined by dynamic light scattering (DLS); conductivity reflects the change of the continuous phase and is a common method to distinguish between oil-in-water and water-in-oil. Thermal behavior is closely related to refractive index, and higher thermal stability helps to prevent phase separation.

液体与半固体制剂——用于皮肤的固体、半固体和液体制剂

Liquid and Semi-solid Formulations—Solid, Semi-solid and Liquid Forms for Dermal Applications

1
用于皮肤的不同剂型分类 Classification of Dermal Dosage Forms

皮肤用剂型分为固体、半固体和液体三类，包括乳膏、软膏、凝胶、膏药及散剂等。 Dermal dosage forms are classified as solid, semi-solid, and liquid forms, including creams, ointments, gels, pastes, and powders.

乳液和乳膏属于乳化相；水包油(w/o)为吸收性软膏，油包水(o/w)为亲水性软膏；还有无水软膏、亲水凝胶、亲油凝胶、膏剂和散剂等形式。 Emulsions and creams are in the emulsified phase; water-in-oil (w/o) are absorbent ointments, oil-in-water (o/w) are hydrophilic ointments; there are also anhydrous ointments, hydrophilic gels, lipophilic gels, pastes and powders.

不同配方包含不同相
固相、亲水相、乳化相、亲油相，通过颜色在图中加以区分。 Different formulations contain different phases: solid phase, hydrophilic phase, emulsified phase, lipophilic phase, marked in color in the diagram.
课上补充
老师强调，各类型产品的物理结构由其组成相决定，实际应用时需依据活性成分和作用部位选择合适剂型。 Class addition: The instructor emphasized that the physical structure of each product type depends on its component phases, and actual application requires selecting the suitable dosage form based on the active ingredient and the intended site of action.
2
半固体制剂的制备工艺 Manufacturing Process of Semi-solid Formulations

半固体制剂如乳膏和软膏的制备过程包括五个主要步骤：固体研磨、溶解、混合（包含温度控制）、调配、稳定性研究。 The manufacturing process of semi-solid formulations like creams and ointments involves five main steps: grinding of solids, dissolution, blending (including temperature control), conditioning, and stability studies.

固体研磨用于减小粒径，促进均匀分布，改善最终制剂的质地和皮肤适用性。 Grinding of solids reduces particle size, promotes uniform dispersion, and improves the texture and suitability of the final product on the skin.

溶解步骤确保有效成分均匀分布在基质中。 The dissolution step ensures that active ingredients are uniformly distributed in the base.

混合阶段常结合加热过程，有助于相溶、彻底分散和最终均匀结构的形成。 The blending stage often involves heating, which helps phases to merge, disperse thoroughly, and form a final homogeneous structure.

调配阶段是将制剂装入最终容器，并根据需要进行调节。 Conditioning involves transferring the formulation into the final container and making necessary adjustments.

稳定性研究用于评估产品在储存期间的物理和化学稳定性。 Stability studies evaluate the physical and chemical stability of the product during storage.

课上补充
老师介绍行星式混合机（Planetary mixer）为常用混合设备，能够实现高效搅拌和均匀混合，适用于高黏度的半固体体系。 Class addition: The instructor introduced the planetary mixer as a commonly used blending apparatus, enabling efficient stirring and homogenous mixing, which is especially suitable for high-viscosity semi-solid systems.

042025-10-28

药片制剂与制造 Tablet formulation and manufacturing

片剂制造工艺（干法制粒）

Process of tablet manufacturing (Dry granulation)

1
药物粉碎 Grinding of drug powders

先对原药进行粉碎，以获得合适的粒径分布。 The drug is first ground to achieve an appropriate particle size distribution.

粉碎有助于后续混合和压片的一致性。 Grinding facilitates uniform mixing and subsequent compression.

2
与辅料混合 Blending with adjuvants

将药物与必要的辅料（如稀释剂、助流剂等）混合。 The drug is mixed with essential adjuvants (such as diluents, glidants, etc.).

混合均匀性对最终含药量分布至关重要。 Uniform blending is critical for final dose uniformity.

3
干法制粒 Dry granulation

采用机械方法将混合粉体制成颗粒（不加水）。 Mechanical methods are used to create granules without adding water.

干法适用于对热或湿敏感的药物。 Dry granulation is suitable for heat- or moisture-sensitive drugs.

4
制粒块的粉碎 Crushing of granules

制粒后需对颗粒进行粉碎，调节粒径分布，方便装填和压片。 After granulation, the granules are crushed to adjust particle size for easier handling and compression.

5
筛分 Screening

通过筛分去除过大或过小的颗粒，获得理想粒度分布。 Screening removes granules that are too large or too small, ensuring an ideal size distribution.

6
加入润滑剂再混合 Addition of lubricant and final blending

润滑剂如硬脂酸镁通常在制粒和筛分后最后加入，防止片剂在模具中粘连。 Lubricants like magnesium stearate are usually added after screening/granulation to prevent sticking during compression.

每次新组分的加入都需再次混合，确保均匀分布。 Every new component addition requires re-blending for even distribution.

7
压片成型 Tabletting

最终混合好的物料被送入压片机，制成片剂。 The final blend is fed into a tablet press to form finished tablets.

8
核心要点总结（结合老师补充） Summary of key points (including instructor’s supplement)

每一步都需关注混合均匀性、流动性和可压性。 At each step, pay attention to blend uniformity, flowability, and compressibility.

粉体的粒径分布越窄，流动性越好，有助于获得均匀片剂。 A narrower particle size distribution improves powder flow and contributes to consistent tablets.

过程设计应根据药物和辅料的物理化学性质选择。 The manufacturing process should be selected based on the physico-chemical properties of the drug and excipients.

加入新辅料时一定要充分混合，确保每一片药含量均一。 When adding new excipients, thorough mixing is essential to ensure dose uniformity in every tablet.

如果混合物流动性、均匀性或可压性不足，不能直接压片，需通过粒化等工艺改进。 If the blend lacks flowability, uniformity or compressibility, direct compression is not possible and granulation steps are necessary.

片剂制造工艺（湿法制粒）

Process of tablet manufacturing (Wet granulation)

1
药物粉碎 Grinding of drug powders

步骤与干法相同，保证基础粒径。 Same as dry granulation, ensuring starting particle size.

2
与辅料混合 Blending with adjuvants

预先与部分辅料混合，保证分布均匀。 Pre-blend with some excipients for uniform distribution.

3
湿法制粒 Wet granulation

加入一定量的液体（如水或结合剂溶液），使粉末黏结成团。 A certain amount of liquid (e.g., water or binder solution) is added so powders can agglomerate.

形成湿颗粒。 Formation of wet granules.

4
干燥 Drying

对湿颗粒进行干燥，去除多余水分，防止储存和压片过程中变质。 Dry granules to remove excess moisture and prevent degradation during storage and compression.

5
颗粒粉碎 Crushing granules

干燥后颗粒可能结块，需要粉碎整粒。 After drying, large agglomerates are crushed for uniform size.

6
筛分 Screening

筛除不合格颗粒，保证颗粒大小适宜，利于压片。 Remove non-conforming particles to ensure proper size for tabletting.

7
与辅助剂混合（再混合） Blending with additional adjuvants

如需加入润滑剂、崩解剂、助流剂等，最后进行混合，确保最终均匀。 Additional excipients (e.g., lubricant, disintegrant, glidant) are added right before compression to achieve final uniformity.

8
压片成型 Tabletting

步骤同上。 Same as above.

工艺选择与辅料分类

Process selection and excipient classification

1
工艺选择依据 Basis for process selection

工艺选择取决于药物和辅料的物理化学性质。 The choice of process depends on physico-chemical properties of drug and excipients.

需要根据药物的多晶型、溶解性、易团聚性等特性进行适配。 Adaptation is required according to properties like polymorphism, solubility, and aggregation tendency.

配方和工艺的适应性是提升制剂成功率的关键。 Adaptability of formulation and process is key for successful tabletting.

2
辅料类型 Types of excipients

体内（in vivo）过程相关：崩解剂，帮助片剂在体内崩解。 For in vivo process: disintegrants, which help the tablet disintegrate in the body.

制造工艺相关
稀释剂、助流剂、黏合剂、润滑剂、包衣剂等，分别用于改善物理性能和制造可行性。 Associated with manufacturing: fillers, glidants, binders, lubricants, coating agents, improving physical properties and processability.
3
预制剂研究 Pre-formulation studies

了解药物和辅料的物理化学性质，包括查阅文献和实际实验。 Acquire knowledge about drug and excipient properties through literature and experimental determination.

预制剂工艺是后续工艺和配方设计的基础。 Pre-formulation studies are the foundation for later formulation and manufacturing design.

混合原理（图片页预告，详细见后续）

Principles of blending (See details in later pages)

药粉混合过程影响含量均匀性、片剂品质。 The powder blending process affects content uniformity and tablet quality.

还需借助先进设备和混合原理实现最佳效果。 Advanced equipment and blending mechanisms are required for optimal results.

说明

老师在录音中特别强调，每次新组分加入都需混合，保证混合物具有良好的流动性、均匀性和可压性，才能顺利进入压片工序，否则需增加制粒等中间步骤。同时，配方和工艺的选择强烈依赖于药物和辅料的物理化学特性，预制剂研究作为配方设计基础非常重要。

The instructor emphasized that each addition of a new component requires mixing to ensure the blend has suitable flowability, uniformity, and compressibility before tabletting; otherwise, additional steps like granulation are necessary. Moreover, the choice of formulation and process must match the physicochemical properties of the drug and excipients, with pre-formulation studies being critical for design.

Blending的影响因素与混合类型 Factors Affecting and Types of Blending

一、影响混合的因素 Factors Affecting Blending

1
粒子形状 Particle shape

规则且表面光滑的球形粒子有利于实现均匀混合，流动性好。 Regular and smooth spherical particles promote uniform blending and good flowability.

不规则或长条形等各向异性粒子的流动性比球形差，易影响混合效果。 Irregular or elongated (anisotropic) particles flow less well than spheres and may reduce blending quality.

老师补充
常用API如对乙酰氨基酚、布洛芬等，大多为各向异性形状。 Teacher's note: Common APIs such as acetaminophen and ibuprofen are mostly anisotropic in shape.
2
粒径分布 Particle size distribution

粒径分布越窄，混合均匀性和流动性越好，有利于获得高品质混合物。 Narrower particle size distributions lead to better blend uniformity and flow properties, resulting in higher quality mixtures.

粒径差异大时，易产生分层、偏析问题。 Large differences in particle size can cause segregation and demixing problems.

老师补充
需尽量调整粒径分布避免混合后颗粒分布分层。 Teacher's note: Particle size distribution should be optimized to prevent post-blending layer segregation.
3
密度 Density

不同密度的粒子混合易导致填充时分层和偏析。 Particles with different densities tend to segregate and form layers during blending and filling.

实际生产中如颗粒和某些赋形剂密度相异时，易在混合、制粒或后序工艺中产生偏析。 In practice, significant density differences between granules and some excipients may cause segregation during blending, granulation, or subsequent processes.

4
湿度 Moisture

含湿量增加会增强粒子间的内聚力，导致颗粒团聚、粘结问题，影响混合均匀性。 Increased moisture raises interparticle cohesion, causing aggregation and sticking, which impairs blend uniformity.

老师补充
制药生产常在空气湿度和温度可控的房间进行，以保持混合与压片质量。 Teacher's note: Pharmaceutical production is usually performed in rooms with controlled humidity and temperature to ensure consistent blending and tablet quality.

二、主要混合类型 Two Main Mix Types

1
无序混合（随机混合） Random mix

粒子在混合过程中呈随机分布。 Particles are randomly distributed during blending.

关键特征是混合各统计区域任意一点出现不同成分粒子的概率均相同。 Key feature: The probability of finding a given component particle at any point is constant across the whole mixture.

适用于粒径、形状、密度相近且无强相互作用力的情况。 Applies when particles have similar size, shape, density, and lack strong interparticle forces.

在随机混合中，理论上混合可逼近理想完全无序（perfect random mix）状态，但实际常受偏析影响。 In theory, random mixing can approach a perfect random state, but in practice is often limited by segregation.

条件
Conditions:

粒子尺寸一致，且无显著的粒子间作用力。 Same particle size, and no significant interparticle forces.

老师补充
在实际生产中极难实现完美随机，通常需对粒径分布、密度和混合能量进行优化。 Teacher's note: Perfect random mixing is rare in practice; optimization of size distribution, density, and blending energy is usually required.
2
有序混合 Ordered mix

一类粒子（通常较小）吸附或“依附”于另一类粒子表面，形成有序结构。 One type of particle (often smaller) adheres to the surface of another, forming an ordered structure.

适用于粒径差异大且存在较强粒子间作用力或吸附作用的体系。 Suitable for systems with large particle size differences and significant particle interactions or adhesion.

有序混合可减少偏析风险，提高含量均匀性，常用于微量组分或功能粒子的定向定位。 Ordered mixing reduces segregation risk and improves content uniformity, and is used for localization of trace actives or functional particles.

老师补充
实际生产中常通过制备有序混合物提高某些成分的分布均一性。 Teacher's note: Ordered mixtures are often deliberately designed in industry to improve distribution of certain components.

三、补充说明 Additional Notes

上述混合类型之间可相互转变，根据系统特点与操作条件可从无序向有序或相反变化。 The above mixing types can interconvert, depending on system properties and process conditions; transitions from random to ordered or vice versa may occur.

偏析问题不只受混合本身影响，还与振动、气流、操作方式密切相关。 Segregation problems are influenced not only by blending itself, but also by vibration, airflow, and handling methods.

小结 Summary

混合效果好坏直接影响片剂含量均匀性及后续工艺，需综合考虑粒子形状、粒径分布、密度和湿度等多因素，并根据物系特性合理设计混合工艺。

The quality of blending directly affects tablet content uniformity and subsequent processing; particle shape, size distribution, density, and moisture all need to be considered, and blending procedures should be tailored to material properties.

Blending中的有序混合与全面混合类型 Blending: Ordered Mix and "Total" Mix Types

有序混合 Ordered mix

1
有序混合的定义 Definition of ordered mix 有序混合是指两种或多种粒子（如蓝色和黑色粒子）在混合过程中均匀分布，但其中存在相互作用力，使系统表现出一定的结构性。 Ordered mix refers to a mixture where two or more types of particles (e.g., blue and black) are evenly distributed, but interactions between components create a certain structural order.
2
有序混合形成的基本条件 Conditions for ordered mix

不同组分的粒子可以粒径不同，通常须存在一定的相互作用力。 The particles of different components may differ in size, and interactions between them are usually required.

这种混合类型要求粒子之间存在某种吸附或结合现象。 This type of mixing requires some kind of adhesion or binding between particles.

3
有序混合的特点 Features of ordered mix

各粒子分布均匀，形成具有顺序的微观结构。 Each particle is evenly distributed to form a microstructure with order.

组分间的相互作用促进有序排列，不再是理想的完全随机。 Interactions between components promote ordered arrangement, not an ideal perfect random state.

4
粒径差异情况下的有序混合 Ordered mix in case of particle size difference

小粒子吸附于大粒子表面，形成吸附层。 Small particles are adsorbed onto the surface of large particles, forming a layer of adhesion.

可表现为部分有序或完全有序结构。 The resulting structure can be partially or perfectly ordered.

5
老师补充说明 Instructor’s additional notes 老师强调实际制药生产中常常利用粒子间的吸附作用设计有序混合，实现微量组分的均匀分布。例如API和乳糖、维生素C和甘露醇，以及对乙酰氨基酚和MCC（微晶纤维素Grade 12）等体系都存在典型的有序混合现象。 The instructor emphasized that in pharmaceutical production, ordered mixing is often achieved by exploiting adhesion between particles, to evenly distribute trace components. Examples include API/lactose, vitamin C/mannitol, and acetaminophen/MCC (microcrystalline cellulose grade 12) systems, which all exhibit typical ordered mix.

“全面混合”（Total mix, Staniforth, 1981） "Total" Mix (Staniforth, 1981)

1
全面混合的定义 Definition of total mix 全面混合指既非完全随机、也非完全可交互的混合状态，是文献中描述复杂混合行为的经典模型。 "Total" mix refers to a state that is neither completely random nor fully interactive—a classic model to describe complex mixing behaviors in the literature.
2
全面混合的结构特点 Structural characteristics of total mix

通常包括赋形剂载体粒子、药物载体粒子、附着赋形剂粒子和附着药物粒子。 Usually includes excipient carrier particles, drug carrier particles, adherent excipient particles, and adherent drug particles.

完美全面当量有序混合中，各组分以理想化比例实现有序吸附和排列；而实际全面混合中，过程更复杂，吸附和分布存在不均。 In a perfect total stoichiometric (ordered) mix, each component is adsorbed and arranged ideally; in a real total mix, the process is more complex with less regular adsorption and distribution.

3
混合类型总结 Summary of mix types

有序混合与全面混合均属于非理想混合类型，关键在于粒子间相互作用和结构化分布。 Both ordered mix and total mix are non-ideal, with key features being inter-particle interaction and structured distribution.

实际混合度往往介于完美总混合和完全无序混合之间。 Actual blending usually lies between perfect total blending and totally random mixing.

4
老师补充说明 Instructor’s additional note 老师指出，“全面混合”尤其用于解释多种赋形剂与多种API在复杂结构中的分布，这在实际固体制剂设计中很有指导意义。 The instructor noted that "total mix" is used especially to explain the distribution of multiple excipients and APIs in complex structures, which is highly instructive for solid dosage design.

随机混合的混合机理（补充） Mixing Mechanisms of Random Mixing (Supplement)

1
对流混合 Convection mixing

粒子以块状在混合机内部运动。 Particles move in blocks within the blender.

适合需要大批量处理的固体混合。 Suitable for blending solids in large batches.

2
扩散混合 Diffusion mixing

粒子在粉体床内呈现个体且随机的微观运动，彼此交换位置。 Individual particles move randomly within the powder bed, causing exchanges of position.

有助于实现较高均匀性。 Helps achieve high uniformity.

3
剪切混合 Shear mixing

粉体内部出现分层，不同层之间相互滑动。 Layers form within the powder mass and slide against each other.

常见于高速混合或带有搅拌桨叶的混合设备中。 Common in high-speed mixers or paddle-equipped mixers.

4
老师补充说明 Instructor’s additional note 实际生产中，三种机理经常同时存在，对不同形式粉体需要选择最合适的混合方式及设备。 In practice, these three mechanisms often coexist; selecting the most appropriate mixing method and equipment depends on the powder’s properties.

总结 Summary

有序混合和全面混合反映了药物制剂中不同粒子间的相互作用和结构特性，而了解这些混合类型及机理，有助于优化药物固体混合物的均匀性与性能。

Ordered and "total" mixing reflect the inter-particle interactions and structural characteristics in pharmaceutical blends. Understanding these types and mechanisms is key to optimizing uniformity and functionality of solid pharmaceutical mixtures.

Blending中的有序混合与随机混合分离原因

Ordered Mixing and Causes of Segregation in Random Mixes in Blending

一、混合机理——有序混合

Mixing Mechanisms – Ordered Mixing

1
机械混合 Mechanical mixing 机械混合是指通过机械运动使粉床分裂与重新结合。 Mechanical mixing refers to the division and reuniting of a powder bed by mechanical movements.
2
吸附作用 Adhesion 吸附作用是指细小颗粒附着在较大颗粒表面。 Adhesion means fine particles adhere to the surface of larger particles.
3
包衣作用 Coating 包衣作用需要引入辅助剂，后续需将其去除。 Coating requires an additive that must be removed later.
4
老师补充 Instructor’s addition 有序混合本质在于组件间的相互作用，如粒子间结合/吸附，导致体系出现部分结构性分布，而不仅是物理混合。 The essence of ordered mixing lies in interactions such as binding or adhesion between particles, leading to a partially structured distribution rather than just physical mixing.

二、去混合与分层

De-mixing and Segregation

1
去混合（反混合过程） De-mixing (reverse process of mixing) 去混合指的是混合体系内各成分重新分离，是混合的逆过程。 De-mixing means the components of the mixed system separate again, which is the reverse process of mixing.
2
粒子的物理化学亲和性 Physico-chemical affinities between particles 某些粒子之间存在物理或化学亲和力，阻碍体系的完全均质化。 There may be physical or chemical affinities between certain particles, preventing complete homogenization of the system.
3
偏好性运动 Preferential movement 部分粒子在搅拌容器内倾向运动到特定位置，形成组分偏移。 Some particles tend to move to a particular part of the blender, resulting in preferential distribution of components.
4
分层混合 Segregated mix 分层混合即体系中不同组分微观上分离，不再均匀分布。 A segregated mix means that different components are separated on a microscopic scale and are no longer uniformly distributed.
5
老师补充 Instructor’s addition 实验中经常由于混合时间设置不当导致分层，尤其是API与辅料性质（如粒径、密度、亲和性）差异大时。 In practice, improper mixing times often lead to segregation, especially when APIs and excipients have differences in properties like particle size, density, or affinity.

三、随机混合中分层原因

Causes of segregation in random mixes

1
轨迹差异 Trajectories

若粒子有不同的粒径 dd 和密度 ρρ，在重力等作用下其运动轨迹不同。 If particles have different diameters dd and densities ρρ, their trajectories vary under gravity.

依据Stokes定律，颗粒在介质中下落距离 xx 可表示为： According to Stokes’ law, the falling distance xx in a medium is

其中 v0v0​ 为速度，ρsρs​ 为密度，dd 为粒径，ηη 为粘度。 Where v0v0​ is speed, ρsρs​ density, dd diameter, ηη viscosity.

2
细粉渗透 Fine powder percolation

多孔粉床中，在晃动或振动下，孔隙可被细小颗粒填充，细粉聚集于底部。 In a porous powder bed, during vibration or shaking, interparticle vacancies are filled by small particles, which are found at the bottom of the powder bed.

3
细粉冒析 Fine powder elutriation

气流或倒料过程中，细粉易随气流上升到粉床顶部（如筒仓卸料时）。 During air flow or product transfer (e.g., silo emptying), fine particles migrate upward to the top of the powder bed.

4
老师补充 Instructor’s addition 分层现象不仅由物理属性差异导致，还与空气流动、外界振动、湿度等环境因素相关。 Segregation is not only caused by differences in physical properties, but also influenced by airflow, external vibration, and ambient humidity.

四、总结

Summary

有序混合强调粒子间相互作用与结构性分布，而分层现象主要由于粒径、密度差异及环境扰动导致。 Ordered mixing emphasizes particle interactions and structured distribution, while segregation mainly results from differences in particle size, density, and environmental disturbances.

药物生产中避免分层与去混合，关键在于合理控制粒子属性、混合能量及现场环境。 In pharmaceutical production, avoiding segregation and demixing requires rational control of particle properties, mixing energy, and environmental conditions.

Blender选择与扩散式混合机Blender Choice and Blenders by Diffusion

Blender选择的基本原则Basic Principles of Blender Choice

搅拌机选择
往往需要折中妥协 Blender choice: Compromises are often necessary
搅拌流程一般遵循以下思路
The typical blending workflow follows these steps:

首先根据配方特性（流动性、分层倾向、组分结构），判断采用哪类通用混合机。 First, select a generic mixer depending on formulation characteristics such as flowability, segregation risk, and degree of structure among components.

对于易流动且易分层的体系，特别关注混合均匀性和分层尺度的控制。 For free-flowing and segregating systems, focus on blend uniformity and control of segregation scale.

对于黏性较大、结构紧密的体系，需满足特定工艺和操作要求。 For cohesive and structured systems, specific process and operational requirements must be met.

合理选择终端混合设备，以平衡工艺、质量和产品特性。 Final mixer selection should balance process demands, product quality, and mixture properties.

混合均匀性控制与质量评价Control of Homogeneity and Quality Evaluation

1
降低分层尺度 Reduction of segregation scale

通过调整混合设备和参数，将分层限制在较小尺度，保证成品局部也能均匀。 By tuning mixer type and settings, keep segregation at a minimal scale for local blend uniformity.

2
降低分层强度 Reduction of segregation intensity

降低体系宏观及微观的不均匀性，确保API和辅料充分混合。 Decreases both macro- and microscopic inhomogeneity, ensuring good mixing of API and excipients.

质量测定
取样 Quality measurement: sampling

通过不同部位的取样来确认混合均匀性，是实际生产中重要的质量评价手段。 Sampling at various positions in the powder bed is a key method for verifying mix quality in practice.

搅拌机选择的工艺要求及评判要素Process Requirements and Evaluation Criteria for Blender Choice

工艺相关因素
Process-related factors:

产量及灵活性Production capacity and flexibility 设备需要满足不同的生产批量要求，并适应工艺变化。 The equipment must handle varying batch sizes and changing process needs.

批次完整性Batch integrity 确保整个批次混合物在成分分布上的一致性。 Ensure component distribution consistency throughout the batch.

污染控制Contamination controlled 对易污染或需无菌工艺尤为关键。 Crucial for sensitive or sterile manufacturing environments.

对产品的敏感性（易碎性、磨蚀性、爆炸危险性、热敏性）Sensitivity of the product (friability, abrasiveness, explosiveness, thermal sensitivity) 混合设备和参数需根据物料特性选择，避免API或辅料损伤或变性。 Mixer and parameters should be chosen based on material traits to avoid product damage or alteration.

清洁性（便捷性、频率、专属性）Cleaning (ease, frequency, specificity) 便于清洁、减少交叉污染风险，适用于频繁更换配方的场合。 Easy cleaning reduces cross-contamination, suitable for frequent formulation changes.

老师补充
预配方研究 Instructor’s addition: Pre-formulation program

设备选择需依赖于API和辅料的物理化学特性，预配方阶段需获取相关信息并实验确定。 Blender choice relies on the physico-chemical properties of API and excipients, which must be tested and confirmed during pre-formulation.

扩散式混合机型及特征Blenders by Diffusion and Their Characteristics

1
常见扩散式混合机 Common diffusion-type blenders:

V型混合机V-shaped blender 通过“V”形滚筒缓慢回转，促进组分间扩散混合。 Slow rotation of a “V” drum promotes diffusive mixing between components.

立方体混合机Cubic blender 以类似原理，通过立方体容器的旋转实现混合。 Rotating cube vessel achieves mixing similarly.

双锥混合机Double cone blender 双锥体结构，自转过程中形成均匀混合。 Blends by rotating double-cone chamber for even distribution.

Turbula®混合机Turbula® blender 三维复杂轨迹运动，提升扩散效率和混合均匀度。 Complex 3D motion enhances diffusion efficiency and blend accuracy.

2
工艺参数 Process parameters:
转速范围Speed
5–30 rpm
工作容积Volume
< 5 m³
功率Power
< 1 kW·m⁻³
3
优点与适用范围 Advantages and applications:

适用于非黏性、易流动粉体的均匀混合，高度依赖物料自身流动和扩散。 Well-suited for free-flowing, non-cohesive powders; relies on particle movement and diffusion.

设备易于装卸及清洗，无运动部件插入粉床，适合需频繁清理的工艺环境。 Easy loading/unloading and cleaning; no internal shafts minimize contamination risks—ideal for processes needing frequent cleaning.

批量较小，适合小批筛选或制药开发阶段。 Generally used for small batches—ideal for R&D or early production phases.

老师补充
限制与注意事项 Instructor’s notes: Limitations and considerations

不适合强聚集或粒径、密度差异大的体系；如存在强相互作用难以获得理想混合。 Not suitable for highly cohesive powders or systems with large particle size/density differences; strong interactions may hinder quality mixing.

小结Summary

混合设备的选择需平衡配方、工艺和质量要求，扩散式混合机因操作简单、易清洁、适用范围广，在药物固体制剂开发中获得广泛应用，但需考虑其对于物料性质的要求。

Blender selection should balance formulation, process, and quality needs. Diffusion blenders are widely used for solid dosage R&D due to their operational simplicity and easy cleaning, but their effectiveness depends strongly on material properties.

混合设备与混合过程控制Blending Equipment and Blending Process Control

一、扩散式混合机Blenders by diffusion

易于装卸。 Easy to load and unload.

易于清洗。 Easy to clean.

维护需求少。 Minimal maintenance.

不会导致颗粒粒径减小。 No reduction of particle size.

不适用于黏性（团聚）粉体。 Non-adapted to cohesive powders.

当粉体粒径或密度不同则易发生分层。 Segregation likely if size/density of powders are different.

补充（结合课堂讲解）：

Supplement (based on lecture):

扩散式混合机没有桨叶或轴插入粉床，混合主要靠容器平缓运动（如V型、双锥等），利用粉体自身自由流动和扩散实现。 Diffusion blenders do not have paddles or shafts inside the powder bed; blending relies on the gentle movement of the whole container (e.g., V-shape, double cone), resulting from self-flow and diffusion of the powder.

适用于流动性好、颗粒大小和密度相近、没有强粘附力的物料混合；如遇团聚性粉体或粒径密度差异大则混合困难，并易发生分层，有取样误差风险。 Suitable for free-flowing, non-cohesive powders with similar particle size and density; when used with cohesive powders or those with big differences in particle size/density, mixing is incomplete and prone to segregation, leading to sampling errors.

二、对流式混合机Blenders by convection

可实现配料的顺序加入。 Sequential addition of products possible.

可进行热量传递（如加热/冷却）。 Thermal transfer possible (e.g., heating/cooling).

可用于制粒。 Can be used for granulation.

清洗困难。 Difficult cleaning.

取样困难。 Difficult sampling.

罐体不可灵活更换。 Non-transposable tank.

补充（结合课堂讲解与图示）：

Supplement (based on lecture and images):

常见设备包括犁刀混合机（Coulter mixer，ploughshare）、螺带混合机（helicoidal ribbon blender）、高速剪切混合机、涡轮混合机（Hosokawa、Bohle等）。 Typical equipment includes ploughshare (Coulter) mixers, helicoidal ribbon blenders, high-shear mixers, and turbine mixers (e.g., Hosokawa, Bohle).

工作原理为轴和桨叶直接搅拌粉床，通过剪切力和整体运动实现强混合，适用于黏性或需强混合的粉体，以及粒径/密度差异大的体系。 The principle is direct mixing using shafts and paddles, relying on shearing and bulk flow to achieve strong blending; suitable for cohesive powders or those with significant size/density differences.

搅拌强度与粉体运动方式取决于轴设计和转速(推荐范围5~300 rpm)。 Mixing intensity and powder flow depend on shaft design and speed (recommended 5–300 rpm).

设备难以彻底清洗，且多为固定罐体，取样难度大，适用于大批量、强制混合需求。 Equipment is hard to clean and often has a fixed tank; sampling is also difficult. Suitable for large-batch, intensive mixing needs.

三、混合过程控制Blending process control

取样监控（多种thief probe类型）。 Sampling (various types of thief probes).

主要用于取不同位置的粉样，评价混合均匀性。 Mainly used to collect powder samples from different positions to assess homogeneity.

在线光学法。 Online optical methods.

光纤与近红外光谱。 Optical fibers and Near-infrared spectroscopy.

成像技术。 Imaging technology.

补充（结合老师讲解）：

Supplement (according to lecture):

通过不同位置、多点取样，分析API或辅料分布情况，是药厂实际中常用的质量控制方法。 By sampling at multiple positions in the blend and analyzing the distribution of API or excipients, this process is commonly used for practical quality control in pharmaceutical production.

亦可采用光学纤维和近红外等方法进行自动、快速在线分析，提高混合一致性的监控效率。 Optical fiber and near-infrared techniques may be used for automatic, fast online analysis, improving blending consistency monitoring efficiency.

052025-10-28（第 2 讲）

颗粒制备Granulation

一、颗粒制备定义 Definition of Granulation

颗粒制备是通过不同粉末的集结形成颗粒的过程。 Granulation is the formation of granules by agglomeration of different powders.

颗粒制备的主要目的是将粉末颗粒尺寸增大，改善流动性和可压缩性，以便后续制备固体制剂。 The main goal of granulation is to increase powder particle size, improving flowability and compressibility for further solid dosage processing.

二、干法制粒 Dry Granulation (干法颗粒制备)

干法制粒是通过压实并粉碎和筛分的方式来增大粉体颗粒尺寸。 Dry granulation increases powder size by compaction, followed by grinding and sizing.

适用于水分敏感、不适合湿法的药物体系。 It is suitable for moisture-sensitive systems, or when wet methods are unsuitable.

三、湿法制粒 Wet Granulation (湿法颗粒制备)

湿法制粒是利用粘合剂溶液，在混合及碰撞作用下使粉体颗粒团聚增大。 Wet granulation enlarges particle size by mixing and collision, in the presence of a binder solution.

能改善颗粒均匀性和压缩性，常用于直接压片性较差的粉体。 It improves uniformity and compressibility, often used when powders lack direct compressibility.

四、挤出-球形化 Extrusion-Spheronisation

该工艺是一种常见的制备球状颗粒（丸剂）的方法，具体过程为首先挤出圆柱状颗粒，再通过球形化使其成为球状。 This is a common method for preparing spherical granules (pellets): extrusion produces cylindrical particles, and spheronisation transforms them into spheres.

五、干法制粒具体流程 Dry Granulation: Process Summary

粉体与干性黏合剂及润滑剂混合。 The powder is mixed with a dry binder and lubricant.

通过压片机制成大块片，或用压实辊进行致密化。 The mixture is processed into large tablets in tablet presses, or densified using a compacting mill.

将大块压实物粉碎、筛分获得所需粒径颗粒。 The compacted material is ground and sieved for desired granule size.

六、干法制粒辅料作用 Dry Granulation: Role of Adjuvants

黏合剂
提升颗粒的致密性和内聚力，使颗粒不易破碎。 Binders: Improve the cohesion and density of granules, making them less fragile.
润滑剂
防止颗粒粘附于制粒设备壁面，同时提升物料在下游装置中的流动性。 Lubricants: Prevent sticking to granulation device walls and improve powder flow in hoppers and the feed system.

七、压实辊制粒要点 Compacting Mill Key Points

滑动区供料
粉体速度小于辊筒周边速度，有利于均匀进料。 Feeding in the "sliding" zone: powder speed is less than the peripheral speed of the rollers, promoting even feeding.
进料区到最大压力区
在辊轴对齐的情况下达到最大压力，物料速度等于辊周速。 Compaction from the intake area to maximum pressure (aligned with roller axes), where material speed equals peripheral roller speed.

八、压实效率影响因素 Factors Affecting Compaction Efficiency

滑动区供料速度依赖于粉体流动性。 Feed speed at the sliding zone depends on powder flow properties.

排气（脱气）效率依赖于物料可压缩性。 Efficiency of deaeration depends on compressibility.

辊筒尺寸、表面、速度及间隙决定可压实性。 Dimensions, surface and speed of rollers, and gap between them, all depend on the compactibility of the material.

九、课堂补充与老师强调 Lecture & Teacher's Emphasis

在进行干法制粒时，最后加入润滑剂，并要再次彻底混合，这是保证润滑分布均匀、后续制片顺利关键操作。 At the end of dry granulation, lubricant must be added last and thoroughly blended to ensure even distribution for smooth downstream tableting.

辅料添加顺序和混合时间优化对于产品最终质量至关重要。 The order and blending time of excipient addition are vital for final product quality.

粉体的可压性与流动性直接影响压实效果和颗粒质量。 Powder compressibility and flow directly determine the compaction outcome and granule quality.

必须根据药物和辅料物理化学特性选择适宜的制粒工艺。 The granulation method must be chosen according to the physicochemical properties of the drug and excipients.

十、小结 Summary

颗粒制备是固体制剂工程中的核心步骤，不同方法适用于不同体系和工艺需求。 Granulation is a core step in solid dosage engineering; the choice of method depends on system and process requirements.

过程控制与辅料选择密切相关，直接影响颗粒质量和后续压片效率。 Process control and excipient selection are closely related and directly impact granule quality and compression efficiency.

颗粒制备 Granulation

问预处方研究内容 What's in the pre-formulation program?
答

粒子形状 Particle shape

热行为 Thermal behaviour

流动性 Flowing properties

可压缩性 Compressibility

可压实性 Compactability

补充说明：

Supplementary Notes:

预处方研究是固体制剂工艺开发的首要步骤，主要评估原料药（API）和辅料的物理化学性质，以预测其在实际生产中的表现与适应工艺。 Pre-formulation study is the first step in solid dosage process development, mainly assessing the physicochemical properties of API and excipients to predict their behavior and process suitability.

粒子的形状直接影响粉体的流动性，均匀的球形有利于流动和混合，针状或不规则粒形则容易引起结块和分层。 Particle shape directly affects powder flowability; spherical and uniform particles promote better flow and mixing, while needle-like or irregular particles tend to clump or segregate.

热行为包括熔点、玻璃转变温度等，影响可控性和稳定性。 Thermal behavior includes melting point and glass transition temperature, impacting controllability and stability.

可压缩性（Compressibility）指粉末在受力下密度升高的性能，对后续压片至关重要。 Compressibility describes the increase in density of powders under pressure, which is critical for tableting.

可压实性（Compactability）为粉末在外力作用下形成机械强度高压实体（如片剂）的能力。 Compactability is the ability of powders to form compacts with high mechanical strength under pressure (such as tablets).

湿法制粒 Wet granulation

湿法制粒是通过加入纯液体（如水或酒精）或黏合剂溶液使粉末团聚生成颗粒的过程。 Wet granulation is the process where powder particles agglomerate into granules by adding either a pure liquid (e.g., water, alcohol) or a binder solution.

第一种情况：先将干燥黏合剂加入粉末混合物，后续喷雾加水来激活黏合剂。 In the first case, a dry binder is added to the powder mix, and later water is sprayed onto the powder bed to activate the binder.

第二种情况：先将黏合剂溶解/分散于液体，制成溶液后用作喷雾。 In the latter case, the binder is dissolved or dispersed in the liquid to prepare a binder solution for spraying.

补充说明：

Supplementary Notes:

湿法制粒可显著改善粉末的流动性与可压性，有利于制备均匀、强度高、片重差异小的颗粒。 Wet granulation greatly improves powder flow and compressibility, making it suitable for producing uniform, strong granules with low tablet weight variation.

液体的种类和黏合剂的用量需根据具体API和辅料的理化性质优化。 The type of liquid and amount of binder must be optimized according to the API and excipients' physicochemical properties.

常用黏合剂如淀粉、羟丙基甲基纤维素（HPMC）、聚乙烯吡咯烷酮（PVP）等。 Common binders include starch, hydroxypropyl methylcellulose (HPMC), and polyvinylpyrrolidone (PVP).

润湿与颗粒生长机制 Mechanism of wetting and granule growth

颗粒润湿与生长通常分为几个连续阶段： The process of wetting and granule growth is typically divided into several stages:

成核（Nucleation）：液桥形成，液含量低，主要为微小颗粒聚集。 Nucleation: Formation of liquid bridges between particles at low liquid contents; small particle clusters form.

稳定生长期（Steady growth）：液桥增厚，桥连增多，颗粒逐步长大，混合持续。 Steady growth: Thickening of bridges, more inter-particle connections, granules grow gradually, mixing continues.

团块生长期（Crumb growth）：液体含量进一步提高，颗粒间完全连接，终止制粒。 Crumb growth: With further liquid increase, granules connect more completely, signaling the end of granulation.

浆状过润湿（Slurry overwetting）：液体过量，颗粒失去结构，形成浆液状态，需避免。 Slurry (overwetting): Excess liquid leads to loss of granule structure, forming slurry; this should be avoided.

对应的液体饱和度指标（S*）： The corresponding saturation rate (S*):

成核阶段： S* < 0.25 Nucleation: S* < 0.25

稳定生长期： S* ≥ 0.25 Steady growth: S* ≥ 0.25

团块生长期： S* = 0.9 ~ 1.0 Crumb growth: S* = 0.9 ~ 1.0

浆状过润湿： S* ≥ 1 Slurry (overwetting): S* ≥ 1

补充说明：

Supplementary Notes:

S*（液体/颗粒间隙容积比）越高，越易出现浆状团聚、影响后续造粒和干燥。 The higher the S* (ratio of liquid to inter-particle void volume), the greater the risk of slurry formation, which negatively affects granulation and drying.

实际工艺中需通过调控加液量、喷雾速度及时监控状态，避免进入浆状区。 In practice, liquid addition and spraying speed must be carefully controlled to avoid reaching the slurry regime.

制粒过程中液桥的形成与消失直接决定颗粒的结构和强度，也是影响片剂质量的关键。 The formation and breakup of liquid bridges during granulation directly determine granule structure and strength, which are critical for final tablet quality.

颗粒制备中的润湿机制与过程控制 Mechanism of Wetting and Process Control in Granulation

一、润湿与颗粒生长机制 Mechanism of Wetting and Granule Growth

在湿法制粒过程中，随着加液量的增加，颗粒生长经历一系列阶段。 During wet granulation, as more liquid is added, granule growth proceeds through several stages.

各阶段顺序如下： The sequence of stages is as follows:

1
成核阶段（Nucleation, Pendular）：此阶段主要为颗粒之间形成液桥，颗粒开始集结，S* < 0.25。 Nucleation (Pendular): In this stage, liquid bridges begin to form between particles and agglomeration initiates, S* < 0.25.
2
稳定生长期（Steady growth, Funicular）：液桥增厚，单颗粒逐渐增大并稳定形成，润湿接近结束，混合充分，S* ≥ 0.25。 Steady growth (Funicular): Liquid bridges thicken, individual granules grow and stabilize, wetting ends and thorough mixing occurs, S* ≥ 0.25.
3
大块增长阶段（Crumb growth, Capillary）：颗粒团聚形成更大块状结构，混合结束，是理想颗粒终点，S* = 0.9–1.0。 Crumb growth (Capillary): Granules agglomerate into larger chunks, mixing ends, this is the ideal granule endpoint, S* = 0.9–1.0.
4
过润湿阶段（Slurry overwetting, Droplet）：液体加入过多，体系变为浆状，颗粒结构被破坏，S* ≥ 1。 Slurry overwetting (Droplet): Excess liquid causes the system to become slurry-like, granule structure is lost, S* ≥ 1.

各阶段所对应的液体饱和度S*（Saturation Rate）： The saturation rate S* corresponding to each stage:

成核（Nucleation）：S* < 0.25 Nucleation: S* < 0.25

稳定生长期（Steady growth）：S* ≥ 0.25 Steady growth: S* ≥ 0.25

大块增长（Crumb growth）：S* = 0.9–1.0 Crumb growth: S* = 0.9–1.0

过润湿（Slurry）：S* ≥ 1 Slurry: S* ≥ 1

老师强调理想制粒应终止于Steady growth到Crumb growth之间，避免体系进入Slurry阶段，否则无法获得合格的固体制剂颗粒。 The instructor emphasized that granulation should ideally stop between the Steady growth and Crumb growth stages, avoiding transition into the Slurry state, as this would prevent obtaining qualified granules for solid dosage forms.

二、通过功率记录实现粉体润湿过程的连续控制 Continuous Control of Powder Wetting by Power Recording

将混合过程中机器消耗的功率（P）用于监测加液与颗粒变换过程，为过程控制提供依据。 The power (P) consumed by the mixer during the process is used to monitor the liquid addition and granule transformation, providing a basis for process control.

公式如下： The formula is:

其中（where）：

PP：设备消耗功率（瓦特，W）

NN：搅拌装置转速（每秒，s⁻¹）

CC：作用在轴上的扭矩（牛·米，N·m）

功率-加液曲线表现为： The curve of power versus liquid addition:

随加液量增加，功率先升高，颗粒逐步增大，后在加液过量或颗粒团聚时，功率急剧波动甚至下降。 With increasing liquid addition, power first rises as granules increase in size; if liquid is excessive or granules agglomerate, power fluctuates sharply or even drops.

可通过监测功率曲线的变化识别颗粒生长各阶段，辅助判断加液终点和避免过润湿。 By monitoring changes in the power curve, one can identify different granule growth stages and determine the endpoint of liquid addition to avoid overwetting.

老师补充说明：熟练的操作员会结合肉眼观察（颗粒手感、外观）与设备功率曲线判断润湿过程进展，并进行调整，实现颗粒质量的最优化。 The teacher added that skilled operators use both visual inspection (granule feel, appearance) and the power curve of the mixer to judge process progress and make adjustments, achieving optimal granule quality.

三、总结 Summary

颗粒制备中的润湿与颗粒生长经历成核、增长、大块及浆状四个阶段，S*（液体饱和度）是关键指标。 Wetting and granule growth in granulation proceed through nucleation, steady growth, crumb growth, and slurry stages; the key indicator is the saturation rate S*.

通过实时监测混合设备的功率变化，可以精准控制加液过程，实现颗粒质量精确可控。 By real-time monitoring of mixing equipment power, the liquid addition process can be precisely controlled, ensuring optimal granule quality.

实践中合理终点在Steady growth与Crumb growth之间，且操作员需善用感官与仪表协同监控与调整。 In practice, the optimal endpoint is between Steady growth and Crumb growth; operators must adeptly use both sensory evaluation and instrumentation for monitoring and adjustment.

颗粒制备中的湿法过程建模Granulation | Modeling of Wet Process Formation

一、湿法过程建模总览 Overview of Modeling Wet Granulation Process

湿法过程的本质在于解释液体与固体颗粒作用下的颗粒结合与生长机制。 The essence of the wet process is to explain the mechanism of particle binding and growth under the interaction of liquid and solid particles.

主要有三类模型用于描述湿法造粒过程
机械模型（Rumpf）、理化模型（Fowkes, Owens, van Oss）、碰撞模型（Ennis）。 There are three main models to describe the wet granulation process: mechanistic model (Rumpf), physico-chemical model (Fowkes, Owens, van Oss), and collision model (Ennis).

二、机械模型（Rumpf）Mechanistic Model (Rumpf)

机械模型用于预测颗粒团聚体中由于液桥作用产生的内聚力。 The mechanistic model is used to predict the cohesion in agglomerates arising from liquid bridge forces.

目标是找到使颗粒团聚体内聚力最大的条件。 The objective is to find the conditions that maximize agglomerate cohesion.

采用粘结断裂概念，即需要克服作用在颗粒界面S上的内聚力。 The concept of cohesive rupture is used, referring to overcoming interparticle forces developed on the surface S.

机械模型关键公式为（σC 近似内聚应力）： The key formula for the mechanistic model (σC as cohesive stress):

其中
Where:

σC = 内聚应力 σC = cohesive stress

S* = 饱和度 S* = saturation rate

ε = 颗粒间孔隙率 ε = intergranular porosity

γ = 液体表面张力 γ = surface tension

θ = 接触角 θ = contact angle

d = 颗粒平均直径 d = particle diameter

S的意义
S = 1/3 时接近吊桥状态，S* = 1 时为毛细状态，最佳粘结状态出现在毛细/吊桥状态之比为3时。 The significance of S*: S* = 1/3 is close to pendular state; S* = 1 is capillary state; best cohesion occurs when the capillary/pendular state ratio is 3.
机械模型结论
Conclusions from mechanistic model:

高的S提升团聚体内聚性。 High S enhances agglomerate cohesiveness.

选择更小的颗粒（小d）、更高表面张力的液体（高γ）、更佳润湿性（cosθ接近1）均有利于制得强度更高的颗粒。 Smaller particles (small d), liquids with higher surface tension (high γ), and better wetting (cosθ close to 1) all favor stronger granules.

三、理化模型（Fowkes, Owens, van Oss）Physico-Chemical Model (Fowkes, Owens, van Oss)

理化模型用于预测液体在颗粒表面铺展特性，优化润湿液。 The physico-chemical model predicts the spreading characteristics of the liquid on particles, optimizing the wetting liquid.

目标是在颗粒表面获得最佳润湿铺展条件，使每个液滴能够最大限度润湿颗粒。 The objective is to achieve the optimal wetting and spreading conditions on particle surfaces so that each droplet wets the particles as fully as possible.

若液滴直径Dp大于颗粒直径Dd，则单颗粒包裹；若Dp小于Dd，则液滴附着于多个颗粒/表面。 If droplet diameter Dp > particle diameter Dd, a single particle is covered; if Dp < Dd, the droplet adheres to multiple particles/surfaces.

老师强调实际操作时喷雾液滴应小于颗粒尺寸以充分润湿而非局部包裹。 The instructor emphasized that, in practice, the sprayed droplet size should be smaller than the particle size for thorough wetting rather than local coverage.

四、碰撞模型（Ennis）Collision Model (Ennis)

碰撞模型考虑颗粒间的动力学相互作用（如弹跳、黏附），对混合剪切速率和制粒效率优化有帮助。 The collision model considers the dynamics of interparticle interactions (e.g., rebound, adhesion) and helps optimize mixing shear speed and granulation efficiency.

需要调节搅拌/剪切速率以减少弹跳、增加黏附，实现更稳定的颗粒结合。 Adjustment of agitation/shear rate is needed to reduce rebound and increase adhesion, thus achieving more stable granule binding.

五、要点总结 Key Points Summary

制粒中的内聚力由液桥（机械）、润湿铺展（理化）和颗粒碰撞动力学（碰撞）共同决定。 The cohesion in granulation is collectively determined by liquid bridges (mechanical), wetting/spreading (physico-chemical), and particle collision dynamics (collision).

选用强润湿（高γ、低θ）、小颗粒和合理液体量（合适S*）可获得最佳颗粒结构。 The use of strong wetting (high γ, low θ), small particles, and proper liquid content (optimal S*) leads to optimal granule structure.

操作中应根据颗粒特性、工艺类型和设备参数选择建模思路和调控方式。 In practice, modeling strategy and control should be chosen according to particle properties, process type, and equipment parameters.

颗粒制备中的湿法过程——理化模型Granulation | Wet Process: The Physico-chemical Model

一、黏附功与内聚功 Work of Adhesion and Work of Cohesion

黏附功定义为从固-液界面分离单位面积所需的可逆功。 Work of adhesion is defined as the reversible work required to separate one unit area from the solid-liquid interface.

公式为
The formula is:

其中 γSγS​ 是固体表面张力，γLγL​ 是液体表面张力，γSLγSL​ 是固-液界面张力。 Where γSγS​ is the solid surface tension, γLγL​ is the liquid surface tension, and γSLγSL​ is the solid-liquid interfacial tension.

液体的内聚功是通过喷雾形成液滴时所需克服的功。 The work of cohesion of a liquid is the work needed to produce droplets by spraying.

公式为
The formula is:

当 Wadh>WcohWadh​>Wcoh​ 时，液体能在固体表面铺展。 If Wadh>WcohWadh​>Wcoh​, the liquid will spread over the surface of the solid.

二、铺展系数与铺展条件 Spreading Coefficient and Spreading Condition

铺展系数λλ定义为黏附功与内聚功之差
The spreading coefficient λλ is defined as the difference between work of adhesion and work of cohesion:

只有当 λ>0λ>0 时，液体才能在固体表面铺展，达到良好润湿。 Only if λ>0λ>0, the liquid will spread on the solid surface and achieve good wetting.

现实中往往只有液体的表面张力γLγL​容易直接获得。 In reality, only the liquid’s surface tension γLγL​ is usually easy to measure directly.

四、铺展系数的实际测定与应用 Example: Determination and Application of Spreading Coefficient

实际中用Fowkes或Owens-Wendt模型，可由实验数据（如表面张力分量）计算颗粒-液体系的铺展系数。 In practice, using the Fowkes or Owens-Wendt model, the spreading coefficient can be calculated from experimental data (e.g., surface tension components).

以对乙酰氨基酚（Acetaminophen）颗粒化为例，不同黏合剂（HPMC, Acacia, PVP, 淀粉）与片剂性能相关的数据： Using acetaminophen granulation as an example, surface tension parameters and spreading coefficients for various binders (HPMC, Acacia, PVP, starch) relate to tablet properties:

铺展系数为正（如HPMC、Acacia）时，润湿效果好；为负（如淀粉）时润湿较差。 Positive spreading coefficient (e.g., HPMC, Acacia) indicates good wetting; negative (e.g., starch) means poor wetting.

铺展性好的体系通常成品片剂强度高、崩解和磨损率低。 Systems with better spreading typically yield higher tablet strength and lower friability/capping index.

五、铺展性与片剂性能的关系 Correlation of Spreading with Tablet Performance

铺展系数高的体系，片剂强度高，脆碎度低，脱帽现象少。 A higher spreading coefficient gives tablets with higher strength, lower friability, and less capping.

实验结果显示HPMC和Acacia等铺展性好的黏合剂，有助于获得更优片剂质量。 Experimental data confirm that binders like HPMC and Acacia with high spreading coefficients help achieve better tablet quality.

六、小结 Summary

理化模型通过分析黏附功、内聚功和铺展系数，指导选择合适的黏合剂与工艺，以提升颗粒和片剂性能。 The physico-chemical model uses work of adhesion, cohesion, and spreading coefficient analysis to guide binder/ process selection for improved granule and tablet properties.

铺展系数是判断液体润湿颗粒效果的关键指标，直接影响最终片剂质量。 The spreading coefficient is a key criterion for evaluating how well a liquid wets powder, and it directly impacts final tablet quality.

颗粒制备中的湿法过程——碰撞模型（Ennis）

Granulation | Wet Process: The Collision Model (Ennis)

一、碰撞模型基础

Basic Principles of the Collision Model

碰撞模型用于描述两个颗粒在运动时相遇、相互作用后的行为，包括弹跳、团聚、永久结合和破碎等多种结果。

The collision model describes the behavior when two particles move and interact upon collision, including rebound, coalescence, permanent binding, and fragmentation.

在颗粒碰撞时，若速度为 u₀，且黏结条件合适，可能形成团聚并进一步变为永久结合或发生破碎；否则则发生弹跳分离。

Upon particle collision at velocity u₀, if binding conditions are favorable, agglomeration occurs, possibly leading to permanent binding or fragmentation; otherwise, particles rebound and separate.

二、黏结与分离机制

Mechanism of Coalescence and Separation

当两个颗粒表面的润湿层相互接触并穿透时，会产生粘性阻力，使颗粒运动速度迅速降低。

If the wetting layers of two particles come into contact and interpenetrate, viscous resistance arises and the particle velocity drops rapidly.

颗粒间的相对运动受到液体层黏度引起的粘性摩擦力制动。

Particle motion is braked by viscous friction stemming from the viscosity of the liquid layer.

三、粘性制动与Stokes数

Viscous Braking and Stokes Number

Stokes粘性制动力公式：

其中：

μ
液体黏度 (viscosity of the liquid)
R
颗粒半径 (particle radius)
U
颗粒运动速度 (particle speed)
x
颗粒中心到对称平面的距离 (distance to plane of symmetry)

颗粒速度变化关系：

或可简化为：

其中 h 为液体层厚度，St 为Stokes数。

四、Stokes数定义

Definition of the Stokes Number

其中 m 为颗粒质量，U₀ 为初速度。

Where m is the mass of the particle and U₀ is the initial velocity.

五、碰撞结果与影响因素

Collision Outcomes and Influencing Factors

当 St 较低时，颗粒速度 U 可降至 0，颗粒易发生团聚（coalescence）。 When St is low, particle velocity U can reach 0, making coalescence more likely.

当 St 过高时，颗粒惯性大于阻力，易发生弹跳（rebound），不易团聚。 When St is high, inertia dominates and particles rebound instead of agglomerating.

六、影响因素与工艺优化

Factors Influencing Collision and Process Optimization

St 与颗粒大小 (m / R²) 成正比。颗粒越大或密度越高，St 越大，不利于团聚。 St is proportional to particle size (m / R²); larger or denser particles yield higher St, reducing agglomeration.

液体黏度 μ 越大，St 越小，制粒效率提高。 Higher liquid viscosity reduces St, improving granulation efficiency.

碰撞模型的“金规则”：

St 要足够低，使得 U = 0 能在颗粒碰撞时实现团聚。

The “golden rule” of the collision model: St must be low enough for U = 0 at collision, ensuring coalescence.

最佳制粒条件
选用小颗粒和高黏度液体。

Best conditions for granulation: use small particles and a high-viscosity liquid.

七、老师补充

Teacher's Supplement

老师强调，实际制粒过程中应优先选用细小颗粒和高黏度液体，避免使用过大颗粒或低黏度液体，否则会导致颗粒弹跳过多，难以稳定结合。

In practice, fine particles and high-viscosity liquids should be preferred; otherwise, excessive rebound will hinder stable granule formation.

八、小结

Summary

碰撞模型揭示了颗粒在润湿、运动与碰撞过程中的多种结果，并指出粒径与液体黏度对团聚有决定性影响。

The collision model explains various outcomes of particle wetting, motion, and collision, emphasizing the decisive roles of particle size and liquid viscosity.

工艺设计应优化颗粒大小与液体性质，严格控制 St，以实现高质量颗粒制备。

Process design must tailor particle size and liquid properties, precisely controlling St to achieve high-quality granulation.

颗粒制备工艺Granulation Process

一、配方组成与典型材料 Formulation Composition and Typical Materials

配方中液体常用水或稀醇。 Liquids commonly used in formulations are water or diluted alcohols.

典型黏合剂包括糖类、淀粉、阿拉伯胶（acacia）、纤维素及其衍生物如羟丙基甲基纤维素（HPMC）、聚乙烯吡咯烷酮（PVP）。 Typical binders include sugars, starch, gum (acacia), cellulose and its derivatives such as hydroxypropyl methylcellulose (HPMC), polyvinylpyrrolidone (PVP).

老师补充
如遇API不耐水，可选用乙醇进行造粒。 Teacher’s addition: If the API is sensitive to water, ethanol can be chosen for granulation.

二、湿法制粒工艺流程 Wet Granulation Process Flow

步骤一
先将所有干粉末充分混合。 Step 1: Mix all dry powders thoroughly.
步骤二
逐渐加入适量润湿液，实现均匀润湿。 Step 2: Gradually add an appropriate amount of wetting liquid to achieve uniform wetting.

老师强调，一定要逐步加液、避免过湿，否则只能报废重做。 The teacher emphasized that liquid must be added progressively to avoid overwetting, or the batch must be discarded and restarted.

常用低剪切搅拌器完成预混与湿润（如Coulter搅拌器）。 Low-shear mixers such as the Coulter mixer are commonly used for pre-mixing and moistening.

湿校准环节采用高剪切制粒机或流化床制粒机，强力促使团聚和均匀润湿。 The wet calibration (granulation) stage uses high-shear granulators or fluidized bed granulators to strongly promote agglomeration and uniform wetting.

可用高剪切搅拌制粒机（如Diosna），配备刮刀和三叶涡轮；流程包括搅拌、加黏合剂、团聚与成粒。 High-shear mixer-granulators (such as Diosna) equipped with chopper and three-blade turbine are used; the process includes mixing, binder addition, agglomeration, and granulation.

另一方案为双螺杆制粒机，分段实现喂料、润湿、混合、均化、排料等。 Alternatively, a twin-screw granulator divides the process into zones for feeding, wetting, mixing, homogenization, and discharge.

三、一体化设备与常见工艺模块 All-in-One Equipment and Common Process Modules

可选单机完成制湿、成粒、干燥一体化（如MGD一体机）。 Integrated machines, such as an MGD mixer-granulator-dryer, can accomplish wetting, granulation, and drying in one unit.

传统工艺也可以分步使用独立设备
先制湿混合，再转移至制粒或烘干设备。 The traditional process can use separate equipment for each stage: wet mixing first, then granulation and drying.

四、干燥、粉碎与筛分 Drying, Grinding, and Screening

湿制粒完成后，需将颗粒干燥至残余水分<5%，防止片剂制备时性能波动。 After wet granulation, granules must be dried to a residual moisture below 5% to ensure stable tablet quality.

干燥后，须进行颗粒粉碎与筛分，保证颗粒粒径分布适中，有利于后续压片与均一性。 After drying, granules should be ground and sieved to ensure appropriate particle size distribution, which benefits tableting and uniformity.

五、工艺监控与关键点 Process Monitoring and Critical Points

特别关注颗粒尺寸与残余水分的控制，作为工艺关键质量指标。 Special attention should be paid to controlling granule size and residual moisture as critical quality attributes.

老师补充
干燥和加液都需关注温度、加液速度与搅拌均匀度，常用取样检测与电功率（扭矩）监控设备协助过程监控。 Teacher’s addition: Both drying and liquid addition require attention to temperature, rate of addition, and uniformity of mixing. Sampling and monitoring of electrical power (torque) are often used for process control.

六、设备说明及其作用 Equipment Description and Functions

低剪切搅拌器（如Coulter）：适用于均匀预混及初步润湿。 Low-shear mixer (e.g., Coulter): For uniform premixing and initial moistening.

摇摆制粒机（带金属筛）：将湿混物强制通过筛网形成颗粒。 Oscillating granulator (with metallic sieve): Forces wet mass through screen to form granules.

高剪切制粒机
快速实现充分混合、团聚和均匀颗粒化。 High-shear granulator: Achieves rapid and thorough mixing, agglomeration, and uniform granulation.
双螺杆制粒机
流程分区精确控制各阶段操作，适合连续化生产。 Twin-screw granulator: Precisely controls each stage by zone, suitable for continuous production.

七、小结 Summary

湿法制粒工艺核心在于干粉混合、润湿（分步加液）、成粒和干燥，设备选择影响效率与颗粒质量。 The core of wet granulation is dry blending, wetting (with stepwise liquid addition), granulation, and drying. Equipment selection greatly affects both efficiency and granule quality.

优秀工艺设计要求精细的过程控制和对“加液—搅拌—干燥”每一步的关注。 Excellent process design requires precise process control and close attention to every step from liquid addition and mixing to drying.

合理选择制粒液、黏合剂与设备，可获得满足后续制剂要求的理想颗粒。 Proper selection of granulating liquid, binders, and equipment yields granules suitable for subsequent formulation requirements.

流化床制粒的工作原理与喷雾方式 Granulation—Fluidized Bed Principle & Spray Modes

一、流化床制粒原理 Fluidized Bed Granulator Principle

1
流化床制粒工艺可实现混合、制粒和干燥的“一体化”操作。 The fluidized bed granulator integrates mixing, granulation, and drying into a one-pot operation.
流化床制粒过程主要包括四个阶段
The process of fluidized bed granulation involves four main stages:
阶段1
气流增加，静止床层上的颗粒被提升 Stage 1: As air flow increases, particles in the static powder bed are lifted.
阶段2
床层膨胀 Stage 2: The powder bed expands.
阶段3
床层破碎并变为流动状态 Stage 3: The bed breaks up and becomes mobile.
阶段4
轻微气流增加会显著提升床层体积 Stage 4: A slight increase in air flow significantly increases the bed volume.
床层压力变化与空气流速的关系
Relationship between bed pressure drop and air speed:

静止床（Bed at rest）：气流低，颗粒未流化。 At low airflow, particles remain static.

流化床（Fluidized bed）：气流足够，颗粒被悬浮、流化。 At sufficient airflow, the particles are suspended and fluidized.

颗粒夹带（Particle entraining）：气流过大，颗粒被吹走。 At excessive airflow, particles are blown out of the bed.

公式：压差 = 入口压力 - 出口压力 Formula: Pressure drop = Inlet pressure - Outlet pressure

二、流化床喷雾方式及特点 Spray Modes in Fluidized Bed & Their Characteristics

1
顶喷（Top spray）——粉床上方喷液 Top Spray—Spraying Above the Powder Bed

喷雾方向与气流相反 The spray direction is against the airflow.

控制湿度较难，易出现过湿或不足湿润 Moisture regulation is challenging, leading to overwetting or insufficient wetting.

容易使筒壁被加湿，需在粉末润湿前先烘干 This tends to humidify the vessel walls, requiring drying before powder wetting.

适用范围为 5-1500 kg Applicable scale: 5–1500 kg.

2
底喷（Bottom spray）——与气流同向喷液 Bottom Spray—Co-current Spraying

喷雾方向与空气同向，液体分布更均匀 Liquid is sprayed in the same direction as the airflow, leading to better distribution.

能实现更精准的液体分布 Ensures more uniform wettability of the particles.

3
切线喷雾（Tangential spray）——在床内切线方向喷液 Tangential Spray—Spraying Inside the Bed

喷雾在粉床内部，以切线方向进行 Liquid is sprayed tangentially within the powder bed.

存在润湿不均匀的风险 This method poses a risk of inhomogeneous wetting.

适用范围为 1-300 kg Applicable scale: 1–300 kg.

三、老师补充要点 Teacher’s Additional Points

1
液体黏合剂的喷洒应确保均匀，避免过多黏在器壁或仅局部润湿。 The binding solution should be sprayed evenly to avoid sticking to vessel walls or only wetting certain areas.
2
为保证良好流动和混合，建议制粒时容器装料不超过一半，留出足够气流空间，便于颗粒运动。 To ensure proper fluidization and mixing, it's recommended to fill the vessel less than half full, leaving air space for particle movement.
3
空气流动和压力常控制在约0.5到1 bar，既要悬浮颗粒，又不能让颗粒被吹出。 Airflow and pressure are typically controlled between 0.5 and 1 bar to suspend particles without blowing them away.
4
不同混合模式（例如有/无搅拌轴，低/高剪切力）和混合时间都会直接影响颗粒的大小和均一性。 Different mixing modes (e.g., with or without shaft, low or high shear) and mixing times directly affect granule size and uniformity.
5
粉体的颗粒尺寸、形状、湿度、组分差异等物理参数都会对制粒过程产生显著影响。 Physical parameters such as particle size, shape, moisture content, and compositional differences significantly influence the granulation process.
6
在设备运行过程中，应避免“粉末面包块”现象，确保润湿和混合均匀。 During operation, avoid powder "clumping" and ensure even wetting and mixing.

挤出-制丸过程在制粒中的应用 Extrusion-Spheronisation in Granulation

1
工艺简介 Process Overview

挤出-制丸是一种用于制备球形颗粒的两步法工艺。 Extrusion-spheronisation is a two-step process for preparing spherical particles.

首先通过挤出器得到圆柱形湿颗粒。 Firstly, cylindrical wet particles are produced using an extruder.

然后利用制丸机进一步将圆柱颗粒揉圆成球形。 Then, the cylindrical particles are rounded into spheres in a spheroniser.

2
挤出过程 Extrusion Process

挤出过程通过螺杆或模具将湿润的混合物推出，形成一致长度的圆柱颗粒。 The extrusion process uses screws or dies to push the wet mixture out, forming cylindrical granules of uniform length.

挤出器内的模孔尺寸影响最终颗粒的初始形状和大小。 The size of the die holes in the extruder determines the starting shape and size of the granules.

产生的颗粒含有一定水分，具有一定的可塑性，便于后续造圆。 The produced granules retain some moisture, giving them plasticity for subsequent spheronisation.

3
制丸过程 Spheronisation Process

制丸环节中，圆柱形颗粒在高速旋转的制丸盘内不断翻滚、抛射。 In the spheronisation step, the cylindrical granules tumble and roll within the high-speed rotating plate.

颗粒在摩擦作用下两端逐渐变圆，最后形成近似完美的球形。 Under friction, the ends of the granules gradually round off, ultimately forming nearly perfect spheres.

工艺流程图和实物图清晰显示了挤出到制丸的全过程。 The flow diagram and real photographs clearly illustrate the full process from extrusion to spheronisation.

4
球化过程中的形态变化 Morphological Changes during Spheronisation

球化过程可分为多个阶段，呈现明显的时间依赖性。 The spheronisation process occurs in stages and is strongly time-dependent.

初始阶段，颗粒呈短圆柱状（cylindrical particles）。 At the initial stage, the granules are short cylinders (cylindrical particles).

球化5秒后，颗粒略变短变圆，但仍较多棱角。 After 5 seconds, granules become shorter and more rounded, but with evident edges.

15秒后，大多数颗粒两端明显圆滑，不规则度降低。 After 15 seconds, most granules have significantly rounded ends and are less irregular.

120秒后，颗粒基本变为规则的近似球形。 After 120 seconds, granules have essentially become regular spheres.

5
工艺控制参数与影响因素 Process Controls and Influencing Factors

挤出阶段需精确控制水分和黏合剂含量，保证颗粒可塑性但不粘连。 The extrusion step requires strict control of moisture and binder content to ensure granule plasticity without sticking.

模孔直径和挤出速度影响初始颗粒尺寸。 Die diameter and extrusion speed affect the initial size of the granules.

制丸时间、筒体转速和负载决定最终球形度与粒径均匀性。 Spheronisation time, drum speed, and load determine the roundness and size uniformity of the final spheres.

粉体组分的粒径分布、形状和水分含量，会影响挤出和制丸效果。 Particle size distribution, shape, and moisture of the powder influence both extrusion and spheronisation outcomes.

若颗粒太湿，则容易粘连，球化不理想；若太干，则易碎裂、难以成球。 If the granules are too wet, they tend to stick and the process is sub-optimal; if too dry, they fracture and are hard to spheronise.

6
应用 Application

挤出-制丸技术广泛用于制备口服控释颗粒、微丸和植入缓释剂。 Extrusion-spheronisation is widely used to produce oral controlled-release pellets, microgranules, and implantable sustained-release agents.

该方法能提供粒径均一、球形度高、流动性和分散性好的颗粒。 This technique produces particles with uniform size, high sphericity, and good flow/dispersibility.
