压片相关定义与原理Definitions and Principles of Tabletting

1
基本定义 Definitions
压缩
Compression 通过外力作用，减少粉末床层体积以获得有内聚力的系统。 Operation aimed at reducing the volume of a powder bed under stress to obtain a cohesive system.
可压性
Compressibility 粉末在应力作用下体积减小的能力。 Ability of a powder to decrease in volume under the effect of stress.

可成形性（成片性）：Compactability 粉末在应力下获得内聚力的能力。 Ability of a powder to acquire cohesion under stress.

2
压片原理 Principle of Tabletting

压片是通过加力，使粉末层体积减少，最终形成结构致密的片剂。 Tabletting reduces the thickness of a powder bed by applied force, resulting in a dense tablet.

加压过程中，产品厚度随外加力的增加而减少，曲线呈下降趋势。 During compression, product thickness decreases as the applied force increases, as shown by the downward trend on the curve.

图示过程分为以下三步
The process, as illustrated, involves three main steps:
1
粉末松散堆积 Loose powder aggregation
2
粉末被压紧并逐渐靠近 Powders are compressed and come together
3
形成致密片剂 A dense tablet is formed
3
单冲压片机的工作过程 Single Tablet Press Functioning

单冲压片机的压片流程包括四个基本操作步骤： The single-tablet press operation consists of four basic steps:

1
送料 Feeding 粉末由加料器进入模具腔。 Powder is fed into the die cavity from the feeder.
2
刮平 Leveling 上冲头下降，将多余粉末刮平，确保填充量一致。 The upper punch descends to level off excess powder, ensuring consistent fill.
3
压缩 Compression 上下冲头共同加力，将粉末压实为片。 Both punches apply force to compact the powder into a tablet.
4
出片 Ejection 下冲头上升，将片剂顶出模具腔。 The lower punch rises to eject the tablet from the die.

单冲压片机动力消耗较高，但产能相对较低（500-1000片/小时）。 The single tablet press has high power consumption but relatively low throughput (500-1000 tablets per hour).

4
课堂补充要点 Additional Lecture Highlights

压缩操作的核心目标是通过机械力减少粉末体积并实现片剂内聚。 The core aim of the compression process is to reduce powder bed volume by mechanical force, achieving cohesive tablets.

可压性体现为粉末体积在应力作用下的缩减能力；可成形性则反映在压实后体系的牢固度和片剂性能上。 Compressibility refers to the reduction in powder volume under stress; compactability concerns the firmness and performance of the tablet formed.

在实际压片过程中，施加压缩力的设备（如单冲压片机）通常包括上冲头、下冲头和模具腔，通过协同作用完成压片。 In practical tablet production, the compression force is applied with devices like single tablet presses using an upper punch, lower punch, and die cavity.

压片过程中“厚度-压力”曲线可用于表征粉末体系的压缩行为和片剂性能。 The “thickness-force” curve during tabletting reflects the powder compaction behavior and resulting tablet properties.

压片机制及设备Tabletting Mechanisms and Devices

一、单冲压片机的工作过程 Single Tablet Press Functioning

1
送料 Feeding 加料器将粉末送入模具腔，粉末堆积于下冲头之上。 The feeder loads the powder into the die cavity, where it accumulates on top of the lower punch.
2
刮平 Leveling 上冲头下降，将粉末刮平，确保腔体填充均匀。 The upper punch descends to level off the powder, ensuring the cavity is evenly filled.
3
压片 Compression 上下冲头同时加压，使粉末致密压实成片剂。 Both punches apply pressure simultaneously, compacting the powder into a dense tablet.
4
出片 Ejection 下冲头上升，将成型片剂从模具腔中推出。 The lower punch rises to eject the formed tablet from the die cavity.
5
性能特点 Characteristics 单冲压片机功耗较高，产能较低（500-1000片/小时）。 The single tablet press consumes high power but has low throughput (500-1000 tablets per hour).

二、旋转式压片机及工具 Rotary Tablet Press and Tools

1
结构与原理 Structure and Principle 多个模具腔和冲头均匀分布在圆盘状转台上；模具腔围绕中心轴旋转，在固定轨迹内依次完成送料、压片和出片。 Multiple die cavities and punches are evenly distributed around a circular turret; the die table rotates around the central axis, sequentially completing feeding, compression, and ejection along a fixed path.
2
模具腔 Die and Punches 每个模具腔配有一对上下冲头，控制压片压力和片剂形状；模具和冲头的设计和排列高度标准化。 Each die cavity is paired with an upper and lower punch, controlling compression force and tablet shape; dies and punches are highly standardized.
3
设备结构 Equipment Structure 旋转压片机的核心部件包括模具盘（die table）、转台（turret）、上下冲头系统，以及送料机构。 The core components of a rotary press include the die table, turret, upper and lower punch systems, and feeding mechanism.

三、旋转压片机的运行过程 Rotary Press Functioning

1
送料 Feeding 粉末通过送料器进入每一个旋转至送料位置的模具腔。 Powder is delivered by the feeder into each die cavity as it rotates into the filling position.
2
预压缩与主压缩 Precompression & Main Compression 上下冲头依次施加预压和主压，实现高效致密压实。 Upper and lower punches sequentially apply precompression and main compression, achieving efficient tablet densification.
3
出片&重量控制 Ejection & Weight Adjustment 片剂被下冲头顶出模具腔，同时通过调节下冲头高度实现片剂重量控制。 Tablets are ejected by the lower punch, with tablet weight regulated by adjusting the lower punch position.
4
高产能特点 High Throughput 旋转压片机能连续高效地产生大量片剂，远超单冲设备产能。 Rotary presses can continuously and efficiently produce a large number of tablets, greatly exceeding the capacity of single punch presses.

四、压片过程中的应力与应变 Stress and Strain in Tabletting

1.理论关系 Theoretical Relation 应力（σ）等于杨氏模量　乘以变形（应变，ε）： Stress (σσ) equals Young’s modulus (EE) times deformation (strain, εε): σ = E · ε
答案 E
2
不同材料的压缩行为 Compression Behavior of Different Materials

易碎材料碎裂，压缩后不可逆。 Fragmentary materials fracture and are irreversibly broken after compression.

弹性材料变形后能恢复原状。 Elastic materials deform under force but return to original shape after compression.

塑性材料受力后持续变形，停压后保持新形状。 Plastic materials deform under force and retain the shape after pressure is removed.

五、课堂补充讲解 Additional Lecture Insights

1
**下冲头初始高度的设定决定片剂剂量，而上下冲头的行程决定片剂密度和强度。 The initial setting of the lower punch determines the tablet dose, while the movement (travel) of the punches defines tablet density and strength.
2
**片剂重量和压实度可通过调整模具腔填充和冲头调节实现。 Tablet weight and compactness are regulated by die filling and punch adjustment.
3
**旋转压片机能实现高连续性、高标准化和大批量生产。 Rotary presses allow high-continuity, highly standardized, large-scale production.
4
**片剂分割线允许患者自由分开服用，增强使用便利性。 Tablet score lines allow patients to split tablets easily for flexible dosing.

致密化适应性与Heckel模型、压实率Suitability for Densification & Heckel Model & Compaction Rate

1
致密化适应性 Suitability for Densification
粉床结构
Powder Bed Structure 粉床=固体+空气（粒间和粒内） Powder bed = solid + air (inter- and intra-particle)
致密化目的
Purpose of Densification 致密化粉床... Densifying a powder bed...   – 即减少孔隙部分 = Reduce the porous fraction – 增加固体部分 = Increase the solid fraction – 通过减少可用体积实现 ...by reducing its available volume.
目标
Objectives 使颗粒相互靠近，增加接触点 Bring the particles closer together and increase the contact points
阻力与压力
Resistance and Pressure 粉床对体积减少存在阻力，因此致密化所需压力会随粉床密度增加而增大 There is resistance of the powder bed to imposed volume reduction, so the pressure needed increases as densification proceeds
建模可行
Modelling 整个过程可通过建模进行定量描述 Modelling is possible for this process
2
Heckel模型 Heckel Model
公式与意义
Equation and Meaning Heckel建立了粉床相对密度变化与施加压力之间的正比关系 Heckel established a proportional relationship between the change in relative density of a powder bed and the applied pressure   公式如下： The equation is: dD/dP = K·(1-D) dD/dP = K*(1-D)  其中K为比例常数 where K is the proportionality constant
积分后表达式
Integrated Expression 将方程对D从D0到D，压力P从0到P积分，得： By integrating from D0 to D, and pressure from 0 to P: -ln(1-D) = bP + K -ln(1-D) = bP + K
相关参数定义
Parameters D0 = 零压力下的相对密度 D0 = relative density in absence of pressure D = D_compact / D_pycnometric（压实后密度/真密度） D = D_compact / D_pycnometric (density of compact under pressure versus pycnometric density of powder) P = 压片压力（MPa） P = pressure of compaction (MPa) b和K为常数 b and K are constants
3
压实率与孔隙率 Compaction Rate & Porosity
压实率定义
Definition of Compaction Rate 压实率（%）或致密度显示了致密化适应性 The compaction rate (%) or compactness highlights suitability for densification   其公式为： It is calculated as: taux de compaction = D_compact_après éjection / D_pycnométrique ×100 taux de compaction = D_compact_après éjection / D_pycnométrique × 100
孔隙率计算
Porosity Calculation e = 100 – compacité e = 100 – compactness   该方法反映了颗粒之间剩余空间的多少 This reflects the remaining space between particles
4
Heckel模型在压片过程中的应用 Application of Heckel Model in Tabletting
粉床致密过程示意
Powder Bed Densification Process 在压片过程中，随着上冲头向下，粉床体积减小、致密度提高 During tabletting, as the upper punch descends, the powder bed volume decreases and density increases   – 初始为松散状态 Starts loose – 加压后逐渐密实 Becomes denser with pressure – 模型适用于密实度-压力曲线的线性部分 The Heckel model fits the linear part of the density-pressure curve
公式在曲线上的应用
Equation on Curve ln(1/1-X) = bP + K ln(1/(1-X)) = bP + K 其中X为致密度，孔隙率=1-X Where X is compactness, porosity = 1-X
5
课堂补充要点 Additional Key Points from Lecture

致密化动力学描述 使用Heckel模型可以用数学方式描述致密化过程中相对密度随压力变化的具体规律 The Heckel model allows mathematical description of how relative density changes with pressure during densification

参数意义 通过拟合Heckel方程，可以获得物料致密能力和所需压力相关信息 Fitting Heckel's equation gives information on material densification ability and pressure required

实际意义 在实际生产中用于评估颗粒致密性能和调控压片工艺 Used in practice to assess granule compactibility and optimize tablet production

床中的变化与片剂可压性、力-位移关系What's Happening in the Powder Bed & Tablet Compact Cohesion & Force-Displacement Relationship

一、粉床中的变化——Heckel模型

What's happening in the powder bed? — Heckel Model

1
Heckel模型基础 Heckel模型通过数学方式描述了压片时粉床致密化的规律。 The Heckel model describes the densification of the powder bed during tabletting using a mathematical relationship.
关键公式 Heckel方程如下
The Heckel equation is: Ln(1/(1-X)) = kP + A 其中X代表致密度，P为压力，k和A为常数。 Where X is the compactness, P is the pressure, and k and A are constants.
3
致密化的四个阶段 There are four stages in powder bed densification:
1
颗粒重排与破碎
2
Rearrangement and fragmentation 初始阶段，颗粒移动、重排或破碎以填补空隙。 In the initial stage, particles move, rearrange, or fracture to fill void spaces.
2
塑性变形
3
Plastic deformation 粉体受力后发生形变并进一步压实。 The material undergoes deformation and further compaction under stress.
3
晶体网络的弹性变形（接近无孔隙时）
4
Elastic deformation of the crystalline network (as porosity nears 0) 当孔隙率接近0时发生弹性形变。 Elastic deformation occurs when porosity is almost zero.
4
黏滞与弹性恢复
5
Viscosity and elastic recovery 解除压力后部分恢复与迟滞现象。 Some recovery and hysteresis may occur after pressure release.
4
产物屈服压力和模型意义 图中斜率K的倒数表示材料的屈服压力。 The reciprocal of slope K on the plot represents the material's yield pressure.

二、片剂可压性的内聚力

Cohesion of a Compact (Compactibility)

作用力类型 片剂内部的内聚力来源包括
The cohesive forces inside the tablet include:

结合力 Binding forces

范德华力 Van der Waals

静电作用 Electrostatic

氢键 Hydrogen bond

固体键（熔融/溶解） Solid bonds (melting)

弹性力 Elastic forces

排模后发生的弹性恢复力 Elastic recovery after ejection

2
机械嵌合作用 颗粒间还存在机械咬合（物理卡合互锁）。 Mechanical interlocking also exists between particles.
3
内聚力对片剂性能的影响 足够的结合力和适度的弹性恢复保证片剂稳定和完整。 Sufficient binding and moderate elastic recovery ensure the stability and integrity of tablets.

三、力-位移关系

Force-Displacement Relationship

1
压片过程中力与冲头位移的关联 The relationship between applied force and upper punch displacement during tabletting:
2
全过程分解 The process can be divided into:
A→B 区域
施加压力，粉末被重排及压实（压实能） Region A→B: Force is applied, particles rearrange and become compacted (compression energy).
B 点
达到最大压力，形成片剂。 Point B: Maximum pressure reached, tablet formed.
B→D 区域
压力释放，产生弹性恢复和膨胀能。 Region B→D: Pressure release, elastic relaxation and expansion energy produced.
3
能量分配

ABC 区域表示净压缩做功 Area ABC = Net work of compression

DBC 区域表示弹性恢复做功 Area DBC = Elastic work of relaxation

还有一部分功耗被用于摩擦 Some energy is consumed by friction

4
能量与最终片剂性能关系 压片过程中能的分布决定了压实度、强度及弹性恢复等关键片剂属性。 The distribution of energies during tabletting determines the tablet’s density, strength, and elastic recovery.

压片过程中的作用力总结Summary of Forces in Tabletting

粉末受力总览 Summary of forces in the powder bed 在压片过程中，模具腔中的粉末会同时受到多个方向的力。 During tabletting, powders inside the die experience forces from multiple directions simultaneously.

1
上冲压力 Force applied on upper punch (FaFa​) 上冲头向下施加压力以压实粉末，该力通过颗粒间的接触逐步传递到整个粉层。 The upper punch applies a downward force to compact the powder, which is transmitted throughout the powder bed via interparticle contacts.
2
下冲压力 Force applied on lower punch (FbFb​) 下冲头向上支撑整个压力系统，是模腔内粉体压实的反向支点。 The lower punch provides an upward counter-force, supporting the compaction system and serving as a reaction point for the entire powder bed.
3
模壁径向力 Radial force applied on die walls (FrFr​) 压片时，粉层颗粒间传导的应力产生径向扩张趋势，这促使粉末向模具壁产生侧向推力。 Under compaction, the interparticle stress transmission induces a tendency of radial expansion, causing the powder to exert lateral force against the die wall.   这种径向力来源于系统因受压力而膨胀的本能响应。 This radial force is due to the system's expansion in response to the applied punching force.
4
轴向摩擦力 Axial friction force (FdFd​) 粉末和模壁之间存在摩擦，部分轴向施加的压力会以摩擦损耗的形式消散。 Friction exists between the powder and the die wall, causing part of the applied axial force to be lost as frictional dissipation.   摩擦力沿模壁和粉层内部产生，使得一部分力没有有效压实粉末。 This friction spreads along the die wall and within the powder bed, resulting in a loss of useful compaction force.
5
力的传递和损耗 Transmission and loss of force 上冲施加的压力在传递过程中，部分力被颗粒-颗粒接触和颗粒-模壁摩擦消耗，导致下冲实际承受的力小于上冲实际施加的力。 During transmission, the force applied by the upper punch is partly lost through particle–particle contacts and particle–die wall friction, so the force measured at the lower punch is less than at the upper punch.
6
工艺关注点 Points of process concern 力的损耗（特别是由于模壁摩擦与径向膨胀）会影响片剂的致密度和机械强度，因此在工艺优化和机器设计时必须加以控制。 Force loss (especially due to die wall friction and radial expansion) affects the compactness and mechanical strength of tablets, so it must be controlled during process optimization and equipment design.

片剂生产过程的质量控制Controls in Tablet Production Process

一、片剂生产中各阶段的控制 Tablet Quality Ensured by Controls at Each Stage

保证片剂质量需要多层次的质量控制。 Tablet quality is ensured by multiple levels of quality control.

主要包括三大环节
There are three main stages:
1
处方开发阶段的控制 Controls during formulation 在配方开发和工艺设计早期，需要进行原辅料和预混物理性质等的检测。 At the early stage of formulation development and process design, it’s necessary to test physical properties of raw materials and pre-mixes.
2
生产过程中的持续控制 Ongoing controls during manufacturing 在生产过程中，需对关键质量参数进行在线监测与调节，以保证工艺一致性。 During production, key quality parameters must be monitored and adjusted in real time to ensure process consistency.
3
成品的最终质量控制 Final controls (Representative sample of the batch) 对最终片剂，从每批次抽取具有代表性的样品进行全面检验。 For the finished tablets, a representative sample is taken from each batch for comprehensive testing.

二、各阶段具体控制内容与检测指标 Detailed Control Items and Test Parameters in Each Stage

1
粉末阶段 Powders

检测颗粒尺寸与粒径分布。 Test particle size and size distribution.

形貌和流动性对后续混合、压片有重要影响。 Shape and flowability are important for subsequent mixing and compression.

2
混合阶段 Mixture

检查均匀性。 Assess homogeneity.

采用多点取样测定API均匀分布，预防混合不均。 Use multi-point sampling to measure API uniformity, preventing poor blending.

3
制粒阶段 Granulation

检测颗粒大小与分布、残余水分（湿法制粒）。 Test particle size/distribution and residual moisture (for wet granulation).

水分影响片剂质量和稳定性。 Moisture content affects tablet quality and stability.

4
压片过程 Tablets

检查质量和硬度的均匀性（包括压片过程中和压片结束时）。 Check uniformity of mass and hardness (both during and after compression).

若片剂包衣，还需检测脆碎度。 If tablets are coated, check for friability.

5
最终片剂（无包衣或包衣片）Final Tablet (bare or coated)

检查质量、硬度均匀性和含量均匀性。 Test for mass, hardness, and content uniformity.

进行崩解时限检查与溶出度测试，评估药物释放性能。 Carry out disintegration and dissolution tests to evaluate drug release performance.

三、主要控制方法与检测仪器 Main Testing Methods and Instruments

粉末流动性测试 Powder Flow Test 利用流动性仪测定粉末流速，是工艺连续和后续操作顺利的保障。 Powder flow testers measure flow rate, ensuring smooth continuous processing.

粉末堆积性测试 Powder Packing Test 通过振实密度仪检测堆积密度。 Bulk/tapped density analyzers measure powder packing properties.

片剂硬度测试 Tablet Hardness Test 采用硬度计测定片剂耐压碎能力。 Tablet hardness testers measure resistance to fracture.

脆碎度测试 Friability Test 用脆碎度仪评估片剂耐磨耗性能。 Friabilators assess resistance of tablets to abrasion.

崩解时限测试 Disintegration Test 用崩解仪检测片剂在规定条件下的崩解速率。 Disintegration apparatus measures the rate at which tablets break apart.

溶出度测试 Dissolution Test 用溶出仪评估药物释放速率与总量。 Dissolution testers determine drug release speed and total drug released.

四、老师课堂补充强调的常规实践与注意要点 Teacher’s Additional Key Points from Lecture

片剂生产全过程质量控制必须贯穿始终，从原材料到成品不得放松。 Quality control must be maintained throughout the tablet production process, from raw materials to finished products.

取样须具代表性，以确保全批次的一致性。 Sampling must be representative to ensure batch consistency.

同一批所有检测，不仅要用同一套方法，也需要考虑均匀性和稳定性的动态检测。 All tests on a batch should be performed using the same methods, with consideration of uniformity and monitoring of any dynamic changes.

对混合均匀性，需多点多次取样。 For blend homogeneity, multiple samples from different positions and at different times should be taken.

残余水分一般通过红外水分仪等精准量化，并需对成品和粉体分别检测。 Residual moisture is generally quantified using infrared balances and should be tested on both the powder and final tablets.

质量控制不仅针对合格产品，也要发现异常点，及时纠正工艺。 QC is not only for ensuring qualified products but also for detecting abnormalities and correcting the process in time.

片剂压制过程的质量控制Tabletting Controls

1
片剂质量控制项目 Tableting Quality Control Items

压片过程中需对片剂进行多项控制以保证药品质量和疗效。 Multiple controls are required during tabletting to ensure drug quality and efficacy.

主要控制项目如下
The main control items are as follows:

片剂质量（质量均匀性） Mass (Uniformity of Mass)

直径抗压碎强度 Diametrical Crushing Strength

有效成分含量 API Content

片剂脆碎度 Tablet Friability

崩解时限 Disintegration Time

有效成分溶出时间 API Dissolution Time

2
质量均匀性 Uniformity of Mass

生产结束后，从每批片剂中随机抽取20片，逐个称量，计算平均质量。 After production, randomly sample 20 tablets per batch, weigh each individually, and calculate the mean mass.

允许一定数量的片剂偏离平均值5%（小部分允许偏差10%），具体标准见药典。 A certain number may deviate ±5% from the mean (a small fraction at ±10%) as per pharmacopeia standards。

保证每片药量的一致性，防止剂量误差。 This ensures dosing consistency and prevents dosage errors。

3
直径抗压碎强度 Diametrical Crushing Strength

测试片剂抵抗破裂的能力。 Tests a tablet’s resistance to breaking.

用压片硬度计施加力直到片剂断裂，记录最大破碎力F（单位N）。 Measure the maximum force F (in newtons) needed to break the tablet, using a hardness tester.

公式： Formula: sigma = 2F / (pi * D * e) 其中F为最大破碎力，D为片剂直径（mm），e为片剂厚度（mm）。 Where F = maximal crushing force, D = diameter (mm), e = thickness (mm).

典型范围为1–2兆帕（MPa）。 Typical values range from 1–2 MPa。

4
片剂脆碎度 Tablet Friability

评估片剂在运输、包装等过程中的耐磨损情况。 Evaluates tablet resistance to abrasion during handling and packaging.

放入脆碎度仪，旋转100次，取出除尘，称量前后质量。 Place tablets in a friabilator, rotate for 100 revolutions, then remove dust and weigh before and after.

损失质量百分比F%计算公式为
The loss percentage F% is calculated as: F% = (M0 – M) / M0 × 100 其中M0为试验前质量，M为试验后质量。 Where M0 is pre-test mass and M is post-test mass.

F%应低于1%，否则需调整处方或工艺。 F% should be <1%; otherwise, adjust formulation or process。

5
崩解时限 Disintegration Time

测定片剂在体内环境中分解的速度。 Measures the speed at which tablets break apart in simulated bodily fluids.

崩解仪一次性测试6–12片，测每片完全崩解所需时间。 Using a disintegration tester, measure the time for each of 6–12 tablets to fully disintegrate.

无包衣片剂崩解时限 ≤15分钟，有包衣片 ≤30分钟。 Uncoated tablets must disintegrate within 15 minutes; coated tablets, within 30 minutes。

崩解不合格则无法保证有效释放。 Failure means inadequate active ingredient release.

6
有效成分含量与溶出度 API Content and Dissolution Rate

随机抽取一定数量片剂，测定每片有效成分的真实含量。 Randomly sample tablets and analyze true API content per tablet.

检查API含量应在规定接受范围（多数为85–115%）内。 The API content should fall within specified acceptance range (usually 85–115%).

溶出度测试观察药物溶出速率，常用溶出仪进行测试。 Dissolution rate is evaluated using a dissolution tester to observe release speed.

只有溶出达到标准才能确保生物利用度合格。 Only standard-compliant dissolution ensures sufficient bioavailability。

7
老师课堂补充要点 Additional Key Points from Lecture

每项测试均为药典规定，全球范围内广泛采用，生产过程中与最终成品都需抽检。 Each test is pharmacopeia-mandated, required globally for process and finished product sampling.

取样需具有代表性，测试用具一致，过程细节需精确（如旋转圈数、测试时间等）。 Sampling must be representative, test devices used must be consistent, and procedural details (revolution count, test time, etc.) precisely followed.

若出现脆碎度/溶出不合格，可通过增加粘合剂、改进干燥工艺等方法优化。 Failures in friability/dissolution may be solved by increasing binder content or improving drying steps。

口服制剂
LDA系统Oral case: LDA system
1
药物从片剂到吸收的三步过程Three-step process from tablet to absorption
第一步
活性成分的释放 Step 1: Release of the active ingredient

片剂进入体液后，首先崩解，释放出分散状态的颗粒。 After the tablet enters body fluids, it first disintegrates, releasing dispersed particles.

第二步
活性成分的溶解 Step 2: Dissolution of the active ingredient

释放出来的药物颗粒进一步溶解于消化道液体，形成分子态的溶解药物（即溶解的活性成分）。 The released drug particles further dissolve in the digestive fluids, forming dissolved molecules (i.e. dissolved active ingredient).

第三步
肠道吸收 Step 3: Intestinal absorption

溶解后的活性成分才能通过肠上皮被吸收进入血液循环，产生药效。 Only the dissolved active ingredient can be absorbed by the intestinal epithelium into the bloodstream for effect.

溶解Dissolution

1
定义Definition

溶解是指将固体物质分散到液体（溶剂）中，直至分子状态。 Dissolution means dispersing a solid substance into a liquid (solvent) at the molecular level.

这是分子分散的最终阶段。 This is the final stage of molecular dispersion.

2
热力学过程Thermodynamic process

溶解过程是一个热力学（达到平衡）的过程，只有当达到溶解度极限才停止。 The dissolution process is thermodynamic (equilibrium-driven), and only stops at the solubility limit.

3
溶解的结果Result of dissolution

溶解完成后，所得为光学透明的溶液相。 The result of the dissolution operation is an optically transparent solution phase.

水相中的溶解=与水分子相互作用Aqueous phase dissolution = interaction with H₂O molecules

1
晶体聚集体与溶剂分子的作用Interaction between crystal aggregates and solvent molecules

在水相中，药物的晶体聚集体与水分子相互作用，解离为溶剂化的离子。 In aqueous solution, aggregates of drug crystals interact with water molecules and dissociate into solvated ions.

水分子通过包裹药物分子或离子，帮助它们从晶体结构中分离出来并在水中分散。 Water molecules wrap around drug molecules or ions, facilitating their removal from the crystal lattice and dispersal in solution.

表观溶解度
CsApparent Solubility: Cs
1
定义及单位Definition and units

Cs为饱和浓度，在特定温度和压力下，固体与液体平衡时可达到的最大浓度。 Cs (saturation concentration) is the maximum concentration achievable at equilibrium between solid and liquid at defined temperature and pressure.

常用单位是质量（摩尔或克）除以体积（升或其倍数）。 The usual unit is mass (e.g., mole or gram) per volume (e.g., liter or submultiple).

2
欧洲药典的表述Ph. Eur. Description

欧洲药典以“溶解一定量物质所需的溶剂体积”来描述溶解度。 According to the European Pharmacopoeia (Ph. Eur.), solubility is often described by the volume of solvent needed to dissolve a given quantity of substance.

表观溶解度的影响因素Factors influencing apparent solubility

1
取决于固态结构Depends on the structure in the solid state

一种物质的表观溶解度（即表观平衡下的溶解度）受其在固态下结构的影响。 Apparent solubility (at apparent equilibrium) depends on the organization in the solid state.

2
原材料和结晶型Raw material and crystalline form

需要了解原材料状况，尤其是其结晶型态，这对溶解度有重要影响。 It is crucial to know the raw material, especially its crystalline form, since it greatly influences solubility.

欧洲药典溶解度分级European Pharmacopoeia solubility classification

非常易溶
每克物质需要溶剂体积小于1毫升。 Very soluble: less than 1 mL solvent required per gram of substance.
易溶
每克需要1至10毫升溶剂。 Freely soluble: 1–10 mL per gram.
可溶
每克需要10至30毫升溶剂。 Soluble: 10–30 mL per gram.
较易溶
30至100毫升每克。 Sparingly soluble: 30–100 mL per gram.
微溶
100至1000毫升每克。 Slightly soluble: 100–1000 mL per gram.
极微溶
1000至10000毫升每克。 Very slightly soluble: 1000–10000 mL per gram.
几乎不溶
大于10000毫升每克。 Practically insoluble: more than 10000 mL per gram.

对乙酰氨基酚溶解度与溶解速率解析Example: Acetaminophen Solubility and Dissolution Rate

1
对乙酰氨基酚在水中的溶解度特性Solubility Characteristics of Acetaminophen in Water

对乙酰氨基酚属于“较易溶”类别，在15至25度时，需要30至100毫升水来溶解1克药物。 Acetaminophen is classified as "fairly soluble"; at 15–25°C, it requires 30–100 mL of water to dissolve 1 gram of the drug.

换算为每升可溶解10至33克药物。 This means that 10 to 33 grams of acetaminophen can be dissolved per liter of water.

2
溶解度的量化参数Characteristic Parameters of Dissolution Quantification
用于表征溶解过程的关键参数是
溶解度Cs和溶解速率。 The key parameters for describing dissolution are solubility Cs and dissolution speed.

溶解度Cs代表平衡条件下药物在溶剂中的最大溶解浓度。 Cs means the maximum concentration of a drug in solvent at equilibrium.

溶解速率是单位时间内固体药物向溶液转移的速率。 The dissolution speed is the rate at which the solid drug passes into solution per unit time.

3
溶解速率的理论基础——扩散层理论Diffusion Layer Theory for Dissolution Speed

被溶解的固体表面被一层静止液膜包围，该膜的厚度用h表示。 The dissolving solid surface is surrounded by a stagnant diffusion film of thickness h.

该层内形成药物分子浓度梯度，溶液最靠近固体表面处为Cs，远离表面时为Ct。 Inside this layer, a concentration gradient is established: Cs at the solid surface, Ct farther from the surface.

静止液膜的厚度h受搅拌和温度影响，搅拌越充分、温度越高，h越小。 The thickness h depends on agitation and temperature; stronger stirring and higher temperature make h smaller.

药物分子需要通过这层膜向外扩散，导致溶解速度受限于通过液膜的扩散速率。 Drug molecules must diffuse through this film, so the dissolution rate is governed by diffusion across this layer.

4
溶解速率方程Dissolution Speed Equation

药物溶解速率可由Noyes-Whitney和Nernst-Brunner方程描述： The dissolution speed can be described by the Noyes-Whitney and Nernst-Brunner equations:

公式如下
The formula is:
其中变量含义如下
Where the variables mean:
dQ/dt
单位时间内溶解的药量，单位mg/s。 dQ/dt: Amount dissolved per unit time, mg/s.
S
固体与溶剂接触的表面积，单位cm²。 S: Surface area of the solid in contact with the medium, cm².
Cs
固体饱和浓度，溶解度，单位mg/ml。 Cs: Saturation concentration (solubility), mg/ml.
Ct
某一时刻溶液的实际浓度，单位mg/ml。 Ct: Actual concentration in solution at time t, mg/ml.
D
药物在该液层中的扩散系数，单位cm²/s。 D: Diffusion coefficient within the diffusion layer, cm²/s.
h
扩散层厚度，单位cm。 h: Diffusion layer thickness, cm.
V
体系体积，单位m³。 V: Volume of the system, m³.
5
溶解过程的动态与速率受限特性Dynamic and Rate-limiting Features of Dissolution

随着药物持续溶解，溶液中Ct不断升高，Cs-Ct逐渐减小，导致溶解速率变慢。 As the drug keeps dissolving, Ct increases, Cs–Ct gets smaller, and the dissolution speed slows down.

达到Cs即饱和浓度后，系统进入固体与溶液共存的动态平衡，浓度不再升高。 When Cs is reached (equilibrium solubility), the system achieves solid–solution equilibrium and concentration stops rising.

6
溶出实验的原则Principle of the Dissolution Test

药典规定溶出实验必须在“汇流条件”（sink condition）下进行，即最终浓度应为Cs的1/3至1/10以下。 Pharmacopoeia requires dissolution tests to be performed under sink conditions, meaning final concentration should be 3 to 10 times lower than Cs.

若浓度接近Cs，溶解驱动力减弱，药物溶出的速度大幅降低。 If concentration approaches Cs, the driving force for dissolution weakens, and dissolution speed drops significantly.

设计实验时要保证溶液体积足够大，使整个药片完全溶解后的浓度仍能满足上述汇流条件。 Experimental design must ensure sufficient solution volume so that even after complete dissolution, the final concentration is still within sink condition.

总结总结To Sum Up

溶解度Cs决定浓度极限，溶解速率决定到达饱和的快慢。 Solubility Cs determines the concentration limit; dissolution speed determines the rate to reach this limit.

溶解速率受扩散层厚度（h）、扩散系数　、表面积（S）、浓度差（Cs–Ct）等物理参数影响。 Dissolution rate depends on physical parameters: layer thickness (h), diffusion coefficient (D), surface area (S), and concentration gradient (Cs–Ct).
答案 D

合理实验条件可帮助评价与优化制剂处方、提高药物生物利用度。 Appropriate experimental conditions help evaluate and optimize formulations, improving drug bioavailability.

阿司匹林硬胶囊的溶出速率研究Rate of dissolution of acetylsalicylic acid from hard capsules

1
溶出速率受崩解剂用量影响Dissolution rate depends on the amount of disintegrating agent

图1显示，随着药物中崩解剂（羧甲淀粉钠）用量的增加，阿司匹林的溶出速率显著提升。 Figure 1 shows that as the amount of disintegrant (sodium starch glycolate) in the drug increases, the dissolution rate of acetylsalicylic acid significantly increases.

崩解剂从0 mg递加至100 mg时，在200分钟内药物的溶出比例不断提升。 When the disintegrant is increased from 0 mg to 100 mg, the drug's percentage dissolved within 200 minutes rises continuously.

崩解剂通过促进药物颗粒的崩散和分散，增加了与溶媒的接触面积，加速了药物的释放。 Disintegrants promote the breakup and dispersion of drug particles, increasing the surface area in contact with solvent and accelerating drug release.

2
溶出速率受稀释剂类别影响Dissolution rate depends on carrier/diluent type

图2显示，不同稀释剂对阿司匹林溶出速率影响较大，羟丙甲纤维素和聚乙烯吡咯烷酮效果最佳，磷酸氢钙和无稀释剂条件下溶出速率最慢。 Figure 2 shows different diluents (carriers) have markedly different effects: hydroxypropyl cellulose and polyvinylpyrrolidone give the fastest dissolution, while dicalcium phosphate and no carrier give the slowest.

部分稀释剂如微晶纤维素、淀粉、乳化剂等也可改善溶出速率，但效果弱于最佳组合。 Some carriers (microcrystalline cellulose, corn starch, emulsifying agents) improve dissolution, but less so than the optimal carriers.

稀释剂影响药物的分散性、润湿性与颗粒结构，进而影响药物释药速率。 Carriers affect drug dispersibility, wettability, and particle structure, and thus the drug release rate.

3
结论：配方优化对释放至关重要Conclusion: Formulation optimization is crucial for release

合理选择崩解剂与稀释剂类型及用量，可大幅提升固体制剂的溶出速率，优化生物利用度。 Rational choice of disintegrant and carrier type and amount can greatly increase the dissolution rate of solid forms and optimize bioavailability.

溶出度测试The dissolution test

1
药典中描述了4种主要溶出度测试仪器Four main dissolution devices described in pharmacopeias
篮筐法
适用于易漂浮或密度低的制剂，保证药物被固定在搅拌篮内溶出。 Basket apparatus: suitable for floating or low-density dosage forms, fixes the drug inside the basket for dissolution.
桨法
桨叶搅拌溶液，确保固液界面的不断更新，常用于片剂，也可搭配浸没装置用于胶囊。 Paddle apparatus: the paddle stirs the solution, ensuring renewal of the solid/liquid interface, commonly used for tablets and (with a sinker) capsules.
活塞装置
通过活塞运动辅助溶出，应用相对较少。 Piston apparatus: uses piston movement to assist dissolution, less commonly used.

药典规定必须根据具体制剂合理选择和验证实验条件，保证测试科学有效。 Pharmacopoeia requires experimental conditions to be reasonably chosen and validated according to dosage form to ensure scientific and effective testing.

2
篮筐法原理与特点Principle and features of the basket apparatus

医药制剂被装入篮筐夹持，篮筐旋转带动物质搅拌，促进固体与液体界面更新，加速溶出。 The dosage form is clamped inside a rotating basket, whose movement stirs the medium and renews the solid/liquid interface, promoting dissolution.

适用于易浮型胶囊等，但环境搅拌均匀性欠佳，容易形成局部浓度梯度。 Designed for floating capsules, but agitation may be non-homogeneous, leading to local concentration gradients.

3
桨法原理与优点Principle and advantages of the paddle apparatus

通过桨叶转动带动圆底容器中液体流动，使固体持续与新鲜溶液接触，促进溶出。 Paddle rotation drives liquid flow in a hemispheric-bottom vessel, ensuring continuous contact between solid and fresh solvent and promoting dissolution.

适用于片剂，也可加装sinker保持胶囊沉底，防止漂浮。 Suitable for tablets; can add a sinker for hard capsules to keep them submerged and prevent floating.

方法简单、重复性好，被广泛应用于溶出度检测。 Simple, reproducible, and widely used for dissolution testing.

4
Sinker的作用及结构Function and structure of a sinker

用于将易浮或密度较小的胶囊、片剂等保持在容器底部，避免影响搅拌与溶出速率。 Used to keep capsules or tablets (which might float) at the vessel bottom, preventing disturbance of stirring and dissolution rate.

常用不锈钢丝网或弹簧结构，大小依照药典标准精确设计。 Usually made of stainless steel mesh or spiral spring, sized according to pharmacopeial standards.

5
课堂补充要点Additional lecture highlights

溶出设备选择需结合药物特性、剂型特点及实验目的综合考虑，选用时应兼顾重现性与代表性。 Choice of dissolution apparatus must consider drug properties, dosage form features, and experimental aims, balancing reproducibility and representativeness.

对于特殊配方（如泡腾片、浮力片、难溶片等），应优先考虑溶出曲线代表性及方法标准化。 For special forms (e.g., effervescent, floating, poorly soluble), focus on representative profiles and method standardization.

浸没装置（sinker）不可影响药物释放行为，只能辅助下沉，设计与材料需严格符合药典标准。 Sinker must not affect drug release; it only aids submersion and must strictly meet pharmacopeial design/material standards.

连续流动池溶出仪与操作条件Continuous flow cell dissolving apparatus and operating conditions

1
连续流动池Continuous flow cells

连续流动池是一种圆柱型装置，进行上下往复垂直运动。 Continuous flow cell is a cylindrical device performing a back and forth vertical movement.

主要用于药物制剂的溶出测试。 It is mainly used for dissolution testing of pharmaceutical dosage forms.

优点是能模拟体内流动条件，适用于速释及控释等不同制剂的生物药剂学研究。 It can simulate in vivo flow conditions and is suitable for various biopharmaceutical studies including immediate and controlled release forms.

缺点：溶出介质体积较小，通常为200至250毫升，仅适合特定的研究需求。 Disadvantage: the volume of dissolution medium is low (usually 200 to 250 ml), limiting its application to specific studies.

2
溶出实验操作条件Operating conditions for dissolution test

药典要求“合理且有理据”的操作条件。 Pharmacopoeia requires "reasonable and justifiable" operating conditions.

温度应控制在37℃，以模拟人体体温。 The temperature should be set at 37°C to simulate human body temperature.

搅拌转速一般为50至200 rpm，低速（50-100 rpm，最高150 rpm）有利于区分不同处方。 Stirring speed generally ranges from 50 to 200 rpm; a lower speed (50-100 rpm, max 150 rpm) helps differentiate formulations.

流速要求保持层流状态，便于结果可重复性（4至50 ml/min）。 Flow rate must be laminar (between 4 and 50 ml/min) for reproducible results.

必要时对介质进行除气处理，避免气泡影响结果。 Deaeration of the medium is necessary if required, to avoid bubbles affecting results.

溶出介质的组分需根据药品溶解性选择体积（满足“汇流条件”），并根据活性物理化学性质和制剂类型调整pH The composition and volume of medium should be chosen according to solubility ("sink conditions") and pH adjusted based on the physicochemical properties of the active substance and formulation type.

3
溶出介质的组成Composition of the dissolution medium

理想溶出介质的选择要兼顾活性成分的溶解特性、剂型特点和测试目的。 Selection of proper medium depends on the dissolving properties of the active substance, type of dosage form, and the test purpose.

所有制剂均使用水相介质。 All pharmaceutical forms use an aqueous medium.

常规释放制剂采用固定pH的介质。 Conventional release forms use fixed pH medium.

缓释或控释制剂需有指令性pH渐变（可有多种方法）。 Extended-release forms require a mandatory pH increase (by various methods).

缓释/肠溶制剂通常两步法
前2小时于酸性介质，随后调整至pH 6.8缓冲液。 Delayed-release forms often require two steps: first in acidic medium for 2 hours, then buffered at pH 6.8 for the remainder.
4
表面活性剂的应用及要求Use of surfactants and requirements

必要时，表面活性剂可作为润湿剂添加（如0.05%十二烷基硫酸钠Na lauryl sulfate）。 Surfactants can be added as wetting agents if necessary (e.g., 0.05% sodium lauryl sulfate).

C12离子型表面活性剂常用作清洁剂，也适用于极难溶活性成分或生物药剂学研究中。 C12 ionic surfactant detergents are used for very poorly soluble active substances and in biopharmaceutical studies.

注意表面活性剂浓度始终应低于临界胶束浓度（CMC），避免因形成胶束导致溶解机制变化。 The surfactant concentration must always be kept below the critical micelle concentration (CMC) to avoid unwanted solubilization.
5
补充要点（课堂讲解）Additional key points (from lecture)

层流控制有助于确保溶出动力学结果的可重现性，减少湍流造成的个体差异。 Laminar flow ensures reproducible dissolution kinetics and reduces individual result variation caused by turbulence.

体外溶出测试的所有条件（温度、pH、搅拌、体积等）均应最大程度接近体内生理环境，以保证生物相关性。 All testing conditions (temperature, pH, stirring, volume) should mimic physiological conditions as closely as possible for biorelevant results.

“汇流条件”指溶剂体积足以保证最大溶解药量远低于饱和度，驱动持续溶出。 "Sink condition" means the solvent volume should keep the drug's maximum dissolved amount much lower than saturation, promoting continuous dissolution.

指定pH变化流程和表面活性剂使用，是为模拟口服制剂由胃到肠的转运与吸收环境。 Specifying pH shifts and optional surfactant use mimics the gastric-to-intestinal environment faced by oral medications.

操作条件
取样Operating conditions: sampling
操作条件
样品Operating conditions: samples
1
取样方法与注意事项Sampling methods and key points

取样可以采用手动或自动方式。 Sampling can be performed manually or automatically.

需要合理选择取样位置，推荐在搅拌充分、避开死角的区域进行采样。 The sampling location should be carefully selected, recommended in areas with thorough mixing, avoiding dead zones.

取样后需立即过滤，仅测定取样时刻已溶解的药物含量。 Immediate filtration after sampling is necessary to measure only the substance dissolved at the time of collection.

若取样时混入颗粒，会导致结果偏差，因颗粒状态下的有效成分并未真正溶解。 If particles are collected during sampling, the results will be inaccurate since undissolved material is not truly indicative of dissolution.

图示说明USP标准，规定取样区为自溶出介质表面下1cm至桨叶上方1cm之间的区域。 According to the USP, the sampling zone is defined as the area from 1 cm below the liquid surface to 1 cm above the paddle (as shown in the figure).

2
取样频率与测试目的Sampling frequency and test objective

取样频率应根据实验目的设定。 The frequency of sampling should be set according to the purpose of the test.

在质量控制中，取样时间间隔需遵循药典规定。 In quality control, sampling time intervals must follow pharmacopeial specifications.

在制剂开发和生物药剂学研究中，必须获取完整的溶出曲线，便于分析药物释放特征。 In formulation development and biopharmaceutical studies, a complete dissolution profile is essential for analyzing drug release characteristics.

溶出曲线通常反映在不同pH条件下溶出量随时间的变化，便于评价制剂性能。 The dissolution profile usually shows the amount dissolved over time under different pH conditions, facilitating evaluation of formulation performance.

3
课堂补充要点Lecture additional notes

取样操作应避免人为误差，例如吸取液面漂浮物或吸入底部沉积的颗粒。 Sampling operations should avoid human error, such as aspirating floating scum or bottom sediment particles.

取样与过滤要迅速进行，避免溶液中药物浓度因后续溶解或沉淀而发生变化。 Sampling and filtration should be performed rapidly to prevent post-sampling concentration changes from further dissolution or precipitation.

图中所示，标准取样区的选择可显著降低取样误差，提高结果重现性。 As shown in the figure, standardizing the sampling zone significantly reduces sampling errors and improves reproducibility.

根据实验目的，如药典检测、配方比较或生物等效性评估，取样点设置有所不同，但都需保证数据完整、真实。 According to the test purpose (pharmacopeia, formulation comparison, or bioequivalence test), sampling points may vary, but data must always be complete and reliable.

操作条件
检测单位数Operating conditions: number of units examined
1
检测单位数量依据药典规定Number of units to be tested is defined by the Pharmacopoeia

初始需检测6个单位（S1水平），以判断结果是否符合标准。 Initially, 6 units must be tested (S1 level) to determine if results meet standard requirements.

若结果不合格，则需再检测6个（S2水平）或12个（S3水平）单位。 If results are not compliant, an additional 6 (S2 level) or 12 (S3 level) units will be tested.

各水平下均有明确的合格判定标准，见下表。 Each level has specific acceptance criteria, as shown in the table.

溶出度测试结果的表达Results of the dissolution test

2
Q值定义及计算方式Definition and calculation method of Q value

Q为在规定时间区间内溶解的有效成分总量。 Q is the total amount of active substance dissolved within a specified time interval.

计算公式为浓度乘以取样时刻溶出介质的体积。 The calculation formula is concentration multiplied by the volume of dissolution medium at the time of sampling.

Q通常以标签含量的百分比（%）表示。 Q is typically expressed as a percentage of the labeled content.

溶出度测试结果的解读Results of the dissolution test

3
判定目标Interpretation objectives

判定生产批次是否符合欧洲药典要求，根据三种剂型的不同规定。 The aim is to determine if the manufactured batch complies with Ph. Eur. requirements based on the specific provisions for three dosage forms.

包括速释制剂、缓释制剂和肠溶（延释）制剂。 This includes conventional (immediate) release forms, extended-release forms, and delayed-release (gastro-resistant) forms.

结果解读
合格判定标准 Interpretation of results: acceptance criteria
4
速释制剂的判断标准Criteria for conventional/immediate release forms

每个检测单位在45分钟内Q需大于或等于80%。 For each unit, Q must be greater than or equal to 80% within 45 minutes.

判定强调“每个单位”，所有单位都需达标。 The criterion applies to each unit individually; all tested units must meet the standard.

5
缓释制剂的判断标准Criteria for extended-release forms

需至少设置3个时间点（t1、t2、t3）进行考察。 At least 3 timepoints (t1, t2, t3) must be set for assessment.

t1时，Q为20%~30%（证明为延释）； At t1, Q should be 20% to 30% (proving delayed release).

t2时，Q为50%； At t2, Q should be 50%.

t3时，Q不小于80%。 At t3, Q should be no less than 80%.

6
延释（肠溶）制剂的判断标准Criteria for delayed-release (gastro-resistant) forms
至少2个时间点
酸性阶段（2小时pH1）末，不应有释放； At least 2 timepoints: at the end of the acid stage (2 hours at pH 1), there should be no drug release.

及随后的pH6.8阶段2小时后（共4小时），Q需大于等于75%。 After an additional 2 hours at pH 6.8 (i.e., by 4 hours in total), Q must be greater than or equal to 75%.

冻干的定义与原理Definition and principle of freeze-drying

1
定义Definition

冻干是指将溶液、混悬液等经过冷冻后，通过升华方式脱除冰的过程。 Freeze-drying means desiccation by sublimation of ice from solutions or suspensions, after solidification by freezing。

该过程区别于普通干燥，冰由固态直接转变为气态，无液态中间过程。 This process differs from conventional drying: ice transitions directly from solid to vapor with no intermediate liquid phase。

2
操作分两步Operation in 2 steps

第一步是将产品冷冻。 The first step is freezing the product。

第二步是冰的升华（即主干燥），将冰直接从固态转为气态。 The second step is ice sublimation (namely, primary drying), turning ice directly from the solid state into vapor。

主干燥阶段主要去除游离水，副干燥阶段去除结合水。 Primary desiccation removes free (open) water, secondary desiccation removes bound water。

3
冻干依赖于水的三相点Relies on the triple point of water

三相点指水的固、液、气三态能共存的特定温度与气压下。 The triple point of water is the specific temperature and pressure where water's solid, liquid, and vapor phases coexist。

因而冻结干燥需要低温低压条件。 Thus, freeze-drying must operate at low pressure and low temperature。

这样冰在不经过液体的情况下直接升华。 This ensures that ice sublimates directly, bypassing the liquid phase。

4
超临界流体的描述Supercritical fluid description（图解说明）

当液体和气体密度相等时，两相界限消失，得到称为“超临界流体”的状态。 When the densities of the liquid and gas become equal, the distinction between the two phases vanishes, resulting in a "supercritical fluid"。

例如水在高温高压时达到超临界点。 For example, water reaches a supercritical state at high temperature and pressure。

冻干的目的与应用Objectives and purpose of freeze-drying

问1. 为什么以及什么用冻干？Why and what is freeze-dried?
答

主要用于保存易水解、易氧化的物质（即要求低残余水分），如生物制品。 Freeze-drying is mainly used to store substances sensitive to hydrolysis or oxidation (requiring low residual moisture), such as biologics。

低残余水分提高了微生物稳定性。 The low residual moisture provides high microbiological stability。

冻干工艺温和，适合热敏感物质。 The process is gentle, suitable for heat-labile (thermosensitive) substances。

2
典型适用对象Typical applications

疫苗 Vaccines

血浆和血液制品 Plasma and blood derivatives

注射用溶液与混悬液 Solutions and suspensions for injection

胶体混悬（如脂质体、纳米粒等） Colloidal suspensions (e.g., liposomes, nanoparticles)

API保护的目的与关键点Objectives: Protection of API

1
低温保护Low temperature protection

冻干全程低温，有助于避免活性成分降解。 The freeze-drying process is carried out at low temperature, helping prevent degradation of actives。

2
低压促使冰升华Reduced pressure to enhance ice sublimation

低气压有利于冰在固-气间直接转化，提高干燥效率。 Reduced pressure facilitates direct sublimation of ice, making the process more efficient。

3
无中间液相Absence of intermediate liquid phase

无液相过程可避免许多通常在普通干燥法出现的变性、失活或污染。 The absence of a liquid phase prevents denaturation, inactivation, or contamination seen in conventional drying。

4
保留最终形态Retention of final shape

干燥结束后恢复至室温，物质结构基本保持不变。 After returning to room temperature post-drying, the product retains its original shape and structure。

冻干制剂的药学应用Pharmacy Applications of Freeze-drying Products

1
冻干制剂的用途Applications of freeze-dried products

冻干产品可实现初始溶液或伪溶液的快速、完全复原（如注射用溶液）。 Freeze-drying products enable rapid and complete reconstitution of the initial solution or pseudo-solution (such as solutions for injection).

口服冻干片在极少量水下可于口中迅速崩解（如速溶片Maxalt Melt）。 Oral lyophilisates rapidly disintegrate in the mouth with minimal water (such as Maxalt Melt, an anti-migraine fast-melt tablet).

老师强调冻干制剂包括注射用冻干粉末、冻干片和其他可迅速复原的固体形式，常用于难以保存或易降解的活性成分。 The professor highlighted that freeze-dried products include injectable lyophilized powders, rapidly dissolving oral tablets, and other solid forms that can be quickly reconstituted, typically for unstable or easily degradable actives.

无菌冻干制剂的生产流程Manufacture of Sterile Lyophilisates

2
生产流程与关键控制点Production process and key control points

水和辅料与活性成分混合，配成溶液。 Water and excipients are mixed with the active ingredient(s) to prepare the solution.

溶液经过澄清过滤与灭菌过滤去除杂质和微生物。 The solution undergoes clarifying and sterilizing filtration to remove particulates and microbes.

灭菌后的液体分装至已洗涤、灭菌的容器和塞中。 The sterilized solution is filled into washed, sterilized containers and closed with sterilized plugs.

关键步骤：冻干，将产品从溶液状态转为无水固体。 Key step: Freeze-drying transforms the solution into a dry, solid state.

随后进行密封、贴签和包装。 Afterwards, sealing, labeling, and packaging are conducted.

老师补充：冻干前后无菌控制至关重要，避免二次污染。 The lecturer added: Sterility must be maintained before and after freeze-drying to prevent secondary contamination.

冻干的缺点Disadvantages of Freeze-drying

3
只有在无法用其他方法时才选择冻干Freeze-drying is used only if there is no better alternative

冻干投资与运行成本极高（每吨废弃水需1500-2500kWh）。 Freeze-drying involves very high investment and operational costs (1500–2500 kWh/ton of water discarded).

操作周期长（24–100小时），生产效率低，例如疫苗冻干一般需90小时。 The process is time-consuming (24–100 hours), resulting in low productivity—e.g., 90 hours for vaccines.

操作风险高：若操作不当可能导致产物失活变性，且产物无法重加工。 Operation is risky: improper procedures may lead to denaturation or inactivation, and freeze-dried products cannot be reprocessed.

老师强调，冻干本质上是“不得已而为之”的选择，尤其用于极度不耐受热或水的药物。 The professor emphasized that freeze-drying is fundamentally a “last resort,” chosen only for substances extremely sensitive to heat or water and when other drying methods are unsuitable.

072025-11-07

冻干（升华干燥）Freeze-drying (Lyophilisation)

定义与原理 Definition and principle

定义：

冻干是指通过升华的方法，将已经被冷冻固化的溶液、悬浮液等中的冰脱除，实现干燥。

Definition
Freeze-drying is the process of desiccation by sublimation of ice from solutions, suspensions etc., previously solidified by freezing.

操作分两步：

（1）将产品冷冻；（2）进行冰的升华。

Operation in 2 steps: (1) Freezing the product; (2) Ice sublimation.

初级干燥
去除游离水；

Primary desiccation: Removal of open water;

次级干燥
去除结合水。

Secondary desiccation: Removal of bound water.

冻干依赖于水的三相点（三相点条件）：

需要低压和低温。

Relies on the triple point of water: requires low pressure and low temperature.

老师补充说明 Teacher’s additional explanation

要实现升华，需要让干燥室内压力降低，同时温度升高到合适值，实现冰的升华。

To achieve sublimation, the pressure in the chamber is decreased and the temperature is increased to a suitable value, allowing the sublimation of ice.

冻干包括两个关键步骤：

（1）样品的冷冻；（2）通过降低压力和升温，使冰直接升华为水蒸气。

Freeze-drying has two key steps: (1) Freezing the sample; (2) By decreasing the pressure and raising the temperature, ice is sublimated directly to vapor.

在实际实验中，瓶盖不可完全密封，要有小孔，保证水蒸气能逸出。

In practical experiments, the vial cap should not be completely sealed. Small holes are needed to allow water vapor to escape.

比喻
冻结过程类似于北极冰层漂浮在盐水上的现象，有冰晶与高浓度盐溶液共存。
Analogy
The freezing process is like arctic ice floats on salty seawater, with ice crystals coexisting with a high concentration salt solution.

在冷冻过程中，样品会形成水分子组成的冰晶，同时会有残存的液滴，导致相分离。

During freezing, ice crystals made of water molecules form, and droplets with concentrated solutes remain, leading to phase separation.

快速冷冻会出现欠冷现象，液态水温度低于零度也未结晶，只有机械震荡或扰动才瞬间成冰，生成细小晶体。

Rapid freezing may cause supercooling, where liquid water remains below zero degrees without crystallizing; only agitation or shock causes instant freezing, forming fine crystals.

冻干的缺点 Disadvantages

We only freeze-dry if we can't do otherwise!

特别是对易损产品如疫苗，可能需要90小时 Especially for fragile products like vaccines, the process may take up to 90 hours

4
操作风险高 Risky operation

若操作不当，产品可能发生变性 Denaturation of the product may occur if freeze-drying is not properly conducted

冻干产品无法重新处理 Freeze-dried products do not allow any reprocessing

主要部件包括

The main operating steps are as follows:
冻结

抽真空
维持低压环境（5-500 μbar）以促使水汽迁移 Applying vacuum: keep low pressure (5-500 μbar) to enable vapor migration

加热

升华条件
PB<PAPB​<PA​，即形成真空 Sublimation requires: PB<PAPB​<PA​, i.e., vacuum present
水汽冷凝条件

五、老师补充说明Teacher’s additional explanation

必须抽真空降低压力，同时升温促进冰的升华 You must apply vacuum to lower pressure and increase temperature to promote ice sublimation 1

样品的瓶盖不能完全密封，需留孔让水汽逸出 The sample bottles should not be completely sealed; holes are needed for water vapor to escape 1

冻结过程中形成冰晶，并有高浓度溶液液滴形成相分离 During freezing, ice crystals form and droplets of high-concentration solution lead to phase separation 1

冷冻过快时可能出现欠冷现象，机械扰动后水分瞬间结晶生成细小冰晶 Rapid freezing may cause supercooling, and after mechanical shock, water instantly crystallizes to form tiny ice crystals 1

六、图片说明Illustrative materials

冻干机可以呈现为大型密闭设备，内部有搁板和大量独立容器 The freeze dryer is typically a large sealed device with shelves and many separate containers inside

冻结Freezing及其理化性质相关方面Physicochemical aspects related to freezing

一、冻结过程中冷冻干燥溶液的变化Changes to the freeze-dried solution during freezing

冻结时会发生相分离。 Phase separation occurs during freezing.

冰块（如极地浮冰）几乎完全由纯水组成，周围为含有高浓度溶质的液态水。 Ice floes (such as those in polar regions) are composed of nearly pure water, surrounded by liquid water with high solute concentration.

在需冷冻的溶液中，情况类似，形成纯水的冰晶与富含溶质的液相共存。 In the solution to be frozen, the situation is similar: pure water ice crystals coexist with a solute-rich liquid phase.

二、冻结对产品的影响Impact on product

冻结可能导致材料组分的改变或损伤，影响最终冻干产物的质量。 Freezing may cause alteration or damage to the components of the material, affecting the final quality of the freeze-dried product.

三、冻结阶段的行为Behavior during the freezing stage

冻结时，水中溶解固体（如API和赋形剂）逐渐浓缩。 During freezing, dissolved solids in water (such as API and excipient) become gradually concentrated.

起初，系统为单一液相，API和赋形剂均匀分布于水中。 Initially, there is a single liquid phase, with API and excipient evenly distributed in the water.

随着冻结进行，出现两相或多相系统：接近纯净的水形成冰，而其他溶质被局限在未冻结的液滴中。 As freezing proceeds, a multi-phase system emerges: nearly pure water forms ice, and the other solutes are concentrated in remaining unfrozen liquid droplets.

四、过冷现象Supercooling phenomenon

溶液在低于0∘0∘C时仍能保持液态，这被称为过冷。 The solution can remain in liquid form below 0∘0∘C, which is called supercooling.

在轻微震动或冲击下，水会瞬间结晶，生成大量小冰晶。 Upon slight shock or disturbance, the water crystallizes instantly, forming many small crystals.

这一过程风险较高，有可能导致活性成分变性。 This process is risky and may lead to denaturation of the active ingredient.

五、配方优化的应对策略Answer by formulation

可通过添加冷冻保护剂（cryoprotectants）改善冷冻行为。 The freezing behavior can be improved by adding cryoprotectants.

冷冻保护剂是能降低水熔点的水溶性物质。 Cryoprotectants are water-soluble substances that lower the melting point of water.

它们能与水及溶质形成氢键，促进水的结合。 They form hydrogen bonds with water and with solutes, promoting water binding.

能提升体系黏度，减缓结晶速率。 They increase the viscosity of the medium, slowing the crystal growth rate.

冷冻保护剂还能减少冰晶的尺寸。 Cryoprotectants also decrease the size of ice crystals.

六、老师补充说明Teacher’s additional explanation

在冷冻过程中，体系并非简单形成均一冰体，而是产生了多相结构，包括纯水冰晶与溶质浓度高的液滴。 During freezing, the system does not simply form a homogeneous ice block but generates a multi-phase structure with pure water ice crystals and solute-rich liquid droplets1。

若冷冻速度较快，可能会发生欠冷，液态水温度低于0∘0∘C但未结晶，轻微扰动即可促使其瞬间结晶，得到细小冰晶。 If freezing is rapid, supercooling may occur; the liquid water stays below 0∘0∘C but does not crystallize. A slight disturbance may induce instant crystallization, forming fine ice crystals1。

配方中的冷冻保护剂除了调节冰晶结构与大小，还能降低对活性成分的不利影响，有利于提高冻干产物品质。 Cryoprotectants in the formulation not only change crystal structure and size but also reduce the adverse effects on active ingredients, supporting higher product quality2。

七、图示补充Illustrative explanation

体系初期只有一种液相，冻结后产生接近纯水的冰及溶质浓缩的液体，呈现两相或多相。 The system begins as a single liquid phase but forms nearly pure ice and solute-concentrated liquid after freezing, resulting in a two- or multi-phase system.

过冷时稍加扰动即大量生成细小冰晶，有变性风险。 With supercooling, slight agitation will rapidly produce many small ice crystals, posing a risk of denaturation.

冻结机制与控制Freezing mechanism and its control

一、结晶的基本过程Crystallization process

结晶=（1）成核，（2）晶核生长 Crystallization = (1) nucleation, (2) nuclei growth

冰晶的数量和大小可以调节，最终产物中无定形结构的比例也可改变 The number and size of ice crystals can be changed, as can the proportion of amorphous structure in the final product23

二、动力学方法
正常冷冻与快速冷冻Kinetic approach: Normal freezing vs rapid freezing

冷冻速率不同会影响冰晶形态和产品品质 The cooling rate affects ice crystal morphology and product quality23

正常冷冻
形成大冰晶 Normal cooling: large ice crystals form
快速冷冻
形成细小冰晶 Rapid cooling: fine ice crystals form

三、正常冷冻的过程Normal cooling process

在预冷搁板（-20至-60℃）上冷冻 Freezing is performed on pre-cooled shelves (from -20 to -60℃)

系统降温至0℃以下，冰晶逐步长大，最终全部冻结 The system cools below 0℃, ice crystals gradually grow, and full solidification occurs

大而纯净的冰晶逐步生成，使溶质局部浓缩于间隙液体 Large pure ice crystals form, concentrating solutes in the interstitial solution

四、快速冷冻的过程Rapid cooling process

快速冷却会导致水出现过冷现象，即液态水在T<0℃时仍未结冰 Rapid cooling causes supercooling of water: liquid solution remains at T<0℃ without freezing3

轻微扰动后，液体能在极短时间内全部结晶，产生大量细小冰晶 Upon disturbance, the liquid instantly crystallizes in a fraction of a second, producing many fine ice crystals

细小的准纯冰晶在0℃时形成，随后间隙液体全部固化 Fine quasi-pure ice crystals form at 0℃, followed by complete solidification of the interstitial liquid

冻结曲线表现为快速降温，短时间内完成固化 The freezing curve shows rapid temperature drop and solidification in a short timeframe

五、成核控制及配方/工艺调节Nucleation control and formulation/process adjustment

细小冰晶的形成可能成为缺点——会延长升华干燥时间 Formation of fine crystals can be a disadvantage—it lengthens the sublimation (drying) phase

需要在细晶和升华效率之间权衡，既要有细晶又不能过多 A compromise must be found: fine crystals, but not too much

可通过配方适应性控制，包括加入无定形赋形剂（如甘氨酸、甘露醇）和温度循环 Adaptation of formulation & process includes addition of amorphous excipients (e.g. glycine, mannitol) and temperature cycling

这些措施有助于控制结晶生长并缩短干燥时间 These measures help control crystal growth and reduce drying time

六、老师讲解补充Teacher’s additional points

正常慢速冷冻（如每分钟降温小于1℃）主要形成大冰晶，快速冷冻（几秒内降温至极低温）则会因过冷而形成密集细小冰晶 Normal slow freezing (e.g., <1℃ drop per minute) mainly forms large crystals, while rapid freezing (temperature drops within seconds to very low T) induces dense fine ice crystals due to supercooling23

化学成分、赋形剂类型及冷冻速率的设置共同决定了最终冻干块的结构与干燥速率 Chemical composition, type of excipients, and freezing rates together determine the structure of the freeze-dried cake and drying rate2

七、图示说明Diagram explanation

上方瓶子为正常冷冻下大冰晶，下方为快速冷冻下密集细小冰晶 In bottle diagrams: upper, large ice crystals from normal freezing; lower, dense fine ice crystals from rapid freezing

快速冷冻时温度先大幅下降至过冷极限，微扰后温度突然回升，伴随大量细小冰晶的瞬间生成 In rapid freezing, temperature drops sharply to supercooling limit; a small agitation triggers a sudden temperature jump, indicating instant formation of many fine ice crystals

升华Sublimation

一、与升华相关的理化性质Physico-chemical aspects related to sublimation

升华过程中待冻干溶液的变化 Changes in the solution to be lyophilized during sublimation

浓度和界面性质发生变化 Change in the concentration and nature of interfaces

对产品的影响 Impact on product

升华过程中蛋白质、胶体体系如脂质体等可能发生变性 Possible denaturation of proteins and colloidal suspensions such as liposomes during sublimation

由浓缩效应导致 By concentration effect

由氢键破坏导致 By breaking hydrogen bonds

由于与空气接触面积增加导致 By increasing the area of contact with the air

还包括 But also:

产品所承受温度的影响 Effect of the temperature imposed on the product

物料脆弱性决定的升华持续时间影响 Effect of the sublimation time imposed by the fragility of the material to be freeze-dried2

二、与工艺相关的理化性质Physicochemical aspects related to the process

问冻结对升华的影响 How does freezing impact sublimation?
问块状冻结还是薄层冻结？ Freezing in thick blocks or thin layers?
答

薄层冻结有利于更快的升华 Thin layer allows for faster sublimation

问大冰晶还是小冰晶的升华？ Sublimation of large or fine ice crystals?
答

冻干品的最终结构会因此不同 The final structure of the lyophilisate is not the same

大冰晶产生大孔结构，细小冰晶产生大量小孔且具无定形结构 Large ice crystals form large pores with crystalline structure, while fine ice crystals lead to numerous small pores and an amorphous structure

冻结是决定性环节 FREEZING = CRITICAL STEP3

三、升华条件Sublimation conditions

将压力降至三相点以下是必须条件 Lowering the pressure below the triple point is a mandatory condition

升华过程遵循水相图的平衡线 Sublimation follows the balance line of the water phase diagram

压力越高，平衡时温度也越高 The higher the pressure, the higher the temperature at equilibrium (in the phase diagram)

只有位于升华区，固相才可以直接变为气相 Only in the sublimation zone can the solid phase transform directly into vapor3

四、升华操作Performing sublimation

初级干燥阶段Primary desiccation phase

在装置A腔内降压
进行真空处理 Lowering the pressure in the A enclosure of the device: Vacuum packing

实现从固态水到气态水的逐步转变 Gradual transition from solid water (ice) to gaseous state

排除自由水（即90-95%的水分） Phasing out of free water (that is, 90-95% of the water content)3

在B腔低温表面捕集水分 Trapping water on a cooled surface in enclosure B

蒸汽冷凝为冰 Condensation of steam into ice

作用类似于“抽取”水分子 Role
"pumping" water molecules

要求冷阱的温度低于冷冻产品 Requires a lower temperature than the frozen product3

五、老师补充说明Teacher’s additional points

冻结阶段形成的冰晶和微孔结构直接决定升华速率以及最终产品的内部结构 The size and arrangement of ice crystals and pores formed during the freezing step directly determine the sublimation rate and the internal structure of the final product23

冻干过程的能效、产品质量和复水性等关键指标依赖于升华效率 Critical parameters such as energy efficiency, product quality, and rehydration properties depend on sublimation efficiency

升华机制Sublimation mechanism

一、产品表面饱和蒸汽压的形成Development of a saturation vapor pressure (p_v) on the surface of the product to be freeze-dried

在被冻干产品表面会形成饱和蒸汽压 p_v。 A saturation vapor pressure (p_v) forms on the surface of the product to be freeze-dried.

p_v 取决于产品本身的温度。 The value of p_v depends on the temperature of the product.

冻结溶液表面随着温度降低，p_v 逐渐减小，例如： As the temperature drops, the p_v of the frozen solution decreases, e.g.:   -20℃ 时 p_v 为 1.032 mbar。 At -20°C, p_v is 1.032 mbar.   -30℃ 时 p_v 为 0.380 mbar。 At -30°C, p_v is 0.380 mbar.   -40℃ 时 p_v 为 0.128 mbar。 At -40°C, p_v is 0.128 mbar.   -50℃ 时 p_v 为 0.039 mbar。 At -50°C, p_v is 0.039 mbar.   -60℃ 时 p_v 为 0.010 mbar。 At -60°C, p_v is 0.010 mbar.

冰的饱和蒸汽压数据表体现温度对蒸汽压的影响。 The vapor pressure table of ice shows the effect of temperature on vapor pressure.

Clausius-Clapeyron 方程描述了 p_v 与温度的关系： The Clausius-Clapeyron equation describes the relation of p_v to temperature:   d(ln p_v)/dT = -ΔH_s M_w / (R T^2)

二、升华驱动力Sublimation fueling

在装有产品的A室 In enclosure A containing the product

水蒸气与气体（空气或氮气等）达到平衡。 The water vapor is in equilibrium with other gases (air or nitrogen).

此时水分子的分压力 p_PA 小于表面饱和蒸汽压 p_v。 The partial pressure of water molecules (P_PA) is less than the saturation vapor pressure (p_v).

在冷凝器B中 In the condenser 　
答案 B

冷凝器B的压力与温度均低于A。 Both pressure and temperature in B are lower than in A.

需要确保升华前沿和冷凝器之间存在蒸汽浓度梯度。 A gradient of water vapor concentration is required between the sublimation front and the condenser.

三、纯水与真实溶液的区别Pure water vs. Real Solution

实际溶液的相图不同于纯水。 The phase diagram for real solutions is different from that of pure water.

低温溶质会导致冷凝点下降（冷冻点下降效应）。 The presence of solute causes freezing point depression (cryoscopic lowering).

三相点的压力也会降低。 The triple point pressure is lowered as well.

结果是升华区变小，升华条件窗口更窄。 As a result, the sublimation zone is reduced, and the operational window for sublimation is narrower.

升华机制Sublimation mechanism

一、初始阶段 AT FIRST

冻结溶液表面会形成饱和蒸汽压 pvpv​。 A saturation vapor pressure pvpv​ forms on the surface of the frozen solution.

冻结溶液的温度用 TsTs​ 表示。 The temperature of the frozen solution is denoted as TsTs​.

通过传导加热为样品提供热能。 Heat is supplied to the sample by conduction heating.

外部加真空，保证系统压力 PenceintePenceinte​ 低于极限压力 PlimPlim​，以防止冰融化。 Apply vacuum to ensure that system pressure Penceinte<PlimPenceinte​<Plim​ to prevent melting of the ice.

二、升华进行中 IN PROGRESS

升华是吸热过程，会冷却固体样品。 Sublimation is an endothermic process and will cool the solid.

系统通过辐射、传导和对流持续加热以维持升华。 Continued energy input to sustain sublimation comes from radiation, conduction, and convection.

形成升华前沿，未升华区域和多孔固体区域分界明显。 A clear sublimation front forms between unsublimated and porous solid areas.

升华过程中，温度必须维持在 Ts<TlimTs​<Tlim​，同样是为防止冰融化。 During sublimation, Ts<TlimTs​<Tlim​ must be maintained (for the same reason, to prevent melting).

三、一段干燥结束/二段干燥 END OF PRIMARY DESICCATION / SECONDARY DESICCATION

一段干燥结束时，产品主要为无定形的多孔固体。 At the end of primary desiccation, the product is mainly amorphous and porous.

开放水减少，pvpv​ 大幅下降。 As open (free) water decreases, pvpv​ falls sharply.

二段干燥主要移除结合水，pvpv​ 接近零，仅残留极少结合水。 Secondary desiccation (desorption of bound water) removes bound water, with the pvpv​ of product essentially null.

需要通过升高温度（但仍需低于临界温度TcTc​）来驱动结合水解析。 Heating (keeping T<TcT<Tc​) is required to drive off bound water.

四、升华过程参数 Sublimation process parameters

升华必须满足两个关键条件
Two critical conditions must be met for sublimation:

装置内压力必须低于冰的蒸汽压。 Enclosure pressure must be lower than the vapor pressure of ice.

搁板温度应足够高，以维持压力差和升华过程进行。 Shelf temperature must be high enough to maintain the pressure difference and allow sublimation to proceed.

仅满足上述两点还不够，实际操作中还涉及多个影响因素（如产品配方、冷冻方式等）。 These two factors alone are not sufficient; in practice, other aspects such as formulation and freezing method also influence sublimation.

五、老师讲话补充 Teacher’s supplementary points

升华过程中需要维持压力差，以促使水蒸气从样品内部迁移至冷阱。 A maintained pressure gradient is necessary to drive water vapor migration from the sample into the cold trap.

一段干燥主要去除游离水，占水分总量的90-95%，二段干燥则主要去除结合水分。 Primary drying removes most free water (90-95% of total), and secondary drying removes water bound by hydrogen bonding or electrostatic interactions.

升华阶段产品逐渐由含水固体变为无水的多孔固体，保持原始形状和体积接近。 During sublimation, the product transforms from hydrated solid to dry porous solid, typically retaining its initial shape and close to its original volume.

温度和压力控制不当会导致蛋白变性、干燥效率降低及外观缺陷等问题。 Improper control of temperature or pressure can lead to protein denaturation, poor drying efficiency, or defects in product appearance.

升华最大温度的寻找与优化How to find and optimize the maximum temperature

一、冻干品质量Quality of the lyophilisate

冻干品的外观Aspect of lyophilisate

不同批次的冻干品外观差异很大。 There can be significant differences in lyophilisate appearance between batches.

满意的冻干品结构致密完整。 Satisfactory lyophilisate has a dense and intact structure.

如果升华过程中温度过高，容易发生“塌陷”现象。 If the temperature is too high during sublimation, "collapse" often occurs.

塌陷即为冻干品“软化”，与质量要求不兼容。 Collapse corresponds to a "softening" of the lyophilisate, incompatible with quality attributes.

塌陷会导致产品结构变差，影响后续保存与复水性。 Collapse causes poor product structure, affecting storage and reconstitution.

二、共晶形成与升华温度极限Formation of an eutectic and sublimation temperature limit

熔点的改变Modification of the solidification temperature (fusion)

配方中的溶质会影响冻结点，从而改变升华温度极限。 Solutes in the formulation alter the freezing point, thereby changing the sublimation temperature limit.

升华温度极限由最低共晶温度（Te）决定。 The sublimation limit temperature is determined by the lowest eutectic temperature (TeTe​).

问如何判定升华极限温度？ How to determine the sublimation limit temperature?
答

需要确定体系共晶组成。 The eutectic composition of the system must be determined.

以甘氨酸/水体系为例，TeTe​ = -3.6℃，XeXe​ = 15%。 For the glycine/water system, TeTe​ = -3.6°C, XeXe​ = 15%.

当温度达到TeTe​时，体系开始熔化，因此Te就是不能超过的升华极限温度。 At TeTe​, the system melts, so TeTe​ becomes the maximum temperature not to be exceeded during sublimation.

常用方法有DSC（差示扫描量热法）分析共晶点。 The eutectic point is often determined by DSC (Differential Scanning Calorimetry) analysis.

三、理化方面补充Physico-chemical aspects

水中溶质的存在导致冷冻点降低（冷凝点下降）。 Presence of solutes in water lowers the freezing point (cryoscopic lowering).

共晶的温度成为升华温度（最大温度）极限。 The temperature of the eutectic becomes the sublimation "limit" (max) temperature.

四、老师补充补充Teacher’s additional explanations

升华过程的最大温度（也就是货架温度/shelf temperature）必须始终低于共晶点，否则容易导致产品塌陷或者结构破坏。 The maximum temperature during sublimation (i.e., shelf temperature) must always be below the eutectic point, otherwise product collapse or structure damage occurs.

共晶温度不同于冰点，因为体系含有溶质，故共晶温度通常比纯水冰点低。 The eutectic temperature is different from the ice point, as the presence of solutes makes the eutectic temperature lower than the melting point of pure water.

现代工艺常通过多次实验DSC分析和冻干品外观对比，确定最佳升华极限温度，并据此优化工艺参数，提高产品质量。 Modern practice involves repeated DSC analyses and comparison of lyophilisate appearance to best determine and optimize the maximum sublimation temperature for quality.

水的存在效应Effect of the presence of water

一、升华过程中的物理状态转变Change of physical state during sublimation

升华过程中，体系由含水固体转变为脱水固体。 During sublimation, the system changes from a hydrated solid to a dehydrated solid.

但在冻结阶段，有一部分水不会固化，会对含水固体产生增塑作用。 However, during freezing, a fraction of water does not solidify and plasticises the hydrated solid.

二、非晶溶质与玻璃化转变Amorphous solute and glass transition

如果溶质为非晶态，则存在玻璃化转变温度 TgTg​。 If the solute is amorphous, it has a glass transition temperature TgTg​.

若有水分以液态残留在基质中，会导致 TgTg​ 下降至 Tg′Tg′​。 If water persists in a liquid state in the matrix, it lowers the TgTg​ to a Tg′Tg′​ value.

三、DSC实验表明水含量对玻璃化转变的影响DSC reveals effect of water content on glass transition

以山梨醇为例，无水样品和含10%水样品的玻璃化转变温度明显不同。 For example, with sorbitol, the glass transition temperature is clearly different between anhydrous and 10% water samples.

含水样品的玻璃化转变温度大幅下降（如-28.9℃开始），而无水样品Tg较高（如-4.0℃开始）。 For the sample with water, the glass transition starts at a much lower temperature (e.g., -28.9°C onset), while the dry sample has a higher Tg (e.g., -4.0°C onset).

四、Tg' 概念与溶质浓度变化Concept of Tg' and variation of solute concentration

以蔗糖溶液为例，不同状态下升华温度的极限不同。 For example, in sucrose solution, the sublimation temperature limit varies by phase condition.

如果溶质能结晶，升华最大温度 TmaxTmax​ 应低于最低共晶温度 TeTe​。 If the solute crystallizes, the maximum sublimation temperature (TmaxTmax​) should be below the minimum eutectic temperature (TeTe​).

如果溶质不能结晶，升华最大温度 TmaxTmax​ 应低于 Tg′Tg′​。 If the solute does not crystallize, TmaxTmax​ should be below Tg′Tg′​.

公式总结：

五、升华温度选择对冻干品外观的影响Effect of chosen sublimation temperature on lyophilisate appearance

如果干燥固体被加热到高于 Tg′Tg′​，非晶固体会转为黏稠液体并在 TcTc​ 温度时塌陷。 If the dry solid is heated above Tg′Tg′​, the amorphous solid turns into a viscous liquid and collapses at TcTc​.

TcTc​与Tg′Tg′​的差值只有几度，工艺窗口狭窄。 The difference between TcTc​ and Tg′Tg′​ is only a few degrees, so the process window is narrow.

六、塌陷的后果Consequences of collapse

塌陷会导致冻干品外观变差。 Collapse leads to an unsightly appearance of the lyophilisate.

塌陷还会显著降低其多孔性，从而复溶时难以彻底分散和重构。 Collapse also reduces porosity, making it difficult to fully reconstruct when rehydrated.

升华温度与塌陷Température de sublimation et collapse | Sublimation and collapse temperatures

一、升华温度选择对冻干品外观的影响Consequences of the choice of sublimation temperature on the final appearance of the lyophilisate

冻干温度对最终冻干品形态有决定性作用。 The freeze-drying temperature has a decisive effect on the final appearance of the lyophilisate.

产品温度低于塌陷温度（Tc）时，可得到结构优良的冻干品。 When the product temperature is below the collapse temperature (Tc), you obtain a well-structured lyophilisate.

产品温度等于Tc时，会出现部分塌陷。 When the product temperature equals Tc, partial collapse occurs.

产品温度高于Tc时，冻干品严重塌陷。 When the product temperature exceeds Tc, the lyophilisate collapses severely.

理想冻干品应占据与初始液体体积几乎相同的空间，结构松散多孔。 The ideal product occupies nearly the same volume as the initial liquid and has a loose, porous structure.

塌陷导致产品体积大大减小，外观变差，质地变硬或不均。 Collapse dramatically reduces the product’s volume, worsens appearance, and results in a dense or uneven texture.

二、显微镜下白蛋白冻干品塌陷可视化Visualization of the collapse of an albumin lyophilisate

A
样品冻结形成气/液界面，结构界线清晰。 A: Sample frozen with an air/liquid interface, clear structural boundary.
B
升华前沿推进，样品在真空下进行主干燥。 B: Drying front advancing, primary drying under vacuum.
C
温度升至塌陷线以上，表层膜断裂，可看到明显塌陷区域。 C: Temperature raised above collapse temperature, rupture of surface skin, and evident collapse front visible.

显微分析帮助理解升温过程中的结构变化。 Microscopy assists in understanding the structural changes during the heating process.

三、老师讲解补充Additional teacher’s explanations

样品温度必须严格控制低于Tc，否则极易导致塌陷并影响产品的复水性与质量。 The product temperature must be strictly kept below Tc, otherwise collapse is likely, impairing reconstitution ability and quality.

塌陷还会造成药品无菌性、均匀性降低，不被临床接受。 Collapse also reduces sterility and uniformity, making the product unacceptable for clinical use【@ref_2】【@ref_1】。

干燥参数选择不当常见的现象包括部分塌陷、整体塌陷与表面硬化。 Common phenomena when drying parameters are inappropriate include partial collapse, full collapse, and surface hardening.

精确确定Tc及工艺窗口对于获得高质量冻干品是关键。 Accurately determining Tc and the process window is crucial for obtaining a high-quality lyophilisate【@ref_1】。

四、常用赋形剂与其作用Most common excipients and their roles

配方中常用赋形剂包括
Common excipients in formulations include:

充填剂（如甘露醇、乳糖）Bulking agents (e.g. mannitol, lactose)

缓冲剂（如柠檬酸钠）Buffers (e.g. sodium citrate)

增溶剂Solubilizers

调节渗透压剂Isotonizers

改变玻璃化转变温度的剂（Tg'调节剂）Tg' modifiers

选择合适的赋形剂，有助于调整产品的塌陷温度和玻璃化转变温度，提高配方的工艺适应性和安全性。 Choosing proper excipients helps adjust the collapse and glass transition temperatures, improving the process adaptability and safety of the formulation.

塌陷温度调节剂如右旋糖酐、羟乙基淀粉等广泛应用，用以提高或降低产品Tc。 Collapse temperature modifiers like dextran and hydroxyethyl starch are used to raise or lower product Tc.

赋形剂的理化性质Physicochemical properties of excipients

一、常见赋形剂的Tg'数值Examples of Tg' values for common substances

Tg'是判断冻干过程中主干燥阶段可允许产品最大温度的关键参数。 Tg' is a key parameter to determine the maximum allowable product temperature during primary drying in lyophilization.

不同类型赋形剂的Tg'值相差较大。 The Tg' values differ significantly among various types of excipients.

单糖的例子
如葡萄糖Tg'为-43℃，山梨醇为-44℃，甘露醇为-35℃。 Examples of monosaccharides: Glucose Tg' = -43°C, Sorbitol = -44°C, Mannitol = -35°C.
双糖的例子
乳糖Tg'为-28℃，麦芽糖为-30℃，蔗糖为-32℃，海藻糖为-30℃。 Examples of disaccharides: Lactose Tg' = -28°C, Maltose = -30°C, Sucrose = -32°C, Trehalose = -30°C.
三糖例子
麦芽三糖Tg'为-24℃。 Example of trisaccharides: Maltotriose Tg' = -24°C.

聚合物赋形剂如右旋糖酐，Tg'为-10℃。 Polymer excipients such as Dextran 70 have Tg' = -10°C.

其他赋形剂如PEG、PVP等，Tg'分别为-13℃和-20℃。 Other excipients: PEG Tg' = -13°C, PVP = -20°C.

甘油Tg'低至-65℃，蛋白质Tg'根据分子量大约为-100℃。 Glycerol has a Tg' as low as -65°C, and proteins' Tg' is around -100°C depending on molecular mass.

二、主要盐和缓冲剂的Tg'和Te数值Tg' and Te values for main salts and buffers

结晶性盐类一般关注最低共晶点Te，以确保升华温度不超过该值。例如Na2SO4的Te为-2℃。 For crystallizing salts, the lowest eutectic point Te is the focus, ensuring sublimation temperature does not exceed this value, e.g., Na2SO4 Te = -2°C.

混合盐和缓冲剂既需关注Tg'也需关注Te。如NaHCO3，Tg'为-52℃，Te为-4℃。 For mixed salts and buffers, both Tg' and Te should be considered, e.g., NaHCO3 Tg' = -52°C, Te = -4°C.

非晶盐类则主要关注Tg'，如Na-乙酸Tg'为-64℃，K-柠檬酸盐Tg'为-62℃。 For amorphous salts, Tg' is most important, e.g., Sodium acetate Tg' = -64°C, Potassium citrate Tg' = -62°C.

不同类型盐、缓冲剂或赋形剂的Tg'和Te差异对配方工艺窗口有重要影响。 Differences in Tg' and Te among salts, buffers, and other excipients significantly impact the formulation’s process window.

三、工艺优化相关的条件，总结和公式Process conditions related to optimization: summary and formulae

溶质浓度会影响Tg'和Te，在所有情况下都需考虑。 Solute concentration must be considered as it influences Tg' and Te in all cases.

不同基体类型在主干燥阶段的温度上限如下： The temperature limits for primary drying in different matrix types are summarized as follows:

非晶基体
Tsublimation < Tg'，如糖类溶质。 Amorphous matrix: Tsublimation < Tg', e.g., sugars.
晶体基体
Tsublimation < Te，如盐类或氨基酸。 Crystalline matrix: Tsublimation < Te, e.g., salts or amino acids.
混合基体
Tsublimation < Tg' 且 < Te。 Mixed matrix: Tsublimation < Tg' and < Te.

溶质的物理状态和浓度共同决定安全干燥窗口的位置。 The physical state and concentration of the solute jointly determine the permitted drying process window.

四、老师补充与实际应用Teacher's explanations and practical applications

选择适合的赋形剂和缓冲剂，可以通过调节Tg'或Te拓展或优化工艺窗口。 Choosing suitable excipients and buffers allows adjustment of Tg' or Te to expand or optimize the process window.

Tg'值较低的溶液需要更低的干燥温度，否则易发生塌陷或结构损坏。 Solutions with low Tg' require lower drying temperatures; otherwise, collapse or structure damage may occur.

不同药品和生物制品需要依据赋形剂和pH的要求，精准选择赋形剂和缓冲盐类型。 Different drugs and biologics require precise choices of excipients and buffer salts based on excipient and pH requirements.

极端情况下，如配方Tg'约为-40℃，主干燥温度必须严格低于此值，否则产品质量受损。 For extreme cases (e.g., formulation Tg' ≈ -40°C), the primary drying temperature must be strictly below this value, or product quality will be compromised.

冻干周期Freeze-drying cycle

一、冻干过程的五个主要阶段Five main stages of the freeze-drying process

1
产品过冷区域 Product supercooling zone
2
真空下产品冷却 Vacuum cooling of the product
3
升华阶段 Sublimation stage
4
失去所有冰后产品温度上升 Product temperature rises after all ice is gone
二次干燥
允许最高温、极低压力 Secondary drying: maximum permissible temperature, extremely low pressure

二、各阶段的温度、压力变化Temperature and pressure changes at each stage

在第一阶段，产品迅速降至0℃以下（通常在0℃至-40℃范围） In the first stage, the product rapidly cools below 0°C (usually to -40°C or so)

真空条件下进一步降温和降压 Further cooling and pressure reduction follow under vacuum

升华阶段需保持低温低压，通过升华去除水分 During sublimation, low temperature and pressure are maintained, and water is removed via sublimation

去除冰后，温度可缓慢升高，进入二次干燥以去除结合水 After removing ice, the temperature can be slowly raised to start secondary drying and remove bound water

全过程陷阱温度保持最低以捕获升华水汽 The trap temperature is kept lowest throughout to capture water vapor from sublimation

三、升华及其操作变量Sublimation and operational variables

升华压力需低于Tg'平衡压力 Sublimation pressure should be lower than the equilibrium pressure corresponding to Tg'

升华温度必须低于Tg'用于主干燥，次干燥需低于产品熔点 Sublimation temperature must stay below Tg' for primary drying, and secondary drying must be below product melting point

配方优化可通过赋形剂调节Tg'扩大工艺窗口 Formulation can be optimized by adding excipients to adjust Tg' and expand the process window

四、冻干产品的质量控制要点Quality control requirements for freeze-dried products

外观（结构、颜色、均匀性、质量） Appearance (structure, color, uniformity, weight)

残余水分含量 Residual moisture content

溶解时间 Dissolution time

复溶溶液的澄清度和活性 Clarity and activity of the reconstituted solution

长期稳定性 Long-term stability

注射剂需无菌 Sterility for injectable forms

五、老师讲解与补充Teacher’s explanation and supplements

主干燥与二次干燥阶段需要实时监测温度和压力，确保不超过Tg'和熔点，否则产品会塌陷或结构损坏 During both primary and secondary drying, it is critical to monitor temperature and pressure in real time to avoid exceeding Tg' and melting point, preventing collapse or structure damage

产品理想外观应体积接近原始液体、结构均匀、质地疏松 Ideal appearance is nearly the same volume as the original solution, with uniform and porous structure

复溶快且溶液澄清是优质冻干品的标志 Good freeze-dried products dissolve quickly and produce clear solution

注射剂需严格检测无菌性和均一性保证安全 Injectables require strict sterility and homogeneity testing for safety

赋形剂种类选择影响Tg'和最终产品质量 Choice of excipients affects Tg' and the final product quality

温度和压力控制直接关系到外观、API活性和长期稳定性 Temperature and pressure control affect appearance, API activity, and stability
